========================================
FILE: BrewStop\Program.cs

using BrewStop.Data;
using BrewStop.Hubs;
using BrewStop.Models;
using BrewStop.Services;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authentication;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authentication.Google;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Identity;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using System.Security.Claims;
using System.Threading.RateLimiting;

var builder = WebApplication.CreateBuilder(args);

var connectionString = builder.Configuration.GetConnectionString("DefaultConnection");
if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(connectionString))
{
    connectionString = "Server=localhost;Port=3306;Database=BrewStopDb;User=root;Password=;";
}

builder.Services.AddDbContext<ApplicationDbContext>(options =>
    options.UseMySql(connectionString, new MySqlServerVersion(new Version(8, 0, 36))));

builder.Services.AddIdentity<ApplicationUser, IdentityRole>(options =>
{
    options.SignIn.RequireConfirmedEmail = true;          // SRS-FR-01
    options.User.RequireUniqueEmail = true;

    options.Lockout.AllowedForNewUsers = true;            // SRS-FR-02
    options.Lockout.MaxFailedAccessAttempts = 5;
    options.Lockout.DefaultLockoutTimeSpan = TimeSpan.FromMinutes(15);

    options.Password.RequiredLength = 8;
    options.Password.RequireDigit = true;
    options.Password.RequireLowercase = true;
    options.Password.RequireUppercase = true;
    options.Password.RequireNonAlphanumeric = false;
})
    .AddEntityFrameworkStores<ApplicationDbContext>()
    .AddDefaultTokenProviders();

var googleClientId = builder.Configuration["Authentication:Google:ClientId"];
var googleClientSecret = builder.Configuration["Authentication:Google:ClientSecret"];

if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(googleClientId) && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(googleClientSecret))
{
    builder.Services.AddAuthentication()
        .AddGoogle(GoogleDefaults.AuthenticationScheme, o =>
        {
            o.ClientId = googleClientId;
            o.ClientSecret = googleClientSecret;
            o.SignInScheme = IdentityConstants.ExternalScheme;
            o.CallbackPath = "/signin-google";
            o.SaveTokens = false;
            o.Scope.Add("email");
            o.Scope.Add("profile");
            o.ClaimActions.MapJsonKey("urn:google:verified_email", "verified_email", ClaimValueTypes.Boolean);
            // Correlation cookie must survive the cross-site return from Google
            o.CorrelationCookie.SameSite = SameSiteMode.None;
            o.CorrelationCookie.SecurePolicy = CookieSecurePolicy.Always;
        });
}

// Identity.External must be Lax so it is sent on Google's cross-site redirect back; main app cookie stays Strict
builder.Services.ConfigureExternalCookie(options =>
{
    options.Cookie.HttpOnly = true;
    options.Cookie.SecurePolicy = CookieSecurePolicy.Always;
    options.Cookie.SameSite = SameSiteMode.Lax;
    options.ExpireTimeSpan = TimeSpan.FromMinutes(10);
});

builder.Services.AddAuthorizationBuilder()
    .AddPolicy("CustomerOnly", policy => policy.RequireRole(AppRoles.Student, AppRoles.Faculty));

builder.Services.ConfigureApplicationCookie(options =>       // SRS-NFR-05
{
    options.Cookie.HttpOnly = true;
    options.Cookie.SecurePolicy = CookieSecurePolicy.Always;
    options.Cookie.SameSite = SameSiteMode.Strict;
    options.SlidingExpiration = true;
    options.ExpireTimeSpan = TimeSpan.FromMinutes(30);
    options.LoginPath = "/Account/Login";
    options.AccessDeniedPath = "/Account/AccessDenied";

    options.Events.OnRedirectToLogin = ctx =>
    {
        if (ctx.Request.Path.StartsWithSegments("/api", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
        {
            ctx.Response.StatusCode = StatusCodes.Status401Unauthorized;
            return ctx.Response.WriteAsJsonAsync(new { success = false, message = "Authentication required." });
        }
        ctx.Response.Redirect(ctx.RedirectUri);
        return Task.CompletedTask;
    };
    options.Events.OnRedirectToAccessDenied = ctx =>
    {
        if (ctx.Request.Path.StartsWithSegments("/api", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
        {
            ctx.Response.StatusCode = StatusCodes.Status403Forbidden;
            return ctx.Response.WriteAsJsonAsync(new { success = false, message = "Access denied." });
        }
        ctx.Response.Redirect(ctx.RedirectUri);
        return Task.CompletedTask;
    };
});

builder.Services.AddRateLimiter(o =>
{
    o.RejectionStatusCode = StatusCodes.Status429TooManyRequests;
    o.OnRejected = async (ctx, ct) =>
    {
        if (ctx.Lease.TryGetMetadata(MetadataName.RetryAfter, out var ra))
            ctx.HttpContext.Response.Headers.RetryAfter = ((int)ra.TotalSeconds).ToString();

        ctx.HttpContext.RequestServices.GetRequiredService<ILoggerFactory>()
            .CreateLogger("RateLimit")
            .LogWarning("Rate limit hit {Path} from {Ip}", ctx.HttpContext.Request.Path, ctx.HttpContext.Connection.RemoteIpAddress);

        if (ctx.HttpContext.Request.Path.StartsWithSegments("/api", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            await ctx.HttpContext.Response.WriteAsJsonAsync(
                new { success = false, message = "Too many requests. Try again later." }, ct);
    };

    static bool IsOrder(HttpContext c) => HttpMethods.IsPost(c.Request.Method)
        && c.Request.Path.StartsWithSegments("/Order/Checkout", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

    static bool IsAuth(HttpContext c) => HttpMethods.IsPost(c.Request.Method)
        && (c.Request.Path.StartsWithSegments("/Account/Login", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
         || c.Request.Path.StartsWithSegments("/Account/Register", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
         || c.Request.Path.StartsWithSegments("/Account/ExternalLogin", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));

    static bool IsScan(HttpContext c) => HttpMethods.IsPost(c.Request.Method)
        && c.Request.Path.StartsWithSegments("/api/barista/scan", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

    static FixedWindowRateLimiterOptions Win(int n) => new()
    { PermitLimit = n, Window = TimeSpan.FromMinutes(1), QueueLimit = 0 };

    o.GlobalLimiter = PartitionedRateLimiter.CreateChained(
        PartitionedRateLimiter.Create<HttpContext, string>(c =>
            IsOrder(c) ? RateLimitPartition.GetFixedWindowLimiter("ord-ip:" + c.Connection.RemoteIpAddress, _ => Win(5))
          : IsAuth(c) ? RateLimitPartition.GetFixedWindowLimiter("auth-ip:" + c.Connection.RemoteIpAddress, _ => Win(10))
          : IsScan(c) ? RateLimitPartition.GetFixedWindowLimiter("scan-ip:" + c.Connection.RemoteIpAddress, _ => Win(30))
          : RateLimitPartition.GetNoLimiter("none")),
        PartitionedRateLimiter.Create<HttpContext, string>(c =>
            IsOrder(c) && c.User.Identity?.IsAuthenticated == true
                ? RateLimitPartition.GetFixedWindowLimiter("ord-user:" + c.User.FindFirstValue(ClaimTypes.NameIdentifier), _ => Win(5))
          : IsScan(c) && c.User.Identity?.IsAuthenticated == true
                ? RateLimitPartition.GetFixedWindowLimiter("scan-user:" + c.User.FindFirstValue(ClaimTypes.NameIdentifier), _ => Win(30))
                : RateLimitPartition.GetNoLimiter("none")));
});

builder.Services.AddScoped<IEmailService, LoggingEmailService>();
builder.Services.AddHttpContextAccessor();
builder.Services.AddSingleton<IImageResolver, ImageResolver>();
builder.Services.AddDistributedMemoryCache();
builder.Services.AddSession(options =>
{
    options.Cookie.Name = ".BrewStop.Cart";
    options.Cookie.HttpOnly = true;
    options.Cookie.IsEssential = true;
    options.Cookie.SecurePolicy = CookieSecurePolicy.Always;
    options.Cookie.SameSite = SameSiteMode.Strict;
    options.IdleTimeout = TimeSpan.FromHours(2);
});

builder.Services.Configure<ShopOptions>(builder.Configuration.GetSection("Shop"));
builder.Services.Configure<QrOptions>(builder.Configuration.GetSection("Qr"));
builder.Services.AddScoped<IQrTokenService, QrTokenService>();
builder.Services.AddScoped<ICartService, CartService>();
builder.Services.AddScoped<IPickupSlotService, PickupSlotService>();
builder.Services.AddScoped<IInventoryService, InventoryService>();
builder.Services.AddScoped<IAuditService, AuditService>();
builder.Services.AddScoped<IPromotionService, PromotionService>();
builder.Services.AddMemoryCache();
builder.Services.Configure<CaptchaOptions>(builder.Configuration.GetSection("Captcha"));
builder.Services.AddScoped<ICaptchaService, CaptchaService>();

builder.Services.AddSignalR();

builder.Services.AddControllersWithViews(options =>          // SRS-NFR-06
    options.Filters.Add(new AutoValidateAntiforgeryTokenAttribute()));

var app = builder.Build();

if (!app.Environment.IsDevelopment())
{
    app.UseExceptionHandler("/Home/Error");
    app.UseHsts();
}

app.UseHttpsRedirection();
app.UseStaticFiles();

app.Use(async (ctx, next) =>
{
    var h = ctx.Response.Headers;
    h["X-Content-Type-Options"] = "nosniff";
    h["X-Frame-Options"] = "DENY";
    h["Content-Security-Policy"] =
        "default-src 'self'; img-src 'self' data: https://images.unsplash.com; connect-src 'self' ws: wss:; " +
        "script-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline' https://cdn.tailwindcss.com https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com; " +
        "style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline' https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com https://fonts.googleapis.com; " +
        "font-src 'self' https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com https://fonts.gstatic.com; frame-ancestors 'none'";
    await next();
});

app.UseRouting();
app.UseSession();
app.UseAuthentication();
app.UseRateLimiter();
app.UseAuthorization();

app.MapControllerRoute(
    name: "default",
    pattern: "{controller=Home}/{action=Index}/{id?}");

app.MapHub<OrderHub>("/hubs/orders");

using (var scope = app.Services.CreateScope())
{
    var db = scope.ServiceProvider.GetRequiredService<ApplicationDbContext>();
    await db.Database.MigrateAsync();
}

await DbInitializer.SeedAsync(app.Services);
app.Run();


========================================
FILE: BrewStop\Controllers\AccountController.cs
========================================
using BrewStop.Data;
using BrewStop.Models;
using BrewStop.Services;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authentication;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authentication.Google;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Identity;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Options;
using System.Net;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;
using System.Security.Claims;

namespace BrewStop.Controllers;

public class AccountController(
    UserManager<ApplicationUser> users,
    SignInManager<ApplicationUser> signIn,
    IEmailService email,
    ICaptchaService captcha,
    IAuditService audit,
    IConfiguration config,
    ApplicationDbContext db,
    IWebHostEnvironment env,
    IOptions<IdentityOptions> identityOptions,
    ILogger<AccountController> log) : Controller
{
    // Register
    [HttpGet, AllowAnonymous]
    public IActionResult Register() => View(new RegisterViewModel());

    [HttpPost, AllowAnonymous]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Register(RegisterViewModel m)
    {
        if (!ModelState.IsValid) return View(m);

        if (!await captcha.VerifyAsync(Request, "register", HttpContext.RequestAborted))
        {
            ModelState.AddModelError(string.Empty, "Captcha verification failed. Please try again.");
            return View(m);
        }

        var domain = config["Campus:AllowedEmailDomain"];
        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(domain) &&
            !m.Email.EndsWith("@" + domain, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            ModelState.AddModelError(nameof(m.Email), $"Use your campus email (@{domain}).");

        if (m.Role is not (AppRoles.Student or AppRoles.Faculty))
            ModelState.AddModelError(nameof(m.Role), "Invalid role.");

        if (await users.Users.AnyAsync(u => u.CampusId == m.CampusId.Trim()))
            ModelState.AddModelError(nameof(m.CampusId), "Campus ID is already registered.");

        if (!ModelState.IsValid) return View(m);

        var user = new ApplicationUser
        {
            UserName = m.Email,
            Email = m.Email,
            FullName = m.FullName.Trim(),
            CampusId = m.CampusId.Trim()
        };

        var result = await users.CreateAsync(user, m.Password);
        if (!result.Succeeded)
        {
            foreach (var e in result.Errors) ModelState.AddModelError("", e.Description);
            return View(m);
        }

        await users.AddToRoleAsync(user, m.Role);

        await audit.LogAsync(user.Id, "RoleAssigned", $"Role={m.Role}; Source=Registration");

        await SendConfirmationAsync(user);
        log.LogInformation("User registered {UserId} as {Role}", user.Id, m.Role);

        return View("Info", new InfoViewModel("Check your email",
            "We sent a verification link. Verify your email before signing in."));
    }

    [HttpGet, AllowAnonymous]
    public async Task<IActionResult> ConfirmEmail(string? userId, string? token)
    {
        var user = userId is null || token is null ? null : await users.FindByIdAsync(userId);
        var ok = user is not null && (await users.ConfirmEmailAsync(user, token!)).Succeeded;

        return View("Info", ok
            ? new InfoViewModel("Email verified", "Your account is active. You can sign in now.")
            : new InfoViewModel("Invalid link", "This verification link is invalid or expired."));
    }

    [HttpGet, AllowAnonymous]
    public IActionResult Login(string? returnUrl = null) => View(new LoginViewModel { ReturnUrl = returnUrl });

    // Login / Logout
    [HttpPost, AllowAnonymous]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Login(LoginViewModel m)
    {
        if (!ModelState.IsValid) return View(m);

        if (!await captcha.VerifyAsync(Request, "login", HttpContext.RequestAborted))
        {
            ModelState.AddModelError(string.Empty, "Please complete the captcha.");
            return View(m);
        }

        // Soft-deleted accounts can never sign in
        var existing = await users.FindByEmailAsync(m.Email);
        if (existing is { IsDeleted: true })
        {
            ModelState.AddModelError("", "Invalid email or password.");
            return View(m);
        }

        var result = await signIn.PasswordSignInAsync(m.Email, m.Password,
            isPersistent: false, lockoutOnFailure: true);

        if (result.Succeeded)
        {
            log.LogInformation("Login success for {Email}", m.Email);
            return LocalRedirect(Url.IsLocalUrl(m.ReturnUrl) ? m.ReturnUrl! : "/");
        }

        if (result.IsLockedOut)
        {
            log.LogWarning("Account locked out: {Email}", m.Email);
            ModelState.AddModelError("", "Account locked for 15 minutes after 5 failed attempts.");
        }
        else if (result.IsNotAllowed)
        {
            ModelState.AddModelError("", "Verify your email before signing in.");
            ViewBag.NeedsVerification = true;
        }
        else
        {
            log.LogWarning("Failed login for {Email}", m.Email);
            ModelState.AddModelError("", "Invalid email or password.");
        }

        return View(m);
    }

    // Google OAuth 2.0 External Login & CampusId Onboarding
    private static bool NeedsCampusId(ApplicationUser u) =>
        string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(u.CampusId) ||
        u.CampusId.StartsWith("GOOGLE-", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

    private ContentResult RedirectAfterExternal(ApplicationUser user, string? returnUrl, string target) =>
        ClientRedirect(NeedsCampusId(user)
            ? Url.Action(nameof(CompleteCampusId), "Account",
                new { returnUrl = Url.IsLocalUrl(returnUrl) ? returnUrl : null })!
            : target);

    [HttpPost, AllowAnonymous]
    public IActionResult ExternalLogin(string provider, string? returnUrl = null)
    {
        if (!string.Equals(provider, GoogleDefaults.AuthenticationScheme, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            return BadRequest();

        var redirectUrl = Url.Action(nameof(ExternalLoginCallback), "Account",
            new { returnUrl = Url.IsLocalUrl(returnUrl) ? returnUrl : null });
        var props = signIn.ConfigureExternalAuthenticationProperties(GoogleDefaults.AuthenticationScheme, redirectUrl);
        return Challenge(props, GoogleDefaults.AuthenticationScheme);
    }

    [HttpGet, AllowAnonymous]
    public async Task<IActionResult> ExternalLoginCallback(string? returnUrl = null, string? remoteError = null)
    {
        var target = Url.IsLocalUrl(returnUrl) ? returnUrl! : "/Menu";

        IActionResult Fail(string message)
        {
            ModelState.AddModelError(string.Empty, message);
            return View("Login", new LoginViewModel { ReturnUrl = returnUrl });
        }

        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(remoteError))
        {
            log.LogWarning("Google remote error: {Error}", remoteError);
            return Fail("Google sign-in failed. Please try again or use your campus email.");
        }

        var info = await signIn.GetExternalLoginInfoAsync();
        if (info is null)
            return Fail("Google sign-in session expired. Please try again.");

        // 1) Already linked
        var result = await signIn.ExternalLoginSignInAsync(
            info.LoginProvider, info.ProviderKey, isPersistent: false, bypassTwoFactor: true);

        if (result.Succeeded)
        {
            var linked = await users.FindByLoginAsync(info.LoginProvider, info.ProviderKey);
            if (linked is null)
                return ClientRedirect(target);

            await audit.LogAsync(linked.Id, "ExternalLogin", $"Provider={info.LoginProvider}");
            return RedirectAfterExternal(linked, returnUrl, target);
        }

        if (result.IsLockedOut)
            return Fail("Account locked for 15 minutes after 5 failed attempts.");

        // 2) Not linked yet
        var emailAddr = info.Principal.FindFirstValue(ClaimTypes.Email);
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(emailAddr))
            return Fail("Google did not return an email address.");

        if (string.Equals(info.Principal.FindFirstValue("urn:google:verified_email"), "false",
                StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            return Fail("Your Google email address is not verified.");

        var user = await users.FindByEmailAsync(emailAddr);

        if (user is null)
        {
            var name = info.Principal.FindFirstValue(ClaimTypes.Name);
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(name)) name = emailAddr.Split('@')[0];
            if (name.Length > 150) name = name[..150];

            user = new ApplicationUser
            {
                UserName = emailAddr,
                Email = emailAddr,
                EmailConfirmed = true,
                FullName = name,
                CampusId = "GOOGLE-" + Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N")[..8].ToUpper()
            };

            var create = await users.CreateAsync(user);
            if (!create.Succeeded)
            {
                log.LogWarning("Google user creation failed for {Email}: {Errors}",
                    emailAddr, string.Join("; ", create.Errors.Select(e => e.Description)));
                return Fail("Could not create your account. Please contact support.");
            }

            await users.AddToRoleAsync(user, AppRoles.Student);
            await audit.LogAsync(user.Id, "RoleAssigned", $"Role={AppRoles.Student}; Source=GoogleOAuth");
            await audit.LogAsync(user.Id, "UserRegistered", $"Provider={info.LoginProvider}; Source=GoogleOAuth");
        }
        else
        {
            if (await users.IsLockedOutAsync(user))
                return Fail("Account locked for 15 minutes after 5 failed attempts.");

            var dirty = false;
            if (!user.EmailConfirmed)
            {
                user.EmailConfirmed = true;
                dirty = true;
            }
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(user.CampusId))
            {
                user.CampusId = "GOOGLE-" + Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N")[..8].ToUpper();
                dirty = true;
            }
            if (dirty) await users.UpdateAsync(user);
        }

        var existingLogins = await users.GetLoginsAsync(user);
        if (!existingLogins.Any(l => l.LoginProvider == info.LoginProvider && l.ProviderKey == info.ProviderKey))
        {
            var add = await users.AddLoginAsync(user, info);
            if (!add.Succeeded)
            {
                log.LogWarning("AddLogin failed for {Email}: {Errors}",
                    emailAddr, string.Join("; ", add.Errors.Select(e => e.Description)));
                return Fail("Could not link your Google account.");
            }
            await audit.LogAsync(user.Id, "ExternalLoginLinked", $"Provider={info.LoginProvider}");
        }

        await signIn.SignInAsync(user, isPersistent: false, info.LoginProvider);
        await HttpContext.SignOutAsync(IdentityConstants.ExternalScheme);
        await audit.LogAsync(user.Id, "ExternalLogin", $"Provider={info.LoginProvider}");

        return RedirectAfterExternal(user, returnUrl, target);
    }

    [HttpGet, Authorize]
    public async Task<IActionResult> CompleteCampusId(string? returnUrl = null)
    {
        var user = await users.GetUserAsync(User);
        if (user is null) return RedirectToAction(nameof(Login));

        if (!NeedsCampusId(user))
            return LocalRedirect(Url.IsLocalUrl(returnUrl) ? returnUrl! : "/Menu");

        return View(new CompleteCampusIdViewModel { ReturnUrl = returnUrl });
    }

    [HttpPost, Authorize, ValidateAntiForgeryToken]
    public async Task<IActionResult> CompleteCampusId(CompleteCampusIdViewModel m)
    {
        var user = await users.GetUserAsync(User);
        if (user is null) return RedirectToAction(nameof(Login));

        var target = Url.IsLocalUrl(m.ReturnUrl) ? m.ReturnUrl! : "/Menu";

        if (!NeedsCampusId(user))
            return LocalRedirect(target);

        var campusId = (m.CampusId ?? string.Empty).Trim();

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(campusId))
            ModelState.AddModelError(nameof(m.CampusId), "Campus ID is required.");
        else if (campusId.StartsWith("GOOGLE-", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            ModelState.AddModelError(nameof(m.CampusId), "Please enter your real Campus ID.");
        else if (campusId.Length > 30)
            ModelState.AddModelError(nameof(m.CampusId), "Campus ID must be at most 30 characters.");
        else if (await users.Users.AnyAsync(u => u.Id != user.Id && u.CampusId == campusId,
                     HttpContext.RequestAborted))
            ModelState.AddModelError(nameof(m.CampusId), "This Campus ID is already registered to another account.");

        if (!ModelState.IsValid) return View(m);

        user.CampusId = campusId;
        var update = await users.UpdateAsync(user);
        if (!update.Succeeded)
        {
            foreach (var e in update.Errors) ModelState.AddModelError(string.Empty, e.Description);
            return View(m);
        }

        await audit.LogAsync(user.Id, "GoogleOAuthRegistered", $"CampusId={campusId}; Source=GoogleOAuth");
        log.LogInformation("Campus ID completed for {UserId}", user.Id);

        return LocalRedirect(target);
    }

    // Breaks the cross-site redirect chain so the SameSite=Strict app cookie is sent on the next request
    private ContentResult ClientRedirect(string url)
    {
        Response.Headers.CacheControl = "no-store";
        var safe = WebUtility.HtmlEncode(url);
        return new ContentResult
        {
            ContentType = "text/html; charset=utf-8",
            Content = $"<!DOCTYPE html><html><head><meta charset=\"utf-8\"><meta http-equiv=\"refresh\" content=\"0;url={safe}\"><title>Signing in...</title></head><body style=\"background:#1c1917;color:#a8a29e;font-family:sans-serif;text-align:center;padding-top:20vh\">Signing you in...<noscript> <a href=\"{safe}\">Continue</a></noscript></body></html>"
        };
    }

    [HttpPost, Authorize, ValidateAntiForgeryToken]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Logout()
    {
        await signIn.SignOutAsync();
        return RedirectToAction("Index", "Home");
    }

    [HttpGet, AllowAnonymous]
    public IActionResult ForgotPassword() => View(new ForgotPasswordViewModel());

    // Password reset
    [HttpPost, AllowAnonymous]
    public async Task<IActionResult> ForgotPassword(ForgotPasswordViewModel m)
    {
        if (!ModelState.IsValid) return View(m);

        var user = await users.FindByEmailAsync(m.Email);

        if (user is null)
        {
            log.LogWarning("Password reset requested for unknown email {Email}", m.Email);
        }
        else if (!await users.IsEmailConfirmedAsync(user))
        {
            log.LogWarning("Password reset skipped: email {Email} is not confirmed yet", m.Email);
        }
        else
        {
            var token = await users.GeneratePasswordResetTokenAsync(user);
            var link = Url.Action(nameof(ResetPassword), "Account",
                new { email = user.Email, token }, Request.Scheme)!;
            await email.SendAsync(user.Email!, "Reset your BrewStop password",
                $"<p>Reset your password: <a href=\"{link}\">click here</a>.</p>");
        }

        return View("Info", new InfoViewModel("Check your email",
            "If that account exists, a reset link has been sent."));
    }

    [HttpGet, AllowAnonymous]
    public IActionResult ResetPassword(string? email, string? token)
    {
        if (email is null || token is null) return BadRequest();
        return View(new ResetPasswordViewModel { Email = email, Token = token });
    }

    [HttpPost, AllowAnonymous]
    public async Task<IActionResult> ResetPassword(ResetPasswordViewModel m)
    {
        if (!ModelState.IsValid) return View(m);

        var user = await users.FindByEmailAsync(m.Email);
        if (user is not null)
        {
            var result = await users.ResetPasswordAsync(user, m.Token, m.Password);
            if (!result.Succeeded)
            {
                foreach (var e in result.Errors) ModelState.AddModelError("", e.Description);
                return View(m);
            }
        }

        return View("Info", new InfoViewModel("Password updated", "You can now sign in with your new password."));
    }

    [HttpGet, AllowAnonymous]
    public IActionResult AccessDenied()
    {
        Response.StatusCode = StatusCodes.Status403Forbidden;
        return View();
    }

    // Helpers
    private async Task SendConfirmationAsync(ApplicationUser user)
    {
        var token = await users.GenerateEmailConfirmationTokenAsync(user);
        var link = Url.Action(nameof(ConfirmEmail), "Account",
            new { userId = user.Id, token }, Request.Scheme)!;

        await email.SendAsync(user.Email!, "Confirm your BrewStop account",
            $"<p>Hi {WebUtility.HtmlEncode(user.FullName)}, <a href=\"{link}\">confirm your email</a>.</p>");
    }

    [HttpPost, AllowAnonymous]
    public async Task<IActionResult> ResendConfirmation(string emailAddress)
    {
        var user = await users.FindByEmailAsync(emailAddress);
        if (user is not null && !await users.IsEmailConfirmedAsync(user))
            await SendConfirmationAsync(user);

        return View("Info", new InfoViewModel("Check your email",
            "If that account needs verification, a new link has been sent."));
    }

    // ===================== Account Settings Hub =====================
    private const long MaxAvatarBytes = 2 * 1024 * 1024;
    private static readonly string[] AvatarExts = [".jpg", ".png", ".webp"];
    private string AvatarDir => Path.Combine(env.WebRootPath, "uploads", "avatars");

    // Accepts 09XXXXXXXXX, 639XXXXXXXXX or +639XXXXXXXXX and stores +639XXXXXXXXX. Empty is allowed.
    private static bool TryNormalizePhone(string? raw, out string? phone)
    {
        phone = null;
        var s = new string((raw ?? string.Empty).Where(c => char.IsDigit(c) || c == '+').ToArray());
        if (s.Length == 0) return true;

        string national;
        if (s.StartsWith("+63")) national = s[3..];
        else if (s.StartsWith("63")) national = s[2..];
        else if (s.StartsWith("09")) national = s[1..];
        else return false;

        if (!Regex.IsMatch(national, @"^9\d{9}$")) return false;
        phone = "+63" + national;
        return true;
    }

    // Checks the real file signature (magic bytes), not just the file name.
    private static string? DetectImageExt(byte[] h, int n)
    {
        if (n >= 3 && h[0] == 0xFF && h[1] == 0xD8 && h[2] == 0xFF) return ".jpg";
        if (n >= 8 && h[0] == 0x89 && h[1] == 0x50 && h[2] == 0x4E && h[3] == 0x47
                   && h[4] == 0x0D && h[5] == 0x0A && h[6] == 0x1A && h[7] == 0x0A) return ".png";
        if (n >= 12 && h[0] == 'R' && h[1] == 'I' && h[2] == 'F' && h[3] == 'F'
                    && h[8] == 'W' && h[9] == 'E' && h[10] == 'B' && h[11] == 'P') return ".webp";
        return null;
    }

    private void DeleteAvatarFile(string? url)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(url) || !url.StartsWith("/uploads/avatars/")) return;
        var path = Path.Combine(AvatarDir, Path.GetFileName(url));
        if (System.IO.File.Exists(path)) System.IO.File.Delete(path);
    }

    private async Task<ApplicationUser?> CurrentUserAsync()
    {
        var u = await users.GetUserAsync(User);
        return u is { IsDeleted: false } ? u : null;
    }

    private async Task<IActionResult> ForceLogoutAsync()
    {
        await signIn.SignOutAsync();
        return RedirectToAction(nameof(Login));
    }

    // ---- Personal Information ----
    [HttpGet, Authorize]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Manage()
    {
        var user = await CurrentUserAsync();
        if (user is null) return await ForceLogoutAsync();

        return View(new ManageProfileViewModel
        {
            Email = user.Email ?? string.Empty,
            AvatarUrl = user.AvatarUrl,
            FullName = user.FullName,
            CampusId = user.CampusId,
            PhoneNumber = user.PhoneNumber
        });
    }

    [HttpPost, Authorize]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Manage(ManageProfileViewModel m)
    {
        var user = await CurrentUserAsync();
        if (user is null) return await ForceLogoutAsync();

        m.Email = user.Email ?? string.Empty;
        m.AvatarUrl = user.AvatarUrl;

        var campusId = m.CampusId?.Trim() ?? string.Empty;
        if (campusId.Length > 0 &&
            await users.Users.AnyAsync(u => u.Id != user.Id && u.CampusId == campusId))
            ModelState.AddModelError(nameof(m.CampusId), "Campus ID is already registered.");

        if (!TryNormalizePhone(m.PhoneNumber, out var phone))
            ModelState.AddModelError(nameof(m.PhoneNumber), "Enter a PH mobile number like 09123456789 or +639123456789.");

        string? newExt = null;
        if (m.Avatar is { Length: > 0 })
        {
            var ext = Path.GetExtension(m.Avatar.FileName).ToLowerInvariant();
            if (m.Avatar.Length > MaxAvatarBytes)
                ModelState.AddModelError(nameof(m.Avatar), "Photo must be 2 MB or smaller.");
            else if (!AvatarExts.Contains(ext))
                ModelState.AddModelError(nameof(m.Avatar), "Only .jpg, .png or .webp photos are allowed.");
            else
            {
                var head = new byte[12];
                int read;
                await using (var s = m.Avatar.OpenReadStream())
                    read = await s.ReadAsync(head.AsMemory(0, 12));

                newExt = DetectImageExt(head, read);
                if (newExt is null || newExt != ext)
                    ModelState.AddModelError(nameof(m.Avatar), "That file is not a valid image.");
            }
        }

        if (!ModelState.IsValid) return View(m);

        if (newExt is not null)
        {
            Directory.CreateDirectory(AvatarDir);
            var fileName = $"{Guid.NewGuid():N}{newExt}";
            await using (var fs = System.IO.File.Create(Path.Combine(AvatarDir, fileName)))
                await m.Avatar!.CopyToAsync(fs);

            DeleteAvatarFile(user.AvatarUrl);
            user.AvatarUrl = $"/uploads/avatars/{fileName}";
        }

        user.FullName = m.FullName.Trim();
        user.CampusId = campusId;
        user.PhoneNumber = phone;

        var result = await users.UpdateAsync(user);
        if (!result.Succeeded)
        {
            foreach (var e in result.Errors) ModelState.AddModelError("", e.Description);
            m.AvatarUrl = user.AvatarUrl;
            return View(m);
        }

        await signIn.RefreshSignInAsync(user);
        await audit.LogAsync(user.Id, "ProfileUpdated", "Profile details updated");
        TempData["Success"] = "Your profile has been updated.";
        return RedirectToAction(nameof(Manage));
    }

    // ---- Change / Set Password ----
    [HttpGet, Authorize]
    public async Task<IActionResult> ChangePassword()
    {
        var user = await CurrentUserAsync();
        if (user is null) return await ForceLogoutAsync();

        return View(new ChangePasswordViewModel { HasPassword = await users.HasPasswordAsync(user) });
    }

    [HttpPost, Authorize]
    public async Task<IActionResult> ChangePassword(ChangePasswordViewModel m)
    {
        var user = await CurrentUserAsync();
        if (user is null) return await ForceLogoutAsync();

        var has = await users.HasPasswordAsync(user);
        m.HasPassword = has;

        if (has && string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(m.CurrentPassword))
            ModelState.AddModelError(nameof(m.CurrentPassword), "Enter your current password.");

        if (!ModelState.IsValid) return View(m);

        IdentityResult result = has
            ? await users.ChangePasswordAsync(user, m.CurrentPassword!, m.NewPassword)
            : await users.AddPasswordAsync(user, m.NewPassword);

        if (!result.Succeeded)
        {
            foreach (var e in result.Errors) ModelState.AddModelError("", e.Description);
            return View(m);
        }

        // New security stamp signs out other devices; refresh keeps this session alive.
        await signIn.RefreshSignInAsync(user);
        await audit.LogAsync(user.Id, has ? "PasswordChanged" : "PasswordSet");

        TempData["Success"] = has ? "Your password has been changed." : "Your password has been set.";
        return RedirectToAction(nameof(ChangePassword));
    }

    // ---- Security ----
    [HttpGet, Authorize]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Security()
    {
        var user = await CurrentUserAsync();
        if (user is null) return await ForceLogoutAsync();

        var lockout = identityOptions.Value.Lockout;
        var logins = await users.GetLoginsAsync(user);

        return View(new SecurityViewModel
        {
            Email = user.Email ?? string.Empty,
            EmailConfirmed = await users.IsEmailConfirmedAsync(user),
            HasPassword = await users.HasPasswordAsync(user),
            LockoutEnabled = await users.GetLockoutEnabledAsync(user),
            IsLockedOut = await users.IsLockedOutAsync(user),
            MaxFailedAttempts = lockout.MaxFailedAccessAttempts,
            LockoutMinutes = (int)lockout.DefaultLockoutTimeSpan.TotalMinutes,
            GoogleLinked = logins.Any(l => l.LoginProvider == "Google")
        });
    }

    [HttpPost, Authorize]
    public async Task<IActionResult> SignOutAllDevices()
    {
        var user = await CurrentUserAsync();
        if (user is null) return await ForceLogoutAsync();

        await users.UpdateSecurityStampAsync(user);
        await audit.LogAsync(user.Id, "SignedOutAllDevices");
        await signIn.SignOutAsync();

        return RedirectToAction(nameof(Login));
    }

    // ---- Notifications ----
    [HttpGet, Authorize]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Notifications()
    {
        var user = await CurrentUserAsync();
        if (user is null) return await ForceLogoutAsync();

        return View(new NotificationsViewModel
        {
            NotifyOrderReady = user.NotifyOrderReady,
            NotifyPromotions = user.NotifyPromotions
        });
    }

    [HttpPost, Authorize]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Notifications(NotificationsViewModel m)
    {
        var user = await CurrentUserAsync();
        if (user is null) return await ForceLogoutAsync();

        user.NotifyOrderReady = m.NotifyOrderReady;
        user.NotifyPromotions = m.NotifyPromotions;

        var result = await users.UpdateAsync(user);
        if (!result.Succeeded)
        {
            foreach (var e in result.Errors) ModelState.AddModelError("", e.Description);
            return View(m);
        }

        await audit.LogAsync(user.Id, "NotificationsUpdated");
        TempData["Success"] = "Notification settings saved.";
        return RedirectToAction(nameof(Notifications));
    }

    // ---- Activity ----
    private static readonly string[] CustomerVisibleActions =
    [
        "OrderPlaced", "OrderCancelled", "PromoApplied", "ReviewCreated",
        "ProfileUpdated", "PasswordChanged", "PasswordSet", "SignedOutAllDevices",
        "NotificationsUpdated", "ExternalLogin", "ExternalLoginLinked",
        "UserRegistered", "GoogleOAuthRegistered", "AccountDeleted"
    ];

    [HttpGet, Authorize]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Activity()
    {
        var user = await CurrentUserAsync();
        if (user is null) return await ForceLogoutAsync();

        var entries = await db.AuditLogs
            .AsNoTracking()
            .Where(a => a.UserId == user.Id && CustomerVisibleActions.Contains(a.Action))
            .OrderByDescending(a => a.CreatedAtUtc)
            .Take(50)
            .ToListAsync();

        return View(new ActivityViewModel { Entries = entries });
    }

    // ---- Delete Account (soft delete + PII anonymization) ----
    [HttpGet, Authorize]
    public async Task<IActionResult> DeleteAccount()
    {
        var user = await CurrentUserAsync();
        if (user is null) return await ForceLogoutAsync();

        return View(new DeleteAccountViewModel { HasPassword = await users.HasPasswordAsync(user) });
    }

    [HttpPost, Authorize]
    public async Task<IActionResult> DeleteAccount(string? confirmPassword, string? confirmText)
    {
        var user = await CurrentUserAsync();
        if (user is null) return await ForceLogoutAsync();

        var has = await users.HasPasswordAsync(user);
        var vm = new DeleteAccountViewModel { HasPassword = has };

        var hasActiveOrders = await db.Orders.AnyAsync(o => o.UserId == user.Id &&
            (o.Status == OrderStatus.Placed || o.Status == OrderStatus.Preparing || o.Status == OrderStatus.Ready));
        if (hasActiveOrders)
        {
            ModelState.AddModelError("", "You still have active pickup orders. Wait until they are claimed or cancelled, then try again.");
            return View(vm);
        }

        var confirmed = has
            ? !string.IsNullOrEmpty(confirmPassword) && await users.CheckPasswordAsync(user, confirmPassword)
            : string.Equals(confirmText?.Trim(), "DELETE", StringComparison.Ordinal);
        if (!confirmed)
        {
            ModelState.AddModelError("", has ? "Incorrect password." : "Type DELETE exactly to confirm.");
            return View(vm);
        }

        var oldAvatar = user.AvatarUrl;
        await using var tx = await db.Database.BeginTransactionAsync();
        try
        {
            var token = Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N")[..8].ToUpperInvariant();

            foreach (var login in await users.GetLoginsAsync(user))
                await users.RemoveLoginAsync(user, login.LoginProvider, login.ProviderKey);

            user.FullName = "Deleted User";
            user.CampusId = $"DELETED-{token}";
            user.UserName = $"deleted-{token}@deleted.invalid";
            user.Email = user.UserName;
            user.EmailConfirmed = false;
            user.PhoneNumber = null;
            user.PhoneNumberConfirmed = false;
            user.AvatarUrl = null;
            user.PasswordHash = null;
            user.NotifyOrderReady = false;
            user.NotifyPromotions = false;
            user.IsDeleted = true;
            user.DeletedAtUtc = DateTime.UtcNow;
            user.LockoutEnabled = true;
            user.LockoutEnd = DateTimeOffset.MaxValue;
            user.SecurityStamp = Guid.NewGuid().ToString();

            var result = await users.UpdateAsync(user);
            if (!result.Succeeded)
                throw new InvalidOperationException(string.Join("; ", result.Errors.Select(e => e.Description)));

            audit.Add(user.Id, "AccountDeleted", "Personal data anonymized");
            await db.SaveChangesAsync();
            await tx.CommitAsync();
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            await tx.RollbackAsync();
            log.LogError(ex, "Account deletion failed for {UserId}", user.Id);
            ModelState.AddModelError("", "We couldn't delete your account. Please try again.");
            return View(vm);
        }

        DeleteAvatarFile(oldAvatar);
        await signIn.SignOutAsync();
        HttpContext.Session.Clear();

        return View("Info", new InfoViewModel("Account deleted",
            "Your personal data has been erased. Past orders are kept in anonymized form for one academic year."));
    }
}


========================================
FILE: BrewStop\Controllers\BaristaController.cs
========================================
using System.Security.Claims;
using BrewStop.Data;
using BrewStop.Hubs;
using BrewStop.Models;
using BrewStop.Services;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.SignalR;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace BrewStop.Controllers;

[Authorize(Roles = $"{AppRoles.Barista},{AppRoles.Admin}")]
[Route("Barista")]
public class BaristaController(
    ApplicationDbContext db,
    IQrTokenService qr,
    IAuditService audit,
    IHubContext<OrderHub> hub,
    IPickupSlotService slots) : Controller
{
    public const int LowStockThreshold = 5;

    private static readonly string[] ReturnTargets = ["Queue", "Preparation", "Status"];
    private static readonly string[] DashboardActions =
        ["OrderPlaced", "OrderCancelled", "OrderStatusChanged", "QrScanAccepted", "QrScanRejected"];

    private string UserId => User.FindFirstValue(ClaimTypes.NameIdentifier)!;

    private IQueryable<Order> Board() => db.Orders.AsNoTracking()
        .Include(o => o.User)
        .Include(o => o.Items).ThenInclude(i => i.AddOns);

    private static string? Field(string? details, string key) =>
        details?.Split(';', StringSplitOptions.TrimEntries)
            .FirstOrDefault(p => p.StartsWith(key + "=", StringComparison.Ordinal))?[(key.Length + 1)..];

    // ---------------- Dashboard ----------------
    [HttpGet(""), HttpGet("Dashboard")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Index()
    {
        var nowUtc = DateTime.UtcNow;
        var localNow = slots.ToLocal(nowUtc);
        var dayStart = nowUtc - (localNow - localNow.Date);

        var topRows = await db.Orders.AsNoTracking()
            .Where(o => o.CreatedAtUtc >= dayStart && o.Status != OrderStatus.Cancelled)
            .SelectMany(o => o.Items)
            .GroupBy(i => i.ItemName)
            .Select(g => new { Name = g.Key, Qty = g.Sum(x => x.Quantity) })
            .OrderByDescending(x => x.Qty)
            .Take(5)
            .ToListAsync();

        var logs = await db.AuditLogs.AsNoTracking()
            .Where(a => DashboardActions.Contains(a.Action))
            .OrderByDescending(a => a.CreatedAtUtc)
            .Take(40)
            .ToListAsync();

        var recent = new List<ActivityDto>();
        foreach (var a in logs)
        {
            var id = Field(a.Details, "OrderId");
            var transition = a.Details?.Split(';', StringSplitOptions.TrimEntries).Skip(1).FirstOrDefault() ?? "";
            switch (a.Action)
            {
                case "OrderPlaced":
                    recent.Add(new ActivityDto("New order placed", $"Order #{id} entered the queue", "fa-bag-shopping", a.CreatedAtUtc));
                    break;
                case "OrderCancelled":
                    recent.Add(new ActivityDto("Order cancelled", $"Order #{id} was cancelled by the customer", "fa-ban", a.CreatedAtUtc));
                    break;
                case "OrderStatusChanged" when transition.EndsWith("->Cancelled") || transition.EndsWith("->Claimed"):
                    break; // already covered by OrderCancelled / QrScanAccepted
                case "OrderStatusChanged":
                    recent.Add(new ActivityDto("Status updated", $"Order #{id}: {transition.Replace("->", " ? ")}", "fa-arrows-rotate", a.CreatedAtUtc));
                    break;
                case "QrScanAccepted":
                    recent.Add(new ActivityDto("Order picked up", $"Order #{id} claimed with a verified QR code", "fa-circle-check", a.CreatedAtUtc));
                    break;
                case "QrScanRejected":
                    recent.Add(new ActivityDto("QR scan rejected", $"Order #{id}: {Field(a.Details, "Reason") ?? "invalid code"}", "fa-triangle-exclamation", a.CreatedAtUtc));
                    break;
            }
            if (recent.Count == 12) break;
        }

        var vm = new BaristaDashboardViewModel
        {
            LocalNow = localNow,
            Placed = await db.Orders.CountAsync(o => o.Status == OrderStatus.Placed),
            Preparing = await db.Orders.CountAsync(o => o.Status == OrderStatus.Preparing),
            Ready = await db.Orders.CountAsync(o => o.Status == OrderStatus.Ready),
            OrdersToday = await db.Orders.CountAsync(o => o.CreatedAtUtc >= dayStart),
            ClaimedToday = await db.Orders.CountAsync(o => o.CreatedAtUtc >= dayStart && o.Status == OrderStatus.Claimed),
            CancelledToday = await db.Orders.CountAsync(o => o.CreatedAtUtc >= dayStart && o.Status == OrderStatus.Cancelled),
            LowStockCount = await db.InventoryItems.CountAsync(i => i.Quantity <= LowStockThreshold),
            TopItems = topRows.Select(r => new TopItemDto(r.Name, r.Qty)).ToList(),
            Recent = recent,
            OpenShift = await db.BaristaShifts.AsNoTracking()
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(s => s.BaristaUserId == UserId && s.ClockInAt != null && s.ClockOutAt == null)
        };
        return View("Dashboard", vm);
    }

    // ---------------- Order Queue / Preparation / Status ----------------
    [HttpGet("Queue")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Queue() =>
        View(await Board().Where(o => o.Status == OrderStatus.Placed).OrderBy(o => o.CreatedAtUtc).ToListAsync());

    [HttpGet("Preparation")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Preparation() =>
        View(await Board().Where(o => o.Status == OrderStatus.Preparing).OrderBy(o => o.PickupAtUtc).ToListAsync());

    [HttpGet("Status")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Status() =>
        View(await Board()
            .Where(o => o.Status == OrderStatus.Placed || o.Status == OrderStatus.Preparing || o.Status == OrderStatus.Ready)
            .OrderBy(o => o.PickupAtUtc)
            .ToListAsync());

    [HttpPost("Advance/{id:int}")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Advance(int id, string? returnTo)
    {
        var target = ReturnTargets.Contains(returnTo) ? returnTo! : "Status";

        var order = await db.Orders.AsNoTracking().FirstOrDefaultAsync(o => o.Id == id);
        if (order is null) return NotFound();

        OrderStatus? next = order.Status switch
        {
            OrderStatus.Placed => OrderStatus.Preparing,
            OrderStatus.Preparing => OrderStatus.Ready,
            _ => null
        };

        if (next is null)
        {
            TempData["Error"] = $"Order #{id} cannot be advanced from {order.Status}.";
            return RedirectToAction(target);
        }

        var from = order.Status;
        var to = next.Value;

        var rows = await db.Orders.Where(o => o.Id == id && o.Status == from)
            .ExecuteUpdateAsync(s => s.SetProperty(o => o.Status, to));

        if (rows == 0)
        {
            TempData["Error"] = $"Order #{id} changed in the meantime. Please review the queue.";
            return RedirectToAction(target);
        }

        await audit.LogAsync(UserId, "OrderStatusChanged", $"OrderId={id}; {from}->{to}");
        await hub.Clients.Group($"user:{order.UserId}")
            .SendAsync("OrderStatusChanged", new { orderId = id, status = to.ToString() });
        await hub.Clients.Group("staff").SendAsync("QueueChanged");

        TempData["Success"] = to == OrderStatus.Preparing
            ? $"Order #{id} accepted and now Preparing."
            : $"Order #{id} is Ready for pickup.";
        return RedirectToAction(target);
    }

    [HttpPost("Scan")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Scan(string? token)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(token) || token.Length > 512)
        {
            TempData["Error"] = "Scan or paste a pickup QR token.";
            return RedirectToAction(nameof(Status));
        }

        var (result, orderId) = await qr.ClaimAsync(token.Trim(), UserId, HttpContext.RequestAborted);

        if (result == ScanResult.Ok)
        {
            var ownerId = await db.Orders.AsNoTracking()
                .Where(o => o.Id == orderId).Select(o => o.UserId).FirstOrDefaultAsync();
            if (ownerId is not null)
                await hub.Clients.Group($"user:{ownerId}")
                    .SendAsync("OrderStatusChanged", new { orderId, status = nameof(OrderStatus.Claimed) });
            await hub.Clients.Group("staff").SendAsync("QueueChanged");

            TempData["Success"] = $"Order #{orderId} claimed. Collect payment at the counter.";
        }
        else
            TempData["Error"] = result switch
            {
                ScanResult.AlreadyUsed => $"Rejected: order #{orderId} was already claimed.",
                ScanResult.Expired => $"Rejected: pickup code for order #{orderId} has expired.",
                ScanResult.Revoked => $"Rejected: order #{orderId} was cancelled.",
                ScanResult.WrongStatus => $"Rejected: order #{orderId} is not Ready yet.",
                _ => "Rejected: invalid QR code."
            };

        return RedirectToAction(nameof(Status));
    }

    // ---------------- Sidebar badge counts ----------------
    [HttpGet("Counts")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Counts()
    {
        var since = DateTime.UtcNow.AddHours(-24);
        var placed = await db.Orders.CountAsync(o => o.Status == OrderStatus.Placed);
        var preparing = await db.Orders.CountAsync(o => o.Status == OrderStatus.Preparing);
        var low = await db.InventoryItems.CountAsync(i => i.Quantity <= LowStockThreshold);
        var notes = await db.Orders.CountAsync(o =>
            (o.Status == OrderStatus.Placed || o.Status == OrderStatus.Preparing) && o.Note != null && o.Note != "");
        var cancelled = await db.AuditLogs.CountAsync(a => a.Action == "OrderCancelled" && a.CreatedAtUtc >= since);
        return Json(new { placed, preparing, alerts = low + notes + cancelled });
    }

    // ---------------- Notifications ----------------
    [HttpGet("Notifications")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Notifications()
    {
        var since = DateTime.UtcNow.AddHours(-24);
        var list = new List<BaristaAlertDto>();

        var stock = await db.InventoryItems.AsNoTracking()
            .Where(i => i.Quantity <= LowStockThreshold)
            .OrderBy(i => i.Quantity).ThenBy(i => i.MenuItem.Name)
            .Select(i => new { i.MenuItem.Name, i.Quantity })
            .ToListAsync();
        list.AddRange(stock.Select(s => new BaristaAlertDto(
            "LowStock",
            s.Quantity == 0 ? "Out of stock" : "Low stock",
            s.Quantity == 0 ? $"{s.Name} is sold out. Customers cannot order it." : $"{s.Name}: only {s.Quantity} left.",
            null, null, s.Quantity == 0)));

        var newOrders = await db.Orders.AsNoTracking()
            .Where(o => o.Status == OrderStatus.Placed)
            .OrderByDescending(o => o.CreatedAtUtc).Take(30)
            .Select(o => new { o.Id, o.User.FullName, o.CreatedAtUtc, Qty = o.Items.Sum(i => i.Quantity) })
            .ToListAsync();
        list.AddRange(newOrders.Select(o => new BaristaAlertDto(
            "NewOrder", $"New order #{o.Id}", $"{o.FullName} · {o.Qty} item(s) waiting to be accepted", o.Id, o.CreatedAtUtc)));

        var noted = await db.Orders.AsNoTracking()
            .Where(o => (o.Status == OrderStatus.Placed || o.Status == OrderStatus.Preparing) && o.Note != null && o.Note != "")
            .OrderByDescending(o => o.CreatedAtUtc).Take(30)
            .Select(o => new { o.Id, o.Note, o.CreatedAtUtc })
            .ToListAsync();
        list.AddRange(noted.Select(o => new BaristaAlertDto(
            "Note", $"Special instructions · Order #{o.Id}", o.Note!, o.Id, o.CreatedAtUtc)));

        var cancelled = await db.AuditLogs.AsNoTracking()
            .Where(a => a.Action == "OrderCancelled" && a.CreatedAtUtc >= since)
            .OrderByDescending(a => a.CreatedAtUtc).Take(30)
            .ToListAsync();
        foreach (var c in cancelled)
        {
            int.TryParse(Field(c.Details, "OrderId"), out var oid);
            list.Add(new BaristaAlertDto("Cancelled", $"Order #{oid} cancelled", "The customer cancelled before preparation.", oid, c.CreatedAtUtc));
        }

        var sorted = list.OrderByDescending(a => a.AtUtc == null).ThenByDescending(a => a.AtUtc).ToList();
        return View(sorted);
    }

    // ---------------- Coffee Recipe Guide (read-only reference) ----------------
    [HttpGet("Recipes")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Recipes() =>
        View(new RecipeGuideViewModel
        {
            Categories = await db.Categories.AsNoTracking().Include(c => c.MenuItems).OrderBy(c => c.Id).ToListAsync(),
            Sizes = await db.ItemSizes.AsNoTracking().Where(s => s.IsActive).OrderBy(s => s.SortOrder).ToListAsync(),
            AddOns = await db.AddOns.AsNoTracking().Where(a => a.IsActive).OrderBy(a => a.Name).ToListAsync()
        });

    // ---------------- Shift Management ----------------
    [HttpGet("Shifts")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Shifts()
    {
        var uid = UserId;
        var nowUtc = DateTime.UtcNow;
        var localNow = slots.ToLocal(nowUtc);
        var dayStart = nowUtc - (localNow - localNow.Date);
        var weekStartLocal = localNow.Date.AddDays(-(((int)localNow.DayOfWeek + 6) % 7));
        var weekStart = nowUtc - (localNow - weekStartLocal);

        var weekShifts = await db.BaristaShifts.AsNoTracking()
            .Where(s => s.BaristaUserId == uid && s.ClockInAt >= weekStart)
            .ToListAsync();

        decimal Hours(BaristaShift s) =>
            s.ClockOutAt is null ? (decimal)(nowUtc - s.ClockInAt!.Value).TotalHours : s.TotalHours ?? 0m;

        return View(new ShiftsViewModel
        {
            Open = await db.BaristaShifts.AsNoTracking()
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(s => s.BaristaUserId == uid && s.ClockInAt != null && s.ClockOutAt == null),
            Upcoming = await db.BaristaShifts.AsNoTracking()
                .Where(s => s.BaristaUserId == uid && s.ClockInAt == null && s.ShiftStatus == ShiftStatus.Scheduled && s.ScheduledEnd >= nowUtc)
                .OrderBy(s => s.ScheduledStart).Take(10).ToListAsync(),
            History = await db.BaristaShifts.AsNoTracking()
                .Where(s => s.BaristaUserId == uid && s.ClockOutAt != null)
                .OrderByDescending(s => s.ClockInAt).Take(30).ToListAsync(),
            HoursThisWeek = Math.Round(weekShifts.Sum(s => Hours(s)), 2),
            HoursToday = Math.Round(weekShifts.Where(s => s.ClockInAt >= dayStart).Sum(s => Hours(s)), 2)
        });
    }

    [HttpPost("Shifts/ClockIn")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> ClockIn()
    {
        var uid = UserId;
        var now = DateTime.UtcNow;

        if (await db.BaristaShifts.AnyAsync(s => s.BaristaUserId == uid && s.ClockInAt != null && s.ClockOutAt == null))
        {
            TempData["Error"] = "You are already clocked in.";
            return RedirectToAction(nameof(Shifts));
        }

        // Match an assigned shift (clock-in allowed up to 2 hours early); otherwise record an unscheduled shift.
        var assignedId = await db.BaristaShifts
            .Where(s => s.BaristaUserId == uid && s.ClockInAt == null && s.ShiftStatus == ShiftStatus.Scheduled
                        && s.ScheduledStart <= now.AddHours(2) && s.ScheduledEnd >= now)
            .OrderBy(s => s.ScheduledStart)
            .Select(s => s.Id)
            .FirstOrDefaultAsync();

        int shiftId;
        if (assignedId != 0)
        {
            var rows = await db.BaristaShifts.Where(s => s.Id == assignedId && s.ClockInAt == null)
                .ExecuteUpdateAsync(u => u
                    .SetProperty(s => s.ClockInAt, now)
                    .SetProperty(s => s.ShiftStatus, ShiftStatus.InProgress));
            if (rows == 0)
            {
                TempData["Error"] = "That shift was just updated. Please try again.";
                return RedirectToAction(nameof(Shifts));
            }
            shiftId = assignedId;
        }
        else
        {
            var shift = new BaristaShift
            {
                BaristaUserId = uid,
                ScheduledStart = now,
                ScheduledEnd = now,   // start == end marks an unscheduled shift
                ClockInAt = now,
                ShiftStatus = ShiftStatus.InProgress
            };
            db.BaristaShifts.Add(shift);
            await db.SaveChangesAsync();
            shiftId = shift.Id;
        }

        await audit.LogAsync(uid, "ShiftClockIn", $"ShiftId={shiftId}");
        TempData["Success"] = "You are clocked in. Have a great shift!";
        return RedirectToAction(nameof(Shifts));
    }

    [HttpPost("Shifts/ClockOut")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> ClockOut()
    {
        var uid = UserId;
        var now = DateTime.UtcNow;

        var open = await db.BaristaShifts.AsNoTracking()
            .FirstOrDefaultAsync(s => s.BaristaUserId == uid && s.ClockInAt != null && s.ClockOutAt == null);
        if (open is null)
        {
            TempData["Error"] = "You are not clocked in.";
            return RedirectToAction(nameof(Shifts));
        }

        var hours = Math.Round((decimal)(now - open.ClockInAt!.Value).TotalHours, 2);
        var rows = await db.BaristaShifts.Where(s => s.Id == open.Id && s.ClockOutAt == null)
            .ExecuteUpdateAsync(u => u
                .SetProperty(s => s.ClockOutAt, now)
                .SetProperty(s => s.TotalHours, hours)
                .SetProperty(s => s.ShiftStatus, ShiftStatus.Completed));

        if (rows == 0)
        {
            TempData["Error"] = "This shift was already closed.";
            return RedirectToAction(nameof(Shifts));
        }

        await audit.LogAsync(uid, "ShiftClockOut", $"ShiftId={open.Id}; Hours={hours:0.00}");
        TempData["Success"] = $"Clocked out. Shift total: {hours:0.00} h.";
        return RedirectToAction(nameof(Shifts));
    }
}


========================================
FILE: BrewStop\Controllers\CaptchaController.cs
========================================
using BrewStop.Services;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.StaticFiles;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Options;

namespace BrewStop.Controllers;

[AllowAnonymous, Route("captcha")]
public class CaptchaController(ICaptchaService captcha, IOptions<CaptchaOptions> options) : Controller
{
    public sealed record VerifyRequest(string ChallengeId, string[] Tiles, string Action);

    private static readonly FileExtensionContentTypeProvider Mime = new();

    [HttpGet("challenge")]
    public IActionResult Challenge([FromQuery] string action)
    {
        Response.Headers.CacheControl = "no-store";
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(action) || action.Length > 32) return BadRequest();

        var ch = captcha.CreateChallenge(action);
        if (ch is null) return StatusCode(503);

        return Json(new
        {
            id = ch.Id,
            tiles = ch.Tiles.Select(t => new { id = t.Id, src = $"{Url.Content("~/captcha/img")}/{ch.Id}/{t.Id}" })
        });
    }

    [HttpGet("img/{challengeId}/{tileId}")]
    public IActionResult Image(string challengeId, string tileId)
    {
        var file = captcha.GetTileFile(challengeId, tileId);
        if (file is null) return NotFound();

        Response.Headers.CacheControl = "no-store";
        Mime.TryGetContentType(file, out var type);
        return PhysicalFile(file, type ?? "application/octet-stream");
    }

    [HttpPost("verify")]
    public IActionResult Verify([FromBody] VerifyRequest r)
    {
        Response.Headers.CacheControl = "no-store";
        if (r?.Tiles is null || r.Tiles.Length > 9) return BadRequest();

        var token = captcha.Solve(r.ChallengeId, r.Tiles, r.Action);
        return token is null
            ? Ok(new { ok = false })
            : Ok(new { ok = true, token, expiresIn = options.Value.TokenMinutes * 60 });
    }
}


========================================
FILE: BrewStop\Controllers\CartController.cs
========================================
using System.Security.Claims;
using BrewStop.Data;
using BrewStop.Models;
using BrewStop.Services;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace BrewStop.Models
{
    public record CartRecommendationDto(
        int Id,
        string Name,
        string Description,
        decimal Price,
        string ImageUrl,
        string CategoryName,
        bool IsDrink,
        int? DefaultSizeId);

    public class CartPageViewModel : CartViewModel
    {
        public Dictionary<int, string> Descriptions { get; set; } = new();
        public HashSet<int> FavoriteIds { get; set; } = new();
        public bool IsAuthenticated { get; set; }
        public bool CanFavorite { get; set; }
        public List<CartRecommendationDto> Recommendations { get; set; } = new();

        public string? PromoCode { get; set; }
        public string? PromoTitle { get; set; }
        public decimal Discount { get; set; }
        public string? PromoError { get; set; }

        public bool HasPromo => !string.IsNullOrEmpty(PromoCode);
        public decimal EstimatedTotal => Math.Max(0m, Total - Discount);
        public bool HasSoldOut => Lines.Any(l => l.StockLeft <= 0);
    }
}

namespace BrewStop.Controllers
{
    public class CartController(
        ICartService cart,
        IInventoryService inventory,
        IImageResolver images,
        ApplicationDbContext db) : Controller
    {
        private const string PromoKey = "brewstop.cart.promo";

        private sealed record PromoPreview(bool Ok, string? Error, string? Code, string? Title, decimal Discount);

        [HttpGet]
        public async Task<IActionResult> Index()
        {
            var c = await cart.GetAsync();
            var vm = new CartPageViewModel { Lines = c.Lines };

            vm.IsAuthenticated = User.Identity?.IsAuthenticated == true;
            vm.CanFavorite = vm.IsAuthenticated
                && (User.IsInRole(AppRoles.Student) || User.IsInRole(AppRoles.Faculty));
            vm.PromoError = TempData["PromoError"] as string;

            if (vm.Lines.Count == 0)
            {
                HttpContext.Session.Remove(PromoKey);
                return View(vm);
            }

            var ids = vm.Lines.Select(l => l.MenuItemId).Distinct().ToList();

            var meta = await db.MenuItems.AsNoTracking()
                .Where(m => ids.Contains(m.Id))
                .Select(m => new { m.Id, m.Description, Group = m.Category.Group })
                .ToListAsync();
            vm.Descriptions = meta.ToDictionary(m => m.Id, m => m.Description);

            if (vm.CanFavorite)
            {
                var uid = User.FindFirstValue(ClaimTypes.NameIdentifier)!;
                var favs = await db.FavoriteItems.AsNoTracking()
                    .Where(f => f.UserId == uid && ids.Contains(f.MenuItemId))
                    .Select(f => f.MenuItemId)
                    .ToListAsync();
                vm.FavoriteIds = favs.ToHashSet();
            }

            var savedCode = HttpContext.Session.GetString(PromoKey);
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(savedCode))
            {
                var preview = await PreviewPromoAsync(savedCode, vm.Total);
                if (preview.Ok)
                {
                    vm.PromoCode = preview.Code;
                    vm.PromoTitle = preview.Title;
                    vm.Discount = preview.Discount;
                }
                else
                {
                    HttpContext.Session.Remove(PromoKey);
                    vm.PromoError ??= preview.Error;
                }
            }

            vm.Recommendations = await BuildRecommendationsAsync(
                ids, meta.Select(m => m.Group).ToHashSet());

            return View(vm);
        }

        [HttpGet]
        public async Task<IActionResult> Customize(int id)
        {
            var item = await db.MenuItems.AsNoTracking()
                .Include(m => m.Category)
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(m => m.Id == id);
            if (item is null) return NotFound();

            var stockLeft = (await inventory.GetStockAsync(new[] { id })).GetValueOrDefault(id);
            if (stockLeft <= 0)
            {
                TempData["MenuError"] = $"{item.Name} is sold out.";
                return RedirectToAction("Index", "Menu");
            }

            var isDrink = item.Category.Group == "Drinks";
            var group = item.Category.Group;

            var sizes = isDrink
                ? await db.ItemSizes.AsNoTracking().Where(s => s.IsActive).OrderBy(s => s.SortOrder).ToListAsync()
                : new List<ItemSize>();
            var addOns = await db.AddOns.AsNoTracking()
                .Where(a => a.IsActive && a.Group == group)
                .OrderBy(a => a.Name)
                .ToListAsync();

            return View(new CustomizeViewModel
            {
                Item = item,
                IsDrink = isDrink,
                Sizes = sizes,
                AddOns = addOns,
                DefaultSizeId = sizes.FirstOrDefault()?.Id
            });
        }

        [HttpPost]
        public async Task<IActionResult> Add(AddToCartInput input)
        {
            var (ok, error) = await cart.AddAsync(input.MenuItemId, input.SizeId, input.AddOnIds, input.Quantity);
            if (!ok)
            {
                TempData["CartError"] = error;
                return RedirectToAction(nameof(Customize), new { id = input.MenuItemId });
            }
            return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
        }

        [HttpPost]
        public IActionResult Update(string key, int quantity)
        {
            cart.SetQuantity(key, quantity);
            return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
        }

        [HttpPost]
        public IActionResult Remove(string key)
        {
            cart.Remove(key);
            return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
        }

        // Read-only preview: never increments Promotion.TimesUsed (that only happens when the order is placed).
        [HttpPost]
        public async Task<IActionResult> ApplyPromo(string? promoCode)
        {
            var code = promoCode?.Trim().ToUpperInvariant();

            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(code))
            {
                TempData["PromoError"] = "Enter a promo code first.";
                return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
            }

            if (code.Length > 30)
            {
                TempData["PromoError"] = "Invalid promo code.";
                return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
            }

            var c = await cart.GetAsync();
            if (c.Lines.Count == 0) return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));

            var preview = await PreviewPromoAsync(code, c.Total);
            if (preview.Ok)
            {
                HttpContext.Session.SetString(PromoKey, preview.Code!);
            }
            else
            {
                HttpContext.Session.Remove(PromoKey);
                TempData["PromoError"] = preview.Error;
            }

            return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
        }

        [HttpPost]
        public IActionResult RemovePromo()
        {
            HttpContext.Session.Remove(PromoKey);
            return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
        }

        private async Task<PromoPreview> PreviewPromoAsync(string code, decimal subtotal)
        {
            static PromoPreview Fail(string msg) => new(false, msg, null, null, 0m);

            var normalized = code.Trim().ToUpperInvariant();
            var now = DateTime.UtcNow;

            var promo = await db.Promotions.AsNoTracking().FirstOrDefaultAsync(p => p.Code == normalized);
            if (promo is null || !promo.IsActive) return Fail("Invalid promo code.");
            if (promo.ExpiryDate < now) return Fail("This promo code has expired.");
            if (promo.MaxUses is not null && promo.TimesUsed >= promo.MaxUses)
                return Fail("This promo code has reached its usage limit.");

            var discount = promo.DiscountType == DiscountType.Percentage
                ? Math.Round(subtotal * promo.DiscountValue / 100m, 2, MidpointRounding.AwayFromZero)
                : promo.DiscountValue;
            discount = Math.Min(discount, subtotal);

            return new PromoPreview(true, null, promo.Code, promo.Title, discount);
        }

        private async Task<List<CartRecommendationDto>> BuildRecommendationsAsync(
            List<int> cartItemIds, HashSet<string> cartGroups)
        {
            var candidates = await db.MenuItems.AsNoTracking()
                .Where(m => !cartItemIds.Contains(m.Id)
                            && db.InventoryItems.Any(i => i.MenuItemId == m.Id && i.Quantity > 0))
                .Select(m => new
                {
                    m.Id,
                    m.Name,
                    m.Description,
                    m.Price,
                    m.ImageUrl,
                    CategoryName = m.Category.Name,
                    Group = m.Category.Group,
                    Sold = db.OrderItems.Where(o => o.MenuItemId == m.Id).Sum(o => (int?)o.Quantity) ?? 0
                })
                .ToListAsync();

            if (candidates.Count == 0) return new();

            var defaultSize = await db.ItemSizes.AsNoTracking()
                .Where(s => s.IsActive)
                .OrderBy(s => s.SortOrder)
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync();

            // Drinks need a size to be added in one click; skip them if no active size exists.
            var ranked = candidates
                .Where(x => x.Group != "Drinks" || defaultSize is not null)
                .OrderByDescending(x => x.Sold)
                .ThenBy(x => x.Name)
                .ToList();

            // Favor the category group the cart is missing, then fill with the rest.
            var complement = ranked.Where(x => !cartGroups.Contains(x.Group)).ToList();
            var same = ranked.Where(x => cartGroups.Contains(x.Group)).ToList();

            return complement.Take(2)
                .Concat(same.Take(2))
                .Concat(complement.Skip(2))
                .Concat(same.Skip(2))
                .Take(4)
                .Select(x =>
                {
                    var isDrink = x.Group == "Drinks";
                    return new CartRecommendationDto(
                        x.Id,
                        x.Name,
                        x.Description,
                        x.Price + (isDrink ? defaultSize!.PriceDelta : 0m),
                        images.Resolve(x.ImageUrl, x.Name),
                        x.CategoryName,
                        isDrink,
                        isDrink ? defaultSize!.Id : null);
                })
                .ToList();
        }
    }
}


========================================
FILE: BrewStop\Controllers\CheckoutController.cs
========================================
using System.ComponentModel.DataAnnotations;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Security.Claims;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;
using BrewStop.Data;
using BrewStop.Models;
using BrewStop.Services;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Identity;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace BrewStop.Controllers;

public class CheckoutContact
{
    public string? FullName { get; set; }
    public string? Email { get; set; }
    public string? Phone { get; set; }
}

[Authorize(Policy = "CustomerOnly")]
public class CheckoutController(
    ICartService cart,
    IPickupSlotService slots,
    ICaptchaService captcha,
    IInventoryService inventory,
    IAuditService audit,
    IQrTokenService qr,
    IPromotionService promotions,
    UserManager<ApplicationUser> users,
    ApplicationDbContext db,
    ILogger<CheckoutController> log) : Controller
{
    // Methods the checkout may offer. Card methods (Visa/Mastercard) are intentionally absent:
    // there is no payment-gateway integration in this codebase, and raw card data must never be handled here.
    // A method is offered only when it is ALSO defined in the PaymentMethod enum (backend-supported).
    private static readonly string[] Offered = ["cash", "gcash", "maya", "qrph"];

    private static readonly Regex PhoneRx = new(@"^\+?[0-9 \-]{7,20}$", RegexOptions.Compiled);

    private static string Norm(string s) =>
        new string(s.Where(char.IsLetterOrDigit).ToArray()).ToLowerInvariant();

    private static IReadOnlyList<PaymentMethod> SupportedMethods() =>
        Enum.GetValues<PaymentMethod>().Where(m => Offered.Contains(Norm(m.ToString()))).ToList();

    [HttpGet]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Index()
    {
        var c = await cart.GetAsync();
        if (c.Lines.Count == 0) return RedirectToAction("Index", "Cart");

        if (c.HasStockProblem)
        {
            TempData["CartError"] = "Some items in your cart are no longer available in the quantity you chose.";
            return RedirectToAction("Index", "Cart");
        }

        var user = await users.GetUserAsync(User);
        return Render(c, new CheckoutInput(), ContactFrom(user), terms: false, methodChosen: false);
    }

    [HttpPost("/Order/Checkout")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Place(
        [Bind(Prefix = "Input")] CheckoutInput input,
        [Bind(Prefix = "Contact")] CheckoutContact? contact,
        [FromForm(Name = "AcceptTerms")] bool acceptTerms)
    {
        var ct = HttpContext.RequestAborted;
        input ??= new CheckoutInput();

        var c = await cart.GetAsync();
        if (c.Lines.Count == 0) return RedirectToAction("Index", "Cart");

        var user = await users.GetUserAsync(User);

        if (contact is null || !Request.Form.Keys.Any(k => k.StartsWith("Contact.", StringComparison.Ordinal)))
            contact = ContactFrom(user);
        contact.FullName = (contact.FullName ?? "").Trim();
        contact.Email = (contact.Email ?? "").Trim();
        contact.Phone = (contact.Phone ?? "").Trim();
        ValidateContact(contact);

        DateTime pickupUtc = default;
        if (input.PickupSlot is null || !TryParseSlot(input.PickupSlot, out pickupUtc) || !slots.IsValid(pickupUtc))
            ModelState.AddModelError("Input.PickupSlot", "Please choose an available pickup time.");

        var methodChosen = Request.Form.ContainsKey("Input.PaymentMethod");
        if (!methodChosen || !Enum.IsDefined(input.PaymentMethod) || !SupportedMethods().Contains(input.PaymentMethod))
            ModelState.AddModelError("Input.PaymentMethod", "Please choose a supported payment method.");

        if (!acceptTerms)
            ModelState.AddModelError("AcceptTerms", "Please accept the ordering terms to continue.");

        var contactChanged = user is not null && (
            !Same(contact.FullName, user.FullName) ||
            !Same(contact.Email, user.Email) ||
            !Same(contact.Phone, user.PhoneNumber));

        var contactLine = contactChanged
            ? SanitizeNote($"Pickup contact: {contact.FullName}"
                           + (contact.Phone.Length > 0 ? $" | {contact.Phone}" : "")
                           + $" | {contact.Email}")
            : null;
        var combinedNote = string.Join("\n", new[] { contactLine, SanitizeNote(input.Note) }.Where(s => s is not null));
        if (combinedNote.Length > 200)
            ModelState.AddModelError("Input.Note", "Your note plus edited contact details exceed 200 characters. Please shorten your note.");

        if (!ModelState.IsValid)
            return Render(c, input, contact, acceptTerms, methodChosen);

        if (!await captcha.VerifyAsync(Request, "checkout", ct))
        {
            ModelState.AddModelError(string.Empty, "Captcha verification failed. Please try again.");
            return Render(c, input, contact, acceptTerms, methodChosen);
        }

        var userId = User.FindFirstValue(ClaimTypes.NameIdentifier)!;

        var reference = input.PaymentMethod == PaymentMethod.GCash
            ? input.PaymentReference?.Trim().ToUpperInvariant()
            : null;
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(reference)) reference = null;

        var order = new Order
        {
            UserId = userId,
            PickupAtUtc = pickupUtc,
            Note = combinedNote.Length == 0 ? null : combinedNote,
            Status = OrderStatus.Placed,
            PaymentMethod = input.PaymentMethod,
            PaymentStatus = PaymentStatus.Unpaid,
            PaymentReference = reference,
            CreatedAtUtc = DateTime.UtcNow
        };

        foreach (var l in c.Lines)
        {
            order.Items.Add(new OrderItem
            {
                MenuItemId = l.MenuItemId,
                ItemName = l.Name,
                SizeName = l.SizeName,
                UnitPrice = l.UnitPrice,
                Quantity = l.Quantity,
                AddOns = l.AddOns.Select(a => new OrderItemAddOn
                {
                    AddOnId = a.Id,
                    Name = a.Name,
                    Price = a.Price
                }).ToList()
            });
        }
        var subtotal = order.Items.Sum(i => i.UnitPrice * i.Quantity);
        order.Total = subtotal;

        await using var tx = await db.Database.BeginTransactionAsync(ct);

        var wanted = c.Lines.GroupBy(l => l.MenuItemId)
                            .ToDictionary(g => g.Key, g => g.Sum(l => l.Quantity));

        var result = await inventory.DeductAsync(wanted, ct);
        if (!result.Ok)
        {
            await tx.RollbackAsync(ct);

            var names = c.Lines.Where(l => result.ShortItemIds.Contains(l.MenuItemId))
                               .Select(l => l.Name).Distinct().ToList();
            log.LogWarning("Checkout rejected for user {UserId}: insufficient stock for {Items}",
                userId, string.Join(", ", names));

            ModelState.AddModelError(string.Empty,
                $"Sorry, not enough stock for: {string.Join(", ", names)}. Please update your cart.");
