            return Render(await cart.GetAsync(), input, contact, acceptTerms, true);
        }

        var promo = await promotions.ApplyPromotionAsync(input.PromoCode, subtotal, ct);
        if (!promo.Ok)
        {
            await tx.RollbackAsync(ct);
            ModelState.AddModelError("Input.PromoCode", promo.Error!);
            return Render(await cart.GetAsync(), input, contact, acceptTerms, true);
        }
        order.PromoCode = promo.Code;
        order.DiscountAmount = promo.Discount;
        order.Total = subtotal - promo.Discount;

        db.Orders.Add(order);
        await db.SaveChangesAsync(ct);

        audit.Add(userId, "OrderPlaced",
            $"OrderId={order.Id}; Status=Placed; Total={order.Total:0.00}; Payment={order.PaymentMethod}");
        if (promo.PromotionId is not null)
            audit.Add(userId, "PromoApplied", $"OrderId={order.Id}; Code={promo.Code}; Discount={promo.Discount:0.00}");
        foreach (var (itemId, qty) in wanted)
            audit.Add(userId, "InventoryDeducted", $"MenuItemId={itemId}; Qty={qty}; OrderId={order.Id}");
        foreach (var itemId in result.SoldOutItemIds)
            audit.Add(null, "ItemSoldOut", $"MenuItemId={itemId}; OrderId={order.Id}");
        await db.SaveChangesAsync(ct);

        await qr.IssueAsync(order, ct);

        await tx.CommitAsync(ct);
        cart.Clear();

        log.LogInformation("Order {OrderId} placed by {UserId}, total {Total}", order.Id, userId, order.Total);
        return RedirectToAction("Confirmation", "Order", new { id = order.Id });
    }

    /// <summary>Read-only promo preview.</summary>
    [HttpPost]
    public async Task<IActionResult> PreviewPromo([FromForm] string? code, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var c = await cart.GetAsync();
        var subtotal = c.Total;

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(code))
            return Json(new { ok = false, message = "Enter a promo code.", code = (string?)null, discount = 0m, total = subtotal });

        var normalized = code.Trim().ToUpperInvariant();
        if (normalized.Length > 30)
            return Json(new { ok = false, message = "Invalid promo code.", code = (string?)null, discount = 0m, total = subtotal });

        var now = DateTime.UtcNow;
        var promo = await db.Promotions.AsNoTracking().FirstOrDefaultAsync(p => p.Code == normalized, ct);

        string? error =
            promo is null || !promo.IsActive ? "Invalid promo code."
            : promo.ExpiryDate < now ? "This promo code has expired."
            : promo.MaxUses is not null && promo.TimesUsed >= promo.MaxUses ? "This promo code has reached its usage limit."
            : null;

        if (error is not null)
            return Json(new { ok = false, message = error, code = (string?)null, discount = 0m, total = subtotal });

        var discount = promo!.DiscountType == DiscountType.Percentage
            ? Math.Round(subtotal * promo.DiscountValue / 100m, 2, MidpointRounding.AwayFromZero)
            : promo.DiscountValue;
        discount = Math.Min(discount, subtotal);

        return Json(new
        {
            ok = true,
            message = $"{promo.Title} applied.",
            code = promo.Code,
            discount,
            total = subtotal - discount
        });
    }

    private IActionResult Render(CartViewModel c, CheckoutInput input, CheckoutContact contact, bool terms, bool methodChosen)
    {
        ViewData["Methods"] = SupportedMethods().Select(m => m.ToString()).ToList();
        ViewData["MethodChosen"] = methodChosen;
        ViewData["AcceptTerms"] = terms;
        ViewData["Contact"] = contact;
        return View("Index", new CheckoutViewModel
        {
            Cart = c,
            Slots = slots.GetAvailableSlots(),
            Input = input
        });
    }

    private static CheckoutContact ContactFrom(ApplicationUser? u) => new()
    {
        FullName = u?.FullName ?? "",
        Email = u?.Email ?? "",
        Phone = u?.PhoneNumber ?? ""
    };

    private void ValidateContact(CheckoutContact c)
    {
        if (c.FullName!.Length == 0)
            ModelState.AddModelError("Contact.FullName", "Please enter your full name.");
        else if (c.FullName.Length > 100)
            ModelState.AddModelError("Contact.FullName", "Name must be 100 characters or fewer.");

        if (c.Email!.Length == 0 || c.Email.Length > 100 || !new EmailAddressAttribute().IsValid(c.Email))
            ModelState.AddModelError("Contact.Email", "Please enter a valid email address.");

        if (c.Phone!.Length > 0 && !PhoneRx.IsMatch(c.Phone))
            ModelState.AddModelError("Contact.Phone", "Please enter a valid phone number.");
    }

    private static bool Same(string? a, string? b) =>
        string.Equals((a ?? "").Trim(), (b ?? "").Trim(), StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

    private static bool TryParseSlot(string value, out DateTime utc) =>
        DateTime.TryParseExact(value, "yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm'Z'", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture,
            DateTimeStyles.AssumeUniversal | DateTimeStyles.AdjustToUniversal, out utc);

    private static string? SanitizeNote(string? note)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(note)) return null;

        var cleaned = new string(note.Where(ch => !char.IsControl(ch) || ch is '\n' or '\r').ToArray())
            .Replace("<", "").Replace(">", "")
            .Trim();

        return cleaned.Length == 0 ? null : cleaned;
    }
}


========================================
FILE: BrewStop\Controllers\FavoritesController.cs
========================================
using System.Security.Claims;
using BrewStop.Data;
using BrewStop.Models;
using BrewStop.Services;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace BrewStop.Controllers;

[Authorize(Policy = "CustomerOnly")]
public class FavoritesController(ApplicationDbContext db, ICartService cart, IImageResolver images) : Controller
{
    private string UserId => User.FindFirstValue(ClaimTypes.NameIdentifier)!;

    private bool IsAjax => Request.Headers.XRequestedWith == "XMLHttpRequest";

    [HttpGet]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Index()
    {
        var uid = UserId;
        var rows = await db.FavoriteItems.AsNoTracking()
            .Where(f => f.UserId == uid)
            .OrderByDescending(f => f.CreatedAtUtc)
            .Select(f => new
            {
                f.MenuItem,
                CategoryName = f.MenuItem.Category.Name,
                CategorySlug = f.MenuItem.Category.Slug
            })
            .ToListAsync();

        var ids = rows.Select(r => r.MenuItem.Id).ToList();
        var stock = await db.InventoryItems.AsNoTracking()
            .Where(i => ids.Contains(i.MenuItemId))
            .ToDictionaryAsync(i => i.MenuItemId, i => i.Quantity);
        var stats = await db.GetRatingStatsAsync(ids);

        var items = rows.Select(r =>
        {
            var s = stats.GetValueOrDefault(r.MenuItem.Id);
            return new MenuItemDto
            {
                Id = r.MenuItem.Id,
                Name = r.MenuItem.Name,
                Description = r.MenuItem.Description,
                Price = r.MenuItem.Price,
                ImageUrl = images.Resolve(r.MenuItem.ImageUrl, r.MenuItem.Name),
                BadgeText = r.MenuItem.BadgeText,
                CategorySlug = r.CategorySlug,
                CategoryName = r.CategoryName,
                Stock = stock.GetValueOrDefault(r.MenuItem.Id, 0),
                AverageRating = s.Avg,
                ReviewCount = s.Count,
                IsFavorite = true
            };
        }).ToList();

        return View(items);
    }

    [HttpPost]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Toggle(int menuItemId, string? returnUrl = null)
    {
        var uid = UserId;
        if (!await db.MenuItems.AnyAsync(m => m.Id == menuItemId))
            return NotFound();

        var existing = await db.FavoriteItems
            .FirstOrDefaultAsync(f => f.UserId == uid && f.MenuItemId == menuItemId);

        if (existing is null)
            db.FavoriteItems.Add(new FavoriteItem { UserId = uid, MenuItemId = menuItemId });
        else
            db.FavoriteItems.Remove(existing);

        try
        {
            await db.SaveChangesAsync();
        }
        catch (DbUpdateException)   // unique-index race (double click): re-read the truth
        {
            db.ChangeTracker.Clear();
        }

        var saved = await db.FavoriteItems.AsNoTracking()
            .AnyAsync(f => f.UserId == uid && f.MenuItemId == menuItemId);

        if (IsAjax) return Json(new { saved, menuItemId });
        return LocalRedirect(Url.IsLocalUrl(returnUrl) ? returnUrl! : "/Favorites");
    }

    [HttpPost]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Remove(int menuItemId)
    {
        var uid = UserId;
        await db.FavoriteItems.Where(f => f.UserId == uid && f.MenuItemId == menuItemId).ExecuteDeleteAsync();
        TempData["FavSuccess"] = "Removed from favorites.";
        return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
    }

    // 1-click add: default (first active) size for drinks, no add-ons, qty 1. Stock rules enforced by CartService.
    [HttpPost]
    public async Task<IActionResult> QuickAdd(int menuItemId)
    {
        var item = await db.MenuItems.AsNoTracking()
            .Include(m => m.Category)
            .FirstOrDefaultAsync(m => m.Id == menuItemId);
        if (item is null) return NotFound();

        int? sizeId = null;
        if (item.Category.Group == "Drinks")
            sizeId = await db.ItemSizes.AsNoTracking()
                .Where(s => s.IsActive)
                .OrderBy(s => s.SortOrder)
                .Select(s => (int?)s.Id)
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync();

        var (ok, error) = await cart.AddAsync(menuItemId, sizeId, Array.Empty<int>(), 1);
        if (!ok)
        {
            TempData["FavError"] = error;
            return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
        }

        return RedirectToAction("Index", "Cart");
    }
}

FILE: BrewStop\Controllers\HomeController.cs
========================================
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Security.Claims;
using BrewStop.Data;
using BrewStop.Models;
using BrewStop.Services;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace BrewStop.Models
{
    public record DashboardItemDto(int Id, string Name, string Description, decimal Price, string ImageUrl, string CategoryName);

    public class DashboardViewModel
    {
        public string FirstName { get; set; } = string.Empty;
        public string Greeting { get; set; } = "Hello";

        public int TotalOrders { get; set; }
        public int Points { get; set; }
        public int FavoriteCount { get; set; }
        public DateTime? LastOrderLocal { get; set; }

        public List<Order> ActiveOrders { get; set; } = new();
        public int ActiveCount { get; set; }

        public List<DashboardItemDto> Popular { get; set; } = new();
        public List<DashboardItemDto> Recommended { get; set; } = new();
        public bool HasHistory { get; set; }

        public string HeroImageUrl { get; set; } = string.Empty;

        public decimal PesosPerPoint { get; set; }
        public RewardTier? NextReward { get; set; }
        public int ProgressPercent { get; set; }
    }
}

namespace BrewStop.Controllers
{
    public class HomeController(
        IAuditService audit,
        ApplicationDbContext db,
        IImageResolver images,
        IPickupSlotService slots) : Controller
    {
        public async Task<IActionResult> Index()
        {
            if (User.IsInRole(AppRoles.Admin))
                return RedirectToAction("Dashboard", "Admin");

            var isCustomer = User.Identity?.IsAuthenticated == true
                && (User.IsInRole(AppRoles.Student) || User.IsInRole(AppRoles.Faculty));

            // Guests, baristas and everyone else keep the existing public homepage.
            if (!isCustomer) return View();

            return View("Dashboard", await BuildDashboardAsync(HttpContext.RequestAborted));
        }

        private async Task<DashboardViewModel> BuildDashboardAsync(CancellationToken ct)
        {
            var uid = User.FindFirstValue(ClaimTypes.NameIdentifier)!;

            var me = await db.Users.AsNoTracking()
                .Where(u => u.Id == uid)
                .Select(u => new { u.FullName, u.LoyaltyPoints })
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(ct);

            var first = (me?.FullName ?? string.Empty)
                .Split(' ', StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries | StringSplitOptions.TrimEntries)
                .FirstOrDefault() ?? "there";

            var hour = slots.ToLocal(DateTime.UtcNow).Hour;
            var greeting = hour < 12 ? "Good morning" : hour < 18 ? "Good afternoon" : "Good evening";

            var points = me?.LoyaltyPoints ?? 0;

            var totalOrders = await db.Orders.AsNoTracking().CountAsync(o => o.UserId == uid, ct);
            var lastOrderUtc = await db.Orders.AsNoTracking()
                .Where(o => o.UserId == uid)
                .OrderByDescending(o => o.CreatedAtUtc)
                .Select(o => (DateTime?)o.CreatedAtUtc)
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(ct);
            var favCount = await db.FavoriteItems.AsNoTracking().CountAsync(f => f.UserId == uid, ct);

            var activeQuery = db.Orders.AsNoTracking()
                .Where(o => o.UserId == uid
                            && (o.Status == OrderStatus.Placed
                                || o.Status == OrderStatus.Preparing
                                || o.Status == OrderStatus.Ready));
            var activeCount = await activeQuery.CountAsync(ct);
            var active = await activeQuery
                .Include(o => o.Items).ThenInclude(i => i.AddOns)
                .Include(o => o.Items).ThenInclude(i => i.MenuItem)
                .OrderByDescending(o => o.CreatedAtUtc)
                .Take(3)
                .ToListAsync(ct);

            // Available items (stock > 0) with all-time popularity.
            var candidates = await db.MenuItems.AsNoTracking()
                .Where(m => db.InventoryItems.Any(i => i.MenuItemId == m.Id && i.Quantity > 0))
                .Select(m => new
                {
                    m.Id,
                    m.Name,
                    m.Description,
                    m.Price,
                    m.ImageUrl,
                    m.CategoryId,
                    CategoryName = m.Category.Name,
                    Sold = db.OrderItems
                        .Where(o => o.MenuItemId == m.Id && o.Order.Status != OrderStatus.Cancelled)
                        .Sum(o => (int?)o.Quantity) ?? 0
                })
                .ToListAsync(ct);

            var ranked = candidates.OrderByDescending(c => c.Sold).ThenBy(c => c.Name).ToList();
            var popular = ranked.Take(3).ToList();
            var popularIds = popular.Select(p => p.Id).ToHashSet();
            var rest = ranked.Where(c => !popularIds.Contains(c.Id)).ToList();

            // Past orders drive the recommendations: untried items from the categories ordered most.
            var mine = await db.OrderItems.AsNoTracking()
                .Where(o => o.Order.UserId == uid && o.Order.Status != OrderStatus.Cancelled)
                .Select(o => new { o.MenuItemId, o.MenuItem.CategoryId, o.Quantity })
                .ToListAsync(ct);
            var hasHistory = mine.Count > 0;

            var recommended = rest;
            if (hasHistory)
            {
                var orderedIds = mine.Select(x => x.MenuItemId).ToHashSet();
                var catRank = mine.GroupBy(x => x.CategoryId)
                    .OrderByDescending(g => g.Sum(x => x.Quantity))
                    .Select((g, i) => new { g.Key, Rank = i })
                    .ToDictionary(x => x.Key, x => x.Rank);

                recommended = rest
                    .OrderBy(c => orderedIds.Contains(c.Id))
                    .ThenBy(c => catRank.TryGetValue(c.CategoryId, out var r) ? r : int.MaxValue)
                    .ThenByDescending(c => c.Sold)
                    .ToList();
            }

            DashboardItemDto Map(string name, int id, string desc, decimal price, string img, string cat) =>
                new(id, name, desc, price, images.Resolve(img, name), cat);

            // Next reward tier is real: LoyaltyTiers + ApplicationUser.LoyaltyPoints.
            var next = LoyaltyTiers.All.OrderBy(t => t.Cost).FirstOrDefault(t => t.Cost > points);
            var progress = next is null ? 100 : Math.Clamp((int)Math.Floor(points * 100m / next.Cost), 0, 100);

            return new DashboardViewModel
            {
                FirstName = first,
                Greeting = greeting,
                TotalOrders = totalOrders,
                Points = points,
                FavoriteCount = favCount,
                LastOrderLocal = lastOrderUtc is DateTime d ? slots.ToLocal(d) : null,
                ActiveOrders = active,
                ActiveCount = activeCount,
                Popular = popular.Select(c => Map(c.Name, c.Id, c.Description, c.Price, c.ImageUrl, c.CategoryName)).ToList(),
                Recommended = recommended.Take(4).Select(c => Map(c.Name, c.Id, c.Description, c.Price, c.ImageUrl, c.CategoryName)).ToList(),
                HasHistory = hasHistory,
                HeroImageUrl = images.Resolve(null, "Caramel Latte"),
                PesosPerPoint = LoyaltyTiers.PesosPerPoint,
                NextReward = next,
                ProgressPercent = progress
            };
        }

        public IActionResult Privacy() => View();

        [HttpGet, AllowAnonymous]
        public IActionResult Support() => View(new SupportViewModel());

        [HttpPost, AllowAnonymous, ValidateAntiForgeryToken]
        public async Task<IActionResult> Support(SupportViewModel m)
        {
            if (!ModelState.IsValid) return View(m);

            // Stored in AuditLog (no new table/migration). Swap for IEmailService to notify the shop by email.
            await audit.LogAsync(User.FindFirstValue(ClaimTypes.NameIdentifier), "SupportMessage",
                $"Topic={m.Topic}; From={m.Name} <{m.Email}>; Msg={m.Message.Replace('\r', ' ').Replace('\n', ' ')}",
                HttpContext.RequestAborted);

            TempData["Success"] = "Thanks! Your message was sent. We'll reply by email soon.";
            return RedirectToAction(nameof(Support), null, "contact");
        }

        [ResponseCache(Duration = 0, Location = ResponseCacheLocation.None, NoStore = true)]
        public IActionResult Error()
            => View(new ErrorViewModel { RequestId = Activity.Current?.Id ?? HttpContext.TraceIdentifier });
    }
}


========================================
FILE: BrewStop\Controllers\MenuController.cs
========================================
using System.Security.Claims;
using BrewStop.Data;
using BrewStop.Models;
using BrewStop.Services;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace BrewStop.Controllers;

public class MenuController(ApplicationDbContext db, IImageResolver images) : Controller
{
    private static readonly string[] PriceProps =
        { "PriceModifier", "PriceDelta", "PriceAdjustment", "ExtraPrice", "AdditionalPrice", "ExtraCost", "Price" };

    private static readonly string[] SortKeys = { "popularity", "price_asc", "price_desc" };

    public async Task<IActionResult> Index(string? category, string? searchTerm, string? group, string? sort)
    {
        if (User.IsInRole(AppRoles.Admin))
            return RedirectToAction("Dashboard", "Admin");

        var term = searchTerm?.Trim();
        if (term is { Length: > 100 }) term = term[..100];

        var sortKey = SortKeys.Contains(sort?.Trim().ToLowerInvariant()) ? sort!.Trim().ToLowerInvariant() : "popularity";

        var categories = await db.Categories
            .AsNoTracking()
            .Include(c => c.MenuItems)
            .OrderBy(c => c.Id)
            .ToListAsync();

        var stock = await db.InventoryItems
            .AsNoTracking()
            .ToDictionaryAsync(i => i.MenuItemId, i => i.Quantity);

        var uid = User.FindFirstValue(ClaimTypes.NameIdentifier);
        var isCustomer = User.IsInRole(AppRoles.Student) || User.IsInRole(AppRoles.Faculty);
        var stats = await db.GetRatingStatsAsync();
        var favs = isCustomer && uid is not null
            ? (await db.FavoriteItems.AsNoTracking().Where(f => f.UserId == uid)
                  .Select(f => f.MenuItemId).ToListAsync()).ToHashSet()
            : new HashSet<int>();

        var all = categories.Select(c => new CategoryGroupDto
        {
            Slug = c.Slug,
            Name = c.Name,
            Group = c.Group,
            ImageUrl = images.Resolve(c.ImageUrl, c.Name),
            Items = c.MenuItems.Select(m =>
            {
                var s = stats.GetValueOrDefault(m.Id);
                return new MenuItemDto
                {
                    Id = m.Id,
                    Name = m.Name,
                    Description = m.Description,
                    Price = m.Price,
                    ImageUrl = images.Resolve(m.ImageUrl, m.Name),
                    BadgeText = m.BadgeText,
                    CategorySlug = c.Slug,
                    CategoryName = c.Name,
                    Group = c.Group,
                    Stock = stock.GetValueOrDefault(m.Id, 0),
                    AverageRating = s.Avg,
                    ReviewCount = s.Count,
                    Popularity = s.Count + s.Avg / 10d,
                    IsFavorite = favs.Contains(m.Id)
                };
            }).ToList()
        }).ToList();

        var flat = all.SelectMany(c => c.Items).ToList();

        var activeGroup = "Drinks";
        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(group))
        {
            activeGroup = group.Trim().Equals("Drinks", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ? "Drinks" : "Pastries";
        }
        else if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(category))
        {
            var match = all.FirstOrDefault(c => c.Slug.Equals(category.Trim(), StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
            if (match is not null && !IsDrink(match.Group)) activeGroup = "Pastries";
        }

        IEnumerable<MenuItemDto> Filter(IEnumerable<MenuItemDto> src) =>
            string.IsNullOrEmpty(term)
                ? src
                : src.Where(i =>
                    i.Name.Contains(term, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                    i.Description.Contains(term, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                    i.CategoryName.Contains(term, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));

        List<MenuItemDto> Sort(IEnumerable<MenuItemDto> src) => (sortKey switch
        {
            "price_asc" => src.OrderBy(i => i.Price).ThenBy(i => i.Name),
            "price_desc" => src.OrderByDescending(i => i.Price).ThenBy(i => i.Name),
            _ => src.OrderByDescending(i => i.IsAvailable)
                    .ThenByDescending(i => i.Popularity)
                    .ThenBy(i => i.Name)
        }).ToList();

        var drinksAll = flat.Where(i => IsDrink(i.Group)).ToList();
        var pastriesAll = flat.Where(i => !IsDrink(i.Group)).ToList();

        var pairPool = drinksAll.OrderByDescending(d => d.Popularity).Select(d => d.Name).Take(6).ToList();
        for (var i = 0; i < pastriesAll.Count; i++)
            pastriesAll[i].PairingNote = pairPool.Count > 0 ? pairPool[i % pairPool.Count] : string.Empty;

        var drinks = Sort(Filter(drinksAll));
        var pastries = Sort(Filter(pastriesAll));

        var hero = drinksAll.Where(d => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(d.ImageUrl))
                            .OrderByDescending(d => d.Popularity)
                            .FirstOrDefault()
                   ?? flat.FirstOrDefault(d => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(d.ImageUrl));

        var sizes = await db.ItemSizes.AsNoTracking()
            .Where(s => s.IsActive).OrderBy(s => s.SortOrder).ToListAsync();
        var addOns = await db.AddOns.AsNoTracking()
            .Where(a => a.IsActive).OrderBy(a => a.Name).ToListAsync();

        await LoadStaffReferenceAsync();

        var visibleCats = all
            .Select(c => new CategoryGroupDto
            {
                Slug = c.Slug,
                Name = c.Name,
                Group = c.Group,
                ImageUrl = c.ImageUrl,
                Items = Sort(Filter(c.Items))
            })
            .Where(c => c.Items.Count > 0)
            .ToList();

        return View(new MenuViewModel
        {
            Categories = visibleCats,
            Pills = all,
            ActiveCategory = activeGroup.ToLowerInvariant(),
            ActiveGroup = activeGroup,
            Sort = sortKey,
            SearchTerm = term,
            Drinks = drinks,
            Pastries = pastries,
            HeroImageUrl = hero?.ImageUrl ?? string.Empty,
            HeroImageAlt = hero?.Name ?? "BrewStop coffee",
            Sizes = sizes.Select(s => new SizeOptionDto { Id = s.Id, Name = s.Name, Price = PriceOf(s) }).ToList(),
            AddOns = addOns.Select(a => new AddOnOptionDto { Id = a.Id, Name = a.Name, Price = PriceOf(a), Group = a.Group }).ToList()
        });
    }

    [HttpGet]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Details(int id)
    {
        var m = await db.MenuItems.AsNoTracking()
            .Include(x => x.Category)
            .FirstOrDefaultAsync(x => x.Id == id);
        if (m is null) return NotFound();

        var stock = await db.InventoryItems.AsNoTracking()
            .Where(i => i.MenuItemId == id)
            .Select(i => i.Quantity)
            .FirstOrDefaultAsync();

        var ratings = await db.ProductReviews.AsNoTracking()
            .Where(r => r.MenuItemId == id)
            .Select(r => r.Rating)
            .ToListAsync();

        var recent = await db.ProductReviews.AsNoTracking()
            .Where(r => r.MenuItemId == id)
            .OrderByDescending(r => r.CreatedAtUtc)
            .Take(10)
            .Select(r => new { r.User.FullName, r.Rating, r.Comment, r.CreatedAtUtc })
            .ToListAsync();

        var uid = User.FindFirstValue(ClaimTypes.NameIdentifier);
        var isCustomer = User.IsInRole(AppRoles.Student) || User.IsInRole(AppRoles.Faculty);
        var isFav = isCustomer && uid is not null &&
                    await db.FavoriteItems.AsNoTracking().AnyAsync(f => f.UserId == uid && f.MenuItemId == id);

        var dist = new int[5];
        foreach (var r in ratings) dist[Math.Clamp(r, 1, 5) - 1]++;

        await LoadStaffReferenceAsync();
        ViewBag.RefGroup = m.Category.Group;

        return View(new MenuItemDetailsViewModel
        {
            CanFavorite = isCustomer,
            Distribution = dist,
            Item = new MenuItemDto
            {
                Id = m.Id,
                Name = m.Name,
                Description = m.Description,
                Price = m.Price,
                ImageUrl = images.Resolve(m.ImageUrl, m.Name),
                BadgeText = m.BadgeText,
                CategorySlug = m.Category.Slug,
                CategoryName = m.Category.Name,
                Group = m.Category.Group,
                Stock = stock,
                AverageRating = ratings.Count > 0 ? ratings.Average() : 0,
                ReviewCount = ratings.Count,
                IsFavorite = isFav
            },
            Reviews = recent.Select(r => new ReviewDto
            {
                Author = DisplayName(r.FullName),
                Rating = r.Rating,
                Comment = r.Comment,
                CreatedAtUtc = r.CreatedAtUtc
            }).ToList()
        });
    }

    private async Task LoadStaffReferenceAsync()
    {
        if (!User.IsInRole(AppRoles.Barista)) return;
        ViewBag.RefSizes = await db.ItemSizes.AsNoTracking()
            .Where(s => s.IsActive).OrderBy(s => s.SortOrder).ToListAsync();
        ViewBag.RefAddOns = await db.AddOns.AsNoTracking()
            .Where(a => a.IsActive).OrderBy(a => a.Name).ToListAsync();
    }

    private static bool IsDrink(string? group) =>
        string.Equals(group?.Trim(), "Drinks", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

    private static decimal PriceOf(object entity)
    {
        var type = entity.GetType();
        foreach (var name in PriceProps)
        {
            var p = type.GetProperty(name);
            if (p is null) continue;
            switch (p.GetValue(entity))
            {
                case decimal d: return d;
                case double db: return (decimal)db;
                case float f: return (decimal)f;
                case int i: return i;
            }
        }
        return 0m;
    }

    private static string DisplayName(string? full)
    {
        var p = (full ?? string.Empty).Split(' ', StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries | StringSplitOptions.TrimEntries);
        return p.Length switch { 0 => "Customer", 1 => p[0], _ => $"{p[0]} {p[^1][0]}." };
    }
}


========================================
FILE: BrewStop\Controllers\OrderController.cs
========================================
using System.Security.Claims;
using BrewStop.Data;
using BrewStop.Models;
using BrewStop.Services;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace BrewStop.Controllers;

[Authorize(Policy = "CustomerOnly")]
public class OrderController(ApplicationDbContext db, IQrTokenService qr, IAuditService audit, ICartService cart) : Controller
{
    private string? UserId => User.FindFirstValue(ClaimTypes.NameIdentifier);

    [HttpGet]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Index()
    {
        var orders = await db.Orders.AsNoTracking()
            .Where(o => o.UserId == UserId)
            .Include(o => o.Items).ThenInclude(i => i.AddOns)
            .Include(o => o.Items).ThenInclude(i => i.MenuItem)
            .OrderByDescending(o => o.CreatedAtUtc)
            .Take(50)
            .ToListAsync();

        return View(orders);
    }

    [HttpGet]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Details(int id)
    {
        var order = await db.Orders.AsNoTracking()
            .Include(o => o.Items).ThenInclude(i => i.AddOns)
            .FirstOrDefaultAsync(o => o.Id == id && o.UserId == UserId);

        if (order is null) return NotFound();

        ViewBag.QrToken = order.Status == OrderStatus.Ready
            ? await qr.GetTokenAsync(order.Id, HttpContext.RequestAborted) ?? await IssueAndGetAsync(order)
            : null;

        if (order.Status == OrderStatus.Claimed)
            ViewBag.Reviews = await db.ProductReviews.AsNoTracking()
                .Where(r => r.UserId == UserId && r.OrderId == order.Id)
                .ToDictionaryAsync(r => r.MenuItemId);

        return View(order);
    }

    private async Task<string?> IssueAndGetAsync(Order order)
    {
        await qr.IssueAsync(order, HttpContext.RequestAborted);
        return await qr.GetTokenAsync(order.Id, HttpContext.RequestAborted);
    }

    [HttpGet]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Confirmation(int id)
    {
        var order = await db.Orders.AsNoTracking()
            .Include(o => o.Items).ThenInclude(i => i.AddOns)
            .Include(o => o.Items).ThenInclude(i => i.MenuItem)
            .FirstOrDefaultAsync(o => o.Id == id && o.UserId == UserId);

        if (order is null) return NotFound();

        ViewBag.QrToken = await QrFor(order);
        return View(order);
    }

    private Task<string?> QrFor(Order o) =>
        o.Status is OrderStatus.Placed or OrderStatus.Preparing or OrderStatus.Ready
            ? qr.GetTokenAsync(o.Id)
            : Task.FromResult<string?>(null);

    [HttpPost, ValidateAntiForgeryToken]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Cancel(int id)
    {
        var order = await db.Orders.Include(o => o.Items)
            .FirstOrDefaultAsync(o => o.Id == id && o.UserId == UserId);

        if (order is null) return NotFound();

        if (order.Status != OrderStatus.Placed)
        {
            TempData["Error"] = "This order can no longer be cancelled.";
            return RedirectToAction(nameof(Details), new { id });
        }

        await using var tx = await db.Database.BeginTransactionAsync();

        foreach (var g in order.Items.GroupBy(i => i.MenuItemId))
        {
            var qty = g.Sum(i => i.Quantity);
            await db.InventoryItems.Where(i => i.MenuItemId == g.Key)
                .ExecuteUpdateAsync(s => s.SetProperty(i => i.Quantity, i => i.Quantity + qty));
        }

        order.Status = OrderStatus.Cancelled;
        db.AuditLogs.Add(new AuditLog
        {
            UserId = UserId,
            Action = "OrderCancelled",
            Details = $"OrderId={order.Id}",
            CreatedAtUtc = DateTime.UtcNow
        });

        await qr.RevokeAsync(order.Id);
        audit.Add(UserId, "OrderStatusChanged", $"OrderId={order.Id}; Placed->Cancelled");

        await db.SaveChangesAsync();
        await tx.CommitAsync();

        TempData["Success"] = "Your order was cancelled.";
        return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
    }

    [HttpGet]
    public async Task<IActionResult> RecentUpdates()
    {
        var rows = await db.Orders.AsNoTracking()
            .Where(o => o.UserId == UserId)
            .OrderByDescending(o => o.CreatedAtUtc)
            .Take(5)
            .Select(o => new { id = o.Id, status = o.Status.ToString(), total = o.Total, createdAtUtc = o.CreatedAtUtc })
            .ToListAsync(HttpContext.RequestAborted);
        return Json(rows);
    }

    [HttpGet]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Receipt(int id)
    {
        var order = await db.Orders.AsNoTracking()
            .Include(o => o.User)
            .Include(o => o.Items).ThenInclude(i => i.AddOns)
            .FirstOrDefaultAsync(o => o.Id == id && o.UserId == UserId);

        if (order is null) return NotFound();
        return View(order);
    }

    [HttpPost, ValidateAntiForgeryToken]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Reorder(int id)
    {
        var order = await db.Orders.AsNoTracking()
            .Include(o => o.Items).ThenInclude(i => i.AddOns)
            .FirstOrDefaultAsync(o => o.Id == id && o.UserId == UserId);

        if (order is null) return NotFound();

        var sizes = await db.ItemSizes.AsNoTracking().Where(s => s.IsActive).ToListAsync();
        var added = 0;
        var skipped = new List<string>();

        foreach (var i in order.Items)
        {
            int? sizeId = null;
            if (i.SizeName is not null)
            {
                sizeId = sizes.FirstOrDefault(s => s.Name == i.SizeName)?.Id;
                if (sizeId is null) { skipped.Add(i.ItemName); continue; }
            }

            var (ok, _) = await cart.AddAsync(i.MenuItemId, sizeId, i.AddOns.Select(a => a.AddOnId), i.Quantity);
            if (ok) added++; else skipped.Add(i.ItemName);
        }

        await audit.LogAsync(UserId, "OrderReordered", $"OrderId={id}; Added={added}; Skipped={skipped.Count}");

        if (skipped.Count > 0)
            TempData["Warning"] = "Skipped (out of stock or unavailable): " + string.Join(", ", skipped.Distinct()) + ".";
        if (added > 0)
            TempData["Success"] = $"{added} item(s) from order #{id} added to your cart.";

        return RedirectToAction("Index", "Cart");
    }
}


========================================
FILE: BrewStop\Controllers\ReviewsController.cs
========================================
using System.Security.Claims;
using BrewStop.Data;
using BrewStop.Models;
using BrewStop.Services;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace BrewStop.Controllers;

[Authorize(Policy = "CustomerOnly")]
public class ReviewsController(ApplicationDbContext db, IAuditService audit, ILogger<ReviewsController> log) : Controller
{
    private string UserId => User.FindFirstValue(ClaimTypes.NameIdentifier)!;

    [HttpPost, ValidateAntiForgeryToken]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Create(ReviewInput input)
    {
        var ct = HttpContext.RequestAborted;

        IActionResult Back(string key, string message)
        {
            TempData[key] = message;
            return RedirectToAction("Details", "Order", new { id = input.OrderId });
        }

        if (!ModelState.IsValid)
        {
            var first = ModelState.Values.SelectMany(v => v.Errors)
                .Select(e => e.ErrorMessage).FirstOrDefault(m => !string.IsNullOrEmpty(m));
            return Back("ReviewError", first ?? "Please choose a rating from 1 to 5.");
        }

        // Ownership (SRS-FR-18): another user's order is indistinguishable from a missing one.
        var order = await db.Orders.AsNoTracking()
            .Include(o => o.Items)
            .FirstOrDefaultAsync(o => o.Id == input.OrderId && o.UserId == UserId, ct);
        if (order is null) return NotFound();

        if (order.Status != OrderStatus.Claimed)
            return Back("ReviewError", "You can review items once your order has been picked up.");

        if (!order.Items.Any(i => i.MenuItemId == input.MenuItemId))
            return Back("ReviewError", "That item is not part of this order.");

        if (await db.ProductReviews.AnyAsync(r =>
                r.UserId == UserId && r.OrderId == order.Id && r.MenuItemId == input.MenuItemId, ct))
            return Back("ReviewError", "You already reviewed this item for this order.");

        db.ProductReviews.Add(new ProductReview
        {
            UserId = UserId,
            OrderId = order.Id,
            MenuItemId = input.MenuItemId,
            Rating = input.Rating,
            Comment = Sanitize(input.Comment),
            CreatedAtUtc = DateTime.UtcNow
        });
        audit.Add(UserId, "ReviewCreated",
            $"OrderId={order.Id}; MenuItemId={input.MenuItemId}; Rating={input.Rating}");

        try
        {
            await db.SaveChangesAsync(ct);
        }
        catch (DbUpdateException ex)  
        {
            log.LogWarning(ex, "Duplicate review rejected for order {OrderId}", order.Id);
            return Back("ReviewError", "You already reviewed this item for this order.");
        }

        return Back("ReviewSuccess", "Thanks for your review!");
    }

    private static string? Sanitize(string? comment)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(comment)) return null;

        var cleaned = new string(comment.Where(ch => !char.IsControl(ch) || ch is '\n' or '\r').ToArray())
            .Replace("<", "").Replace(">", "")
            .Trim();

        if (cleaned.Length == 0) return null;
        return cleaned.Length > 300 ? cleaned[..300] : cleaned;
    }
}


========================================
FILE: BrewStop\Controllers\Admin\AdminController.cs
========================================
using System.Globalization;
using System.Security.Claims;
using System.Text;
using BrewStop.Data;
using BrewStop.Models;
using BrewStop.Services;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Identity;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace BrewStop.Controllers.Admin;

[Authorize(Roles = AppRoles.Admin)]
[Route("Admin")]
public class AdminController(
    ApplicationDbContext db,
    IAuditService audit,
    IQrTokenService qr,
    UserManager<ApplicationUser> users,
    IPickupSlotService slots,
    IImageResolver images) : Controller
{
    private string? ActorId => User.FindFirstValue(ClaimTypes.NameIdentifier);

    [HttpGet(""), HttpGet("Index")]
    public IActionResult Index() => View("~/Views/Admin/Index.cshtml");

    [HttpGet("Dashboard")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Dashboard(DateOnly? startDate, DateOnly? endDate, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        const int LowStockThreshold = 5;

        // ---- Shop-local date handling (fixed-offset zones; Asia/Manila has no DST) ----
        var nowUtc = DateTime.UtcNow;
        var nowLocal = slots.ToLocal(nowUtc);
        var offset = TimeSpan.FromMinutes(Math.Round((nowLocal - nowUtc).TotalMinutes));
        var todayLocal = DateOnly.FromDateTime(nowLocal);

        var end = endDate ?? todayLocal;
        var start = startDate ?? end.AddDays(-6);
        if (start > end) (start, end) = (end, start);
        if (end.DayNumber - start.DayNumber > 365) start = end.AddDays(-365);

        DateTime ToUtc(DateOnly d) =>
            DateTime.SpecifyKind(d.ToDateTime(TimeOnly.MinValue) - offset, DateTimeKind.Utc);

        var rangeStart = ToUtc(start);
        var rangeEnd = ToUtc(end.AddDays(1));            // exclusive
        var todayStart = ToUtc(todayLocal);
        var tomorrowStart = ToUtc(todayLocal.AddDays(1));
        var yesterdayStart = ToUtc(todayLocal.AddDays(-1));

        // ---- Orders in range (one projection, bucketed in memory) ----
        var range = await db.Orders.AsNoTracking()
            .Where(o => o.CreatedAtUtc >= rangeStart && o.CreatedAtUtc < rangeEnd)
            .Select(o => new { o.CreatedAtUtc, o.Total, o.Status, o.PaymentMethod })
            .ToListAsync(ct);

        var billable = range.Where(o => o.Status != OrderStatus.Cancelled).ToList();   // revenue excludes Cancelled

        var days = Enumerable.Range(0, end.DayNumber - start.DayNumber + 1).Select(start.AddDays).ToList();
        var byDay = billable
            .GroupBy(o => DateOnly.FromDateTime(o.CreatedAtUtc + offset))
            .ToDictionary(g => g.Key, g => (Revenue: g.Sum(x => x.Total), Count: g.Count()));

        var statusLabels = Enum.GetNames<OrderStatus>();
        var statusCounts = Enum.GetValues<OrderStatus>()
            .Select(s => range.Count(o => o.Status == s)).ToArray();

        // ---- Today vs yesterday (independent of the filter) ----
        var twoDays = await db.Orders.AsNoTracking()
            .Where(o => o.CreatedAtUtc >= yesterdayStart && o.CreatedAtUtc < tomorrowStart
                        && o.Status != OrderStatus.Cancelled)
            .Select(o => new { o.CreatedAtUtc, o.Total })
            .ToListAsync(ct);

        var revToday = twoDays.Where(o => o.CreatedAtUtc >= todayStart).Sum(o => o.Total);
        var ordToday = twoDays.Count(o => o.CreatedAtUtc >= todayStart);
        var revYesterday = twoDays.Where(o => o.CreatedAtUtc < todayStart).Sum(o => o.Total);

        var pending = await db.Orders.AsNoTracking().CountAsync(o =>
            o.Status == OrderStatus.Placed || o.Status == OrderStatus.Preparing || o.Status == OrderStatus.Ready, ct);

        // ---- Best-selling drinks (range, non-cancelled) ----
        var top = await db.OrderItems.AsNoTracking()
            .Where(i => i.Order.CreatedAtUtc >= rangeStart && i.Order.CreatedAtUtc < rangeEnd
                        && i.Order.Status != OrderStatus.Cancelled
                        && i.MenuItem.Category.Group == "Drinks")
            .GroupBy(i => new { i.MenuItemId, i.MenuItem.Name, i.MenuItem.ImageUrl })
            .Select(g => new
            {
                g.Key.Name,
                g.Key.ImageUrl,
                Units = g.Sum(i => i.Quantity),
                Revenue = g.Sum(i => i.UnitPrice * i.Quantity)
            })
            .OrderByDescending(x => x.Units).ThenByDescending(x => x.Revenue)
            .Take(5)
            .ToListAsync(ct);

        // ---- Shifts ----
        var onShift = await db.BaristaShifts.AsNoTracking()
            .CountAsync(s => s.ClockInAt != null && s.ClockOutAt == null, ct);
        var scheduledToday = await db.BaristaShifts.AsNoTracking()
            .CountAsync(s => s.ScheduledStart >= todayStart && s.ScheduledStart < tomorrowStart, ct);
        var completedToday = await db.BaristaShifts.AsNoTracking()
            .CountAsync(s => s.ClockOutAt != null && s.ClockOutAt >= todayStart && s.ClockOutAt < tomorrowStart, ct);

        // ---- Attention alerts: stock + rejected QR scans (last 7 days) ----
        var stockRows = await db.InventoryItems.AsNoTracking()
            .Where(i => i.Quantity <= LowStockThreshold)
            .OrderBy(i => i.Quantity).ThenBy(i => i.MenuItem.Name)
            .Select(i => new { i.MenuItem.Name, i.Quantity })
            .Take(8)
            .ToListAsync(ct);

        var scanRows = await db.AuditLogs.AsNoTracking()
            .Where(a => a.Action == "QrScanRejected" && a.CreatedAtUtc >= nowUtc.AddDays(-7))
            .OrderByDescending(a => a.CreatedAtUtc)
            .Select(a => new { a.Details, a.CreatedAtUtc })
            .Take(5)
            .ToListAsync(ct);

        static string? Field(string? details, string key) =>
            details?.Split(';', StringSplitOptions.TrimEntries)
                .FirstOrDefault(p => p.StartsWith(key + "=", StringComparison.Ordinal))?[(key.Length + 1)..];

        var alerts = new List<AttentionAlertDto>();
        alerts.AddRange(stockRows.Select(r => new AttentionAlertDto
        {
            Kind = r.Quantity == 0 ? "OutOfStock" : "LowStock",
            Title = r.Name,
            Detail = r.Quantity == 0 ? "Out of stock. Customers cannot order this item." : $"Only {r.Quantity} left in stock.",
            ActionUrl = "/Admin/Inventory",
            ActionLabel = "Restock"
        }));
        alerts.AddRange(scanRows.Select(r =>
        {
            var orderId = Field(r.Details, "OrderId");
            return new AttentionAlertDto
            {
                Kind = "RejectedScan",
                Title = "Rejected QR scan",
                Detail = $"{(string.IsNullOrEmpty(orderId) ? "Unknown order" : $"Order #{orderId}")} · {Field(r.Details, "Reason") ?? "Rejected"}",
                OccurredAt = slots.ToLocal(r.CreatedAtUtc)
            };
        }));

        // ---- Recent orders in range ----
        var recent = await db.Orders.AsNoTracking()
            .Where(o => o.CreatedAtUtc >= rangeStart && o.CreatedAtUtc < rangeEnd)
            .OrderByDescending(o => o.CreatedAtUtc)
            .Select(o => new
            {
                o.Id,
                o.User.FullName,
                o.User.CampusId,
                o.PickupAtUtc,
                o.Total,
                o.PaymentMethod,
                o.PaymentStatus,
                o.Status,
                o.CreatedAtUtc
            })
            .Take(10)
            .ToListAsync(ct);

        var vm = new AdminDashboardViewModel
        {
            StartDate = start,
            EndDate = end,
            NowLocal = nowLocal,

            RevenueToday = revToday,
            RevenueYesterday = revYesterday,
            RevenueChangePercent = revYesterday > 0
                ? Math.Round((revToday - revYesterday) / revYesterday * 100m, 1) : null,
            AverageOrderValue = billable.Count > 0 ? Math.Round(billable.Sum(o => o.Total) / billable.Count, 2) : 0m,
            OrdersToday = ordToday,
            PendingPickupCount = pending,
            RangeRevenue = billable.Sum(o => o.Total),
            RangeOrders = billable.Count,

            DailyLabels = days.Select(d => d.ToString("MMM d", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)).ToArray(),
            DailyRevenueData = days.Select(d => byDay.TryGetValue(d, out var v) ? v.Revenue : 0m).ToArray(),
            DailyOrderCountData = days.Select(d => byDay.TryGetValue(d, out var v) ? v.Count : 0).ToArray(),

            PaymentMethodLabels = new[] { nameof(PaymentMethod.Cash), nameof(PaymentMethod.GCash) },
            PaymentMethodCounts = new[]
            {
                billable.Count(o => o.PaymentMethod == PaymentMethod.Cash),
                billable.Count(o => o.PaymentMethod == PaymentMethod.GCash)
            },
            OrderStatusLabels = statusLabels,
            OrderStatusCounts = statusCounts,

            TopSellingDrinks = top.Select(t => new TopSellingDrinkDto
            {
                MenuItemName = t.Name,
                ImageUrl = images.Resolve(t.ImageUrl, t.Name),
                UnitsSold = t.Units,
                TotalRevenue = t.Revenue
            }).ToList(),

            BaristasOnShiftCount = onShift,
            ScheduledShiftsTodayCount = scheduledToday,
            CompletedShiftsTodayCount = completedToday,

            AttentionAlerts = alerts,
            RecentOrders = recent.Select(o => new RecentOrderRowDto
            {
                OrderId = o.Id,
                CustomerName = o.FullName,
                CampusId = o.CampusId,
                PickupSlot = slots.ToLocal(o.PickupAtUtc),
                TotalAmount = o.Total,
                PaymentMethod = o.PaymentMethod,
                PaymentStatus = o.PaymentStatus,
                Status = o.Status,
                CreatedAt = slots.ToLocal(o.CreatedAtUtc)
            }).ToList()
        };

        return View(vm);
    }

    [HttpGet("Orders")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Orders(string? status, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var q = db.Orders.AsNoTracking().Include(o => o.User).AsQueryable();
        var filter = "All";
        if (Enum.TryParse<OrderStatus>(status, true, out var s) && Enum.IsDefined(s))
        {
            q = q.Where(o => o.Status == s);
            filter = s.ToString();
        }

        return View(new AdminOrdersViewModel
        {
            Filter = filter,
            Orders = await q.OrderByDescending(o => o.CreatedAtUtc).ToListAsync(ct),
            PendingCount = await db.Orders.CountAsync(o => o.Status == OrderStatus.Placed || o.Status == OrderStatus.Preparing, ct)
        });
    }

    [HttpGet("OrderDetails/{id:int}")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> OrderDetails(int id, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var order = await db.Orders.AsNoTracking()
            .Include(o => o.User)
            .Include(o => o.Items).ThenInclude(i => i.AddOns)
            .FirstOrDefaultAsync(o => o.Id == id, ct);
        return order is null ? NotFound() : View(order);
    }

    [HttpPost("UpdateOrderStatus"), ValidateAntiForgeryToken]
    public async Task<IActionResult> UpdateOrderStatus(int id, OrderStatus status, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var order = await db.Orders.AsNoTracking().Include(o => o.Items).FirstOrDefaultAsync(o => o.Id == id, ct);
        if (order is null) return NotFound();

        var from = order.Status;
        if (!AdminOrderRules.NextOptions(from).Contains(status))
        {
            TempData["Error"] = $"Order #{id} cannot move from {from} to {status}.";
            return RedirectToAction(nameof(OrderDetails), new { id });
        }

        await using var tx = await db.Database.BeginTransactionAsync(ct);

        var rows = await db.Orders.Where(o => o.Id == id && o.Status == from)
            .ExecuteUpdateAsync(s => s.SetProperty(o => o.Status, status), ct);
        if (rows == 0)
        {
            await tx.RollbackAsync(ct);
            TempData["Error"] = $"Order #{id} changed in the meantime. Please review it again.";
            return RedirectToAction(nameof(OrderDetails), new { id });
        }

        var cancelled = status == OrderStatus.Cancelled && from != OrderStatus.Cancelled;
        if (cancelled)
        {
            foreach (var g in order.Items.GroupBy(i => i.MenuItemId).OrderBy(g => g.Key))
            {
                var itemId = g.Key;
                var qty = g.Sum(i => i.Quantity);
                await db.InventoryItems.Where(i => i.MenuItemId == itemId)
                    .ExecuteUpdateAsync(s => s.SetProperty(i => i.Quantity, i => i.Quantity + qty), ct);
            }
            await qr.RevokeAsync(id, ct);
        }

        audit.Add(ActorId,
            cancelled ? "OrderCancelled" : "OrderStatusChanged",
            $"OrderId={id}; {from}->{status}");
        await db.SaveChangesAsync(ct);
        await tx.CommitAsync(ct);

        TempData["Success"] = $"Order #{id} is now {status}.";
        return RedirectToAction(nameof(OrderDetails), new { id });
    }

    [HttpGet("Menu")]
    public IActionResult Menu() => RedirectToAction("Index", "MenuItems");

    // ---------------- Customer Management ----------------

    [HttpGet("Customers")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Customers(string? q, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var roleMap = (await (from ur in db.UserRoles
                              join r in db.Roles on ur.RoleId equals r.Id
                              select new { ur.UserId, r.Name }).ToListAsync(ct))
            .GroupBy(x => x.UserId)
            .ToDictionary(g => g.Key, g => g.Select(x => x.Name!).ToList());

        var stats = (await db.Orders.AsNoTracking()
                .Where(o => o.Status != OrderStatus.Cancelled)
                .GroupBy(o => o.UserId)
                .Select(g => new
                {
                    UserId = g.Key,
                    Count = g.Count(),
                    Spent = g.Sum(o => o.Total),
                    Completed = g.Count(o => o.Status == OrderStatus.Claimed)
                }).ToListAsync(ct))
            .ToDictionary(x => x.UserId);

        var now = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow;
        var rows = (await db.Users.AsNoTracking().OrderBy(u => u.FullName).ToListAsync(ct))
            .Select(u =>
            {
                stats.TryGetValue(u.Id, out var s);
                return new AdminCustomerRow
                {
                    Id = u.Id,
                    FullName = u.FullName,
                    CampusId = u.CampusId,
                    Email = u.Email,
                    Roles = roleMap.GetValueOrDefault(u.Id) ?? new List<string>(),
                    EmailConfirmed = u.EmailConfirmed,
                    IsLocked = u.LockoutEnd.HasValue && u.LockoutEnd > now,
                    TotalOrders = s?.Count ?? 0,
                    TotalSpent = s?.Spent ?? 0,
                    IsFrequent = (s?.Completed ?? 0) >= 5
                };
            });

        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(q))
        {
            var t = q.Trim();
            bool Has(string? v) => v?.Contains(t, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == true;
            rows = rows.Where(r => Has(r.FullName) || Has(r.Email) || Has(r.CampusId) || r.Roles.Any(Has));
        }

        return View(new AdminCustomersViewModel { Rows = rows.ToList(), Query = q });
    }

    [HttpGet("CustomerDetails/{id}")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> CustomerDetails(string id, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var user = await users.FindByIdAsync(id);
        if (user is null) return NotFound();

        return View(new AdminCustomerDetailsViewModel
        {
            User = user,
            Roles = (await users.GetRolesAsync(user)).ToList(),
            IsLocked = user.LockoutEnd.HasValue && user.LockoutEnd > DateTimeOffset.UtcNow,
            Orders = await db.Orders.AsNoTracking()
                .Where(o => o.UserId == id)
                .OrderByDescending(o => o.CreatedAtUtc)
                .ToListAsync(ct)
        });
    }

    [HttpPost("UpdateUserRole"), ValidateAntiForgeryToken]
    public async Task<IActionResult> UpdateUserRole(string id, string role, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var user = await users.FindByIdAsync(id);
        if (user is null) return NotFound();

        if (!AppRoles.All.Contains(role))
        {
            TempData["Error"] = "Invalid role.";
            return RedirectToAction(nameof(CustomerDetails), new { id });
        }

        var current = (await users.GetRolesAsync(user)).ToList();
        if (current.Count == 1 && current[0] == role)
        {
            TempData["Error"] = $"User is already {role}.";
            return RedirectToAction(nameof(CustomerDetails), new { id });
        }

        if (current.Contains(AppRoles.Admin) && role != AppRoles.Admin
            && (await users.GetUsersInRoleAsync(AppRoles.Admin)).Count <= 1)
        {
            TempData["Error"] = "Cannot remove the role of the last Admin.";
            return RedirectToAction(nameof(CustomerDetails), new { id });
        }

        await using var tx = await db.Database.BeginTransactionAsync(ct);

        var ok = true;
        if (current.Count > 0) ok = (await users.RemoveFromRolesAsync(user, current)).Succeeded;
        ok = ok && (await users.AddToRoleAsync(user, role)).Succeeded;
        if (!ok)
        {
            await tx.RollbackAsync(ct);
            TempData["Error"] = "Could not update the role.";
            return RedirectToAction(nameof(CustomerDetails), new { id });
        }

        await users.UpdateSecurityStampAsync(user);
        audit.Add(ActorId, "UserRoleChanged", $"UserId={id}; {string.Join(",", current)}->{role}");
        await db.SaveChangesAsync(ct);
        await tx.CommitAsync(ct);

        TempData["Success"] = $"{user.FullName} is now {role}.";
        return RedirectToAction(nameof(CustomerDetails), new { id });
    }

    [HttpPost("ToggleUserLock"), ValidateAntiForgeryToken]
    public async Task<IActionResult> ToggleUserLock(string id, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var user = await users.FindByIdAsync(id);
        if (user is null) return NotFound();

        var locked = user.LockoutEnd.HasValue && user.LockoutEnd > DateTimeOffset.UtcNow;

        await using var tx = await db.Database.BeginTransactionAsync(ct);

        IdentityResult result;
        if (locked)
        {
            result = await users.SetLockoutEndDateAsync(user, null);
            if (result.Succeeded) await users.ResetAccessFailedCountAsync(user);
        }
        else
        {
            await users.SetLockoutEnabledAsync(user, true);
            result = await users.SetLockoutEndDateAsync(user, DateTimeOffset.MaxValue);
        }

        if (!result.Succeeded)
        {
            await tx.RollbackAsync(ct);
            TempData["Error"] = "Could not update the account lock.";
            return RedirectToAction(nameof(CustomerDetails), new { id });
        }

        audit.Add(ActorId, locked ? "UserUnlocked" : "UserLocked", $"UserId={id}; CampusId={user.CampusId}");
        await db.SaveChangesAsync(ct);
        await tx.CommitAsync(ct);

        TempData["Success"] = $"{user.FullName} has been {(locked ? "unlocked" : "locked")}.";
        return RedirectToAction(nameof(CustomerDetails), new { id });
    }

    // ---------------- Payment Management ----------------

    [HttpGet("Payments")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Payments(string? paymentStatus, string? method, string? date, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var today = DateTime.UtcNow.Date;
        var all = db.Orders.AsNoTracking();

        async Task<decimal> Sum(IQueryable<Order> src) =>
            await src.SumAsync(o => (decimal?)o.Total, ct) ?? 0;

        var q = all.Include(o => o.User).AsQueryable();
        var vm = new AdminPaymentsViewModel();

        if (Enum.TryParse<PaymentStatus>(paymentStatus, true, out var ps) && Enum.IsDefined(ps))
        {
            q = q.Where(o => o.PaymentStatus == ps);
            vm.PaymentStatusFilter = ps.ToString();
        }
        if (Enum.TryParse<PaymentMethod>(method, true, out var pm) && Enum.IsDefined(pm))
        {
            q = q.Where(o => o.PaymentMethod == pm);
            vm.MethodFilter = pm.ToString();
        }
        if (DateTime.TryParseExact(date, "yyyy-MM-dd", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture,
                DateTimeStyles.AssumeUniversal | DateTimeStyles.AdjustToUniversal, out var d))
        {
            var from = d.Date;
            var to = from.AddDays(1);
            q = q.Where(o => o.CreatedAtUtc >= from && o.CreatedAtUtc < to);
            vm.Date = from.ToString("yyyy-MM-dd");
        }

        vm.Orders = await q.OrderByDescending(o => o.CreatedAtUtc).ToListAsync(ct);
        vm.TodayCollected = await Sum(all.Where(o => o.PaymentStatus == PaymentStatus.Paid && o.PaidAtUtc >= today));
        vm.UnpaidPending = await Sum(all.Where(o => o.PaymentStatus == PaymentStatus.Unpaid
            && (o.Status == OrderStatus.Placed || o.Status == OrderStatus.Preparing || o.Status == OrderStatus.Ready)));
        vm.CashTotal = await Sum(all.Where(o => o.PaymentStatus == PaymentStatus.Paid && o.PaymentMethod == PaymentMethod.Cash));
        vm.GCashTotal = await Sum(all.Where(o => o.PaymentStatus == PaymentStatus.Paid && o.PaymentMethod == PaymentMethod.GCash));
        vm.RefundedTotal = await Sum(all.Where(o => o.PaymentStatus == PaymentStatus.Refunded));

        return View(vm);
    }

    [HttpPost("UpdatePaymentStatus"), ValidateAntiForgeryToken]
    public async Task<IActionResult> UpdatePaymentStatus(
        int id, PaymentStatus status, string? fp, string? fm, string? fd, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        IActionResult Back() => RedirectToAction(nameof(Payments), new { paymentStatus = fp, method = fm, date = fd });

        var order = await db.Orders.AsNoTracking().FirstOrDefaultAsync(o => o.Id == id, ct);
        if (order is null) return NotFound();

        var from = order.PaymentStatus;
        var allowed = (from, status) is (PaymentStatus.Unpaid, PaymentStatus.Paid) or (PaymentStatus.Paid, PaymentStatus.Refunded);
        if (!allowed || (status == PaymentStatus.Paid && order.Status == OrderStatus.Cancelled))
        {
            TempData["Error"] = $"Order #{id} payment cannot move from {from} to {status}.";
            return Back();
        }

        await using var tx = await db.Database.BeginTransactionAsync(ct);

        var now = DateTime.UtcNow;
        var rows = status == PaymentStatus.Paid
            ? await db.Orders.Where(o => o.Id == id && o.PaymentStatus == from)
                .ExecuteUpdateAsync(s => s
                    .SetProperty(o => o.PaymentStatus, status)
                    .SetProperty(o => o.PaidAtUtc, now), ct)
            : await db.Orders.Where(o => o.Id == id && o.PaymentStatus == from)
                .ExecuteUpdateAsync(s => s.SetProperty(o => o.PaymentStatus, status), ct);

        if (rows == 0)
        {
            await tx.RollbackAsync(ct);
            TempData["Error"] = $"Order #{id} changed in the meantime. Please review it again.";
            return Back();
        }

        audit.Add(ActorId, "PaymentStatusChanged",
            $"OrderId={id}; {from}->{status}; Method={order.PaymentMethod}; Amount={order.Total:0.00}");
        await db.SaveChangesAsync(ct);
        await tx.CommitAsync(ct);

        TempData["Success"] = $"Order #{id} marked {status}.";
        return Back();
    }

    // ---------------- Discounts & Promotions ----------------

    private async Task<AdminPromotionsViewModel> BuildPromotionsVm(CreatePromotionInput? input, CancellationToken ct) => new()
    {
        Promotions = await db.Promotions.AsNoTracking().OrderByDescending(p => p.Id).ToListAsync(ct),
        Input = input ?? new CreatePromotionInput()
    };

    [Authorize(Roles = AppRoles.Admin)]
    [HttpGet("Promotions")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Promotions(CancellationToken ct) =>
        View(await BuildPromotionsVm(null, ct));

    [Authorize(Roles = AppRoles.Admin)]
    [HttpPost("CreatePromotion"), ValidateAntiForgeryToken]
    public async Task<IActionResult> CreatePromotion([Bind(Prefix = "Input")] CreatePromotionInput input, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var code = input.Code?.Trim().ToUpperInvariant() ?? "";

        if (input.DiscountType == DiscountType.Percentage && input.DiscountValue > 100)
            ModelState.AddModelError("Input.DiscountValue", "Percentage cannot exceed 100.");
        if (input.ExpiryDate is { } exp && exp.Date < DateTime.UtcNow.Date)
            ModelState.AddModelError("Input.ExpiryDate", "Expiry date cannot be in the past.");
        if (code.Length > 0 && await db.Promotions.AnyAsync(p => p.Code == code, ct))
            ModelState.AddModelError("Input.Code", "This code already exists.");

        if (!ModelState.IsValid)
            return View("Promotions", await BuildPromotionsVm(input, ct));

        var promo = new Promotion
        {
            Code = code,
            Title = input.Title.Trim(),
            DiscountType = input.DiscountType,
            DiscountValue = input.DiscountValue,
            ExpiryDate = DateTime.SpecifyKind(input.ExpiryDate!.Value.Date.AddDays(1).AddSeconds(-1), DateTimeKind.Utc),
            MaxUses = input.MaxUses,
            IsActive = true
        };
        db.Promotions.Add(promo);
        await db.SaveChangesAsync(ct);

        await audit.LogAsync(ActorId, "PromotionCreated",
            $"PromotionId={promo.Id}; Code={promo.Code}; {promo.DiscountType}={promo.DiscountValue:0.00}; Expires={promo.ExpiryDate:yyyy-MM-dd}; MaxUses={promo.MaxUses?.ToString() ?? "none"}", ct);

        TempData["Success"] = $"Promotion {promo.Code} created.";
        return RedirectToAction(nameof(Promotions));
    }

    [Authorize(Roles = AppRoles.Admin)]
    [HttpPost("TogglePromotion"), ValidateAntiForgeryToken]
    public async Task<IActionResult> TogglePromotion(int id, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var promo = await db.Promotions.FirstOrDefaultAsync(p => p.Id == id, ct);
        if (promo is null) return NotFound();

        promo.IsActive = !promo.IsActive;
        audit.Add(ActorId, "PromotionToggled", $"PromotionId={id}; Code={promo.Code}; IsActive={promo.IsActive}");
        await db.SaveChangesAsync(ct);

        TempData["Success"] = $"{promo.Code} is now {(promo.IsActive ? "active" : "inactive")}.";
        return RedirectToAction(nameof(Promotions));
    }

    // ---------------- Reports & Records ----------------

    private static (DateTime Start, DateTime End) ResolveRange(DateTime? startDate, DateTime? endDate)
    {
        var today = DateTime.UtcNow.Date;
        var start = (startDate ?? new DateTime(today.Year, today.Month, 1)).Date;
        var end = (endDate ?? today).Date;
        if (end < start) (start, end) = (end, start);
        return (start, end);
    }

    private async Task<AdminReportsViewModel> BuildReportAsync(DateTime start, DateTime end, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var to = end.AddDays(1);
        var orders = await db.Orders.AsNoTracking()
            .Include(o => o.User).Include(o => o.Items)
            .Where(o => o.CreatedAtUtc >= start && o.CreatedAtUtc < to)
            .OrderByDescending(o => o.CreatedAtUtc)
            .ToListAsync(ct);

        var sales = orders.Where(o => o.Status != OrderStatus.Cancelled).ToList();

        static AdminSalesRow Row(string label, IEnumerable<Order> g)
        {
            var l = g.ToList();
            return new AdminSalesRow(label, l.Count, l.Sum(o => o.Total + o.DiscountAmount),
                l.Sum(o => o.DiscountAmount), l.Sum(o => o.Total));
        }

        var sold = sales.SelectMany(o => o.Items).GroupBy(i => i.MenuItemId)
            .ToDictionary(g => g.Key, g => (Units: g.Sum(i => i.Quantity), Revenue: g.Sum(i => i.UnitPrice * i.Quantity)));

        var perf = (await db.MenuItems.AsNoTracking().Select(m => new { m.Id, m.Name }).ToListAsync(ct))
            .Select(m => sold.TryGetValue(m.Id, out var s)
                ? new AdminProductRow(m.Name, s.Units, s.Revenue)
                : new AdminProductRow(m.Name, 0, 0m))
            .ToList();

        return new AdminReportsViewModel
        {
            StartDate = start,
            EndDate = end,
            Daily = sales.GroupBy(o => o.CreatedAtUtc.Date).OrderBy(g => g.Key)
                .Select(g => Row(g.Key.ToString("yyyy-MM-dd"), g)).ToList(),
            Monthly = sales.GroupBy(o => new { o.CreatedAtUtc.Year, o.CreatedAtUtc.Month })
                .OrderBy(g => g.Key.Year).ThenBy(g => g.Key.Month)
                .Select(g => Row($"{g.Key.Year}-{g.Key.Month:00}", g)).ToList(),
            MostPopular = perf.OrderByDescending(p => p.Units).ThenByDescending(p => p.Revenue).ThenBy(p => p.Name).Take(5).ToList(),
            LeastPopular = perf.OrderBy(p => p.Units).ThenBy(p => p.Revenue).ThenBy(p => p.Name).Take(5).ToList(),
            Transactions = orders
        };
    }

    [Authorize(Roles = AppRoles.Admin)]
    [HttpGet("Reports")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Reports(DateTime? startDate, DateTime? endDate, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var (start, end) = ResolveRange(startDate, endDate);
        return View(await BuildReportAsync(start, end, ct));
    }

    [Authorize(Roles = AppRoles.Admin)]
    [HttpGet("ExportReportsCsv")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> ExportReportsCsv(DateTime? startDate, DateTime? endDate, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var (start, end) = ResolveRange(startDate, endDate);
        var r = await BuildReportAsync(start, end, ct);

        static string Q(string? v)
        {
            v ??= "";
            if (v.Length > 0 && "=+-@\t\r".Contains(v[0])) v = "'" + v; // CSV formula-injection guard
            return "\"" + v.Replace("\"", "\"\"") + "\"";
        }
        static string N(decimal d) => d.ToString("0.00", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);

        var sb = new StringBuilder();
        sb.AppendLine($"BrewStop Report,{start:yyyy-MM-dd} to {end:yyyy-MM-dd}");
        sb.AppendLine();

        void Sales(string title, string col, List<AdminSalesRow> rows)
        {
            sb.AppendLine(title);
            sb.AppendLine($"{col},Order Count,Gross Sales,Discounts,Net Revenue");
            foreach (var x in rows) sb.AppendLine($"{Q(x.Label)},{x.Orders},{N(x.Gross)},{N(x.Discounts)},{N(x.Net)}");
            sb.AppendLine();
        }
        Sales("Daily Sales Summary", "Date", r.Daily);
        Sales("Monthly Revenue Summary", "Month", r.Monthly);

        void Products(string title, List<AdminProductRow> rows)
        {
            sb.AppendLine(title);
            sb.AppendLine("Product,Units Sold,Revenue");
            foreach (var x in rows) sb.AppendLine($"{Q(x.Name)},{x.Units},{N(x.Revenue)}");
            sb.AppendLine();
        }
        Products("Most Popular Products", r.MostPopular);
        Products("Least Popular Products", r.LeastPopular);

        sb.AppendLine("Transaction Records");
        sb.AppendLine("Order ID,Date/Time (UTC),Customer,Items,Payment Method,Status,Total");
        foreach (var o in r.Transactions)
            sb.AppendLine($"{o.Id},{o.CreatedAtUtc:yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm},{Q(o.User.FullName)},{Q(AdminReportsViewModel.ItemsSummary(o))},{o.PaymentMethod},{o.Status},{N(o.Total)}");

        return File(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes("\uFEFF" + sb),
            "text/csv", $"BrewStop_Report_{start:yyyyMMdd}_{end:yyyyMMdd}.csv");
    }
}
