using System.Security.Claims;
using BrewStop.Data;
using BrewStop.Models;
using BrewStop.Services;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace BrewStop.Controllers.Admin;

[Authorize(Roles = AppRoles.Admin)]
[Route("Admin/Inventory")]
public class InventoryController(ApplicationDbContext db, IAuditService audit) : Controller
{
    [HttpGet("")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Index() =>
        View("~/Views/Admin/Inventory/Index.cshtml",
            await db.InventoryItems.AsNoTracking()
                .Include(i => i.MenuItem).ThenInclude(m => m.Category)
                .OrderBy(i => i.MenuItem.Category.Name).ThenBy(i => i.MenuItem.Name)
                .ToListAsync());

    [HttpPost("Update/{id:int}"), ValidateAntiForgeryToken]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Update(int id, int quantity)
    {
        if (quantity < 0)
        {
            TempData["Error"] = "Quantity cannot be negative.";
            return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
        }

        var item = await db.InventoryItems.Include(i => i.MenuItem).FirstOrDefaultAsync(i => i.Id == id);
        if (item is null) return NotFound();

        var old = item.Quantity;
        item.Quantity = quantity;

        audit.Add(User.FindFirstValue(ClaimTypes.NameIdentifier), "InventoryUpdated",
            $"MenuItemId={item.MenuItemId}; {old}->{quantity}");

        await db.SaveChangesAsync();

        TempData["Success"] = $"{item.MenuItem.Name} stock set to {quantity}.";
        return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
    }
}


========================================
FILE: BrewStop\Controllers\Admin\MenuItemsController.cs
========================================
using BrewStop.Data;
using BrewStop.Models;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc.Rendering;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace BrewStop.Controllers.Admin;

[Authorize(Roles = AppRoles.Admin)]
[Route("Admin/MenuItems")]
public class MenuItemsController(ApplicationDbContext db, IWebHostEnvironment env) : Controller
{
    private const string FormView = "~/Views/Admin/MenuItems/Form.cshtml";
    private const string UploadUrl = "/uploads/menu/";
    private const long MaxBytes = 2 * 1024 * 1024;
    private static readonly string[] AllowedExt = [".jpg", ".jpeg", ".png", ".webp"];

    private async Task LoadCategoriesAsync(int? selected = null) =>
        ViewBag.Categories = new SelectList(await db.Categories.AsNoTracking().OrderBy(c => c.Name).ToListAsync(), "Id", "Name", selected);

    private static string? ValidateImage(IFormFile? file)
    {
        if (file is null || file.Length == 0) return null;
        if (file.Length > MaxBytes) return "Image must be 2 MB or smaller.";
        var ext = Path.GetExtension(file.FileName).ToLowerInvariant();
        if (!AllowedExt.Contains(ext) || !file.ContentType.StartsWith("image/"))
            return "Only JPG, PNG or WEBP images are allowed.";
        return null;
    }

    private async Task<string> SaveImageAsync(IFormFile file)
    {
        var dir = Path.Combine(env.WebRootPath, "uploads", "menu");
        Directory.CreateDirectory(dir);
        var name = $"{Guid.NewGuid():N}{Path.GetExtension(file.FileName).ToLowerInvariant()}";
        await using var fs = System.IO.File.Create(Path.Combine(dir, name));
        await file.CopyToAsync(fs);
        return UploadUrl + name;
    }

    private void DeleteImage(string? url)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(url) || !url.StartsWith(UploadUrl)) return;
        var path = Path.Combine(env.WebRootPath, "uploads", "menu", Path.GetFileName(url));
        if (System.IO.File.Exists(path)) System.IO.File.Delete(path);
    }

    [HttpGet("")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Index()
    {
        ViewBag.Stock = await db.InventoryItems.AsNoTracking().ToDictionaryAsync(i => i.MenuItemId, i => i.Quantity);
        return View("~/Views/Admin/MenuItems/Index.cshtml",
            await db.MenuItems.AsNoTracking().Include(m => m.Category)
                .OrderBy(m => m.Category.Name).ThenBy(m => m.Name).ToListAsync());
    }

    [HttpGet("Create")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Create()
    {
        await LoadCategoriesAsync();
        return View(FormView, new MenuItem());
    }

    [HttpPost("Create"), ValidateAntiForgeryToken]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Create([Bind("Name,Description,Price,BadgeText,CategoryId")] MenuItem m, IFormFile? image)
    {
        ModelState.Remove(nameof(MenuItem.Category));
        ModelState.Remove(nameof(MenuItem.ImageUrl));
        if (ValidateImage(image) is { } err) ModelState.AddModelError(string.Empty, err);

        if (!ModelState.IsValid)
        {
            await LoadCategoriesAsync(m.CategoryId);
            return View(FormView, m);
        }

        if (image is { Length: > 0 }) m.ImageUrl = await SaveImageAsync(image);

        db.MenuItems.Add(m);
        db.InventoryItems.Add(new InventoryItem { MenuItem = m, Quantity = 0 });
        await db.SaveChangesAsync();

        TempData["Success"] = $"{m.Name} created.";
        return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
    }

    [HttpGet("Edit/{id:int}")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Edit(int id)
    {
        var m = await db.MenuItems.FindAsync(id);
        if (m is null) return NotFound();
        await LoadCategoriesAsync(m.CategoryId);
        return View(FormView, m);
    }

    [HttpPost("Edit/{id:int}"), ValidateAntiForgeryToken]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Edit(int id, [Bind("Id,Name,Description,Price,BadgeText,CategoryId")] MenuItem input,
        IFormFile? image, bool removeImage = false)
    {
        if (id != input.Id) return BadRequest();
        ModelState.Remove(nameof(MenuItem.Category));
        ModelState.Remove(nameof(MenuItem.ImageUrl));

        var m = await db.MenuItems.FindAsync(id);
        if (m is null) return NotFound();

        if (ValidateImage(image) is { } err) ModelState.AddModelError(string.Empty, err);

        if (!ModelState.IsValid)
        {
            input.ImageUrl = m.ImageUrl;
            await LoadCategoriesAsync(input.CategoryId);
            return View(FormView, input);
        }

        m.Name = input.Name;
        m.Description = input.Description;
        m.Price = input.Price;
        m.BadgeText = input.BadgeText;
        m.CategoryId = input.CategoryId;

        if (image is { Length: > 0 })
        {
            DeleteImage(m.ImageUrl);
            m.ImageUrl = await SaveImageAsync(image);
        }
        else if (removeImage)
        {
            DeleteImage(m.ImageUrl);
            m.ImageUrl = string.Empty;
        }

        await db.SaveChangesAsync();

        TempData["Success"] = $"{m.Name} updated.";
        return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
    }

    [HttpPost("Delete/{id:int}"), ValidateAntiForgeryToken]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Delete(int id)
    {
        var m = await db.MenuItems.FindAsync(id);
        if (m is null) return NotFound();

        if (await db.OrderItems.AnyAsync(i => i.MenuItemId == id))
        {
            TempData["Error"] = $"{m.Name} has existing orders and cannot be deleted.";
            return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
        }

        var imageUrl = m.ImageUrl;
        db.MenuItems.Remove(m);
        await db.SaveChangesAsync();
        DeleteImage(imageUrl);

        TempData["Success"] = $"{m.Name} deleted.";
        return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
    }
}


========================================
FILE: BrewStop\Controllers\Api\BrewStopApiController.cs
========================================
using System.Security.Claims;
using BrewStop.Data;
using BrewStop.Models;
using BrewStop.Services;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace BrewStop.Controllers.Api;

public record MenuItemDto(int Id, string Name, string Description, string CategoryName,
    decimal BasePrice, int StockQuantity, bool IsAvailable);

public record OrderStatusDto(int OrderId, string Status, DateTime PickupSlot,
    string PaymentMethod, string PaymentStatus, DateTime UpdatedAt);

public record ScanRequest(string? Token);

[ApiController]
[Produces("application/json")]
[IgnoreAntiforgeryToken] // global AutoValidateAntiforgeryToken is for MVC forms only
[Route("api")]
public class BrewStopApiController(ApplicationDbContext db, IQrTokenService qr) : ControllerBase
{
    private string UserId => User.FindFirstValue(ClaimTypes.NameIdentifier)!;

    [AllowAnonymous]
    [HttpGet("menu")]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(List<MenuItemDto>), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> GetMenu(CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var rows = await (
            from m in db.MenuItems.AsNoTracking()
            join i in db.InventoryItems.AsNoTracking() on m.Id equals i.MenuItemId into inv
            from i in inv.DefaultIfEmpty()
            orderby m.Category.Name, m.Name
            select new
            {
                m.Id,
                m.Name,
                m.Description,
                CategoryName = m.Category.Name,
                BasePrice = m.Price,
                Stock = i != null ? i.Quantity : 0
            }).ToListAsync(ct);

        return Ok(rows.Select(r => new MenuItemDto(
            r.Id, r.Name, r.Description, r.CategoryName, r.BasePrice, r.Stock, r.Stock > 0)));
    }

    [Authorize]
    [HttpGet("orders/{id:int}/status")]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(OrderStatusDto), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> GetOrderStatus(int id, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var o = await db.Orders.AsNoTracking()
            .Where(x => x.Id == id)
            .Select(x => new
            {
                x.Id,
                x.UserId,
                x.Status,
                x.PickupAtUtc,
                x.PaymentMethod,
                x.PaymentStatus,
                x.CreatedAtUtc,
                x.PaidAtUtc
            })
            .FirstOrDefaultAsync(ct);

        if (o is null)
            return NotFound(new { success = false, message = "Order not found." });

        var isStaff = User.IsInRole(AppRoles.Barista) || User.IsInRole(AppRoles.Admin);
        if (o.UserId != UserId && !isStaff)
            return StatusCode(StatusCodes.Status403Forbidden,
                new { success = false, message = "You do not have access to this order." });

        var exact = $"OrderId={id}";
        var prefix = $"OrderId={id};";
        var lastAudit = await db.AuditLogs.AsNoTracking()
            .Where(a => (a.Action == "OrderStatusChanged" || a.Action == "QrScanAccepted")
                     && (a.Details == exact || a.Details!.StartsWith(prefix)))
            .MaxAsync(a => (DateTime?)a.CreatedAtUtc, ct);

        var updated = new[] { o.CreatedAtUtc, o.PaidAtUtc ?? DateTime.MinValue, lastAudit ?? DateTime.MinValue }.Max();

        return Ok(new OrderStatusDto(
            o.Id, o.Status.ToString(),
            DateTime.SpecifyKind(o.PickupAtUtc, DateTimeKind.Utc),
            o.PaymentMethod.ToString(), o.PaymentStatus.ToString(),
            DateTime.SpecifyKind(updated, DateTimeKind.Utc)));
    }

    [Authorize(Roles = $"{AppRoles.Barista},{AppRoles.Admin}")]
    [HttpPost("barista/scan")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Scan([FromBody] ScanRequest? req, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var token = req?.Token?.Trim();
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(token))
            return BadRequest(new { success = false, message = "QR token is required." });
        if (token.Length > 512)
            return BadRequest(new { success = false, message = "Invalid QR token." });

        var (result, orderId) = await qr.ClaimAsync(token, UserId, ct);

        if (result == ScanResult.Ok)
            return Ok(new { success = true, message = $"Order #{orderId} claimed.", orderId });

        var msg = result switch
        {
            ScanResult.AlreadyUsed => $"Order #{orderId} was already claimed.",
            ScanResult.Expired => $"Pickup code for order #{orderId} has expired.",
            ScanResult.Revoked => $"Order #{orderId} was cancelled.",
            ScanResult.WrongStatus => $"Order #{orderId} is not Ready yet.",
            _ => "Invalid QR token."
        };
        return BadRequest(new { success = false, message = msg, orderId });
    }
}

FILE: BrewStop\Data\ApplicationDbContext.cs
========================================
using BrewStop.Models;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Identity.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace BrewStop.Data;

public class ApplicationDbContext : IdentityDbContext<ApplicationUser>
{
    public ApplicationDbContext(DbContextOptions<ApplicationDbContext> options) : base(options) { }

    public DbSet<Category> Categories => Set<Category>();
    public DbSet<MenuItem> MenuItems => Set<MenuItem>();
    public DbSet<ItemSize> ItemSizes => Set<ItemSize>();
    public DbSet<AddOn> AddOns => Set<AddOn>();
    public DbSet<Order> Orders => Set<Order>();
    public DbSet<OrderItem> OrderItems => Set<OrderItem>();
    public DbSet<OrderItemAddOn> OrderItemAddOns => Set<OrderItemAddOn>();
    public DbSet<InventoryItem> InventoryItems => Set<InventoryItem>();
    public DbSet<AuditLog> AuditLogs => Set<AuditLog>();
    public DbSet<OrderQrToken> OrderQrTokens => Set<OrderQrToken>();
    public DbSet<Promotion> Promotions => Set<Promotion>();
    public DbSet<FavoriteItem> FavoriteItems => Set<FavoriteItem>();
    public DbSet<ProductReview> ProductReviews => Set<ProductReview>();
    public DbSet<LoyaltyTransaction> LoyaltyTransactions => Set<LoyaltyTransaction>();
    public DbSet<BaristaShift> BaristaShifts => Set<BaristaShift>();

    protected override void OnModelCreating(ModelBuilder modelBuilder)
    {
        base.OnModelCreating(modelBuilder);

        modelBuilder.Entity<ApplicationUser>(e =>
        {
            e.HasIndex(u => u.CampusId).IsUnique();
            e.ToTable(t => t.HasCheckConstraint("CK_AspNetUsers_LoyaltyPoints", "`LoyaltyPoints` >= 0"));
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<Promotion>().HasIndex(p => p.OwnerUserId);

        modelBuilder.Entity<OrderQrToken>(e =>
        {
            e.HasIndex(t => t.Nonce).IsUnique();
            e.HasIndex(t => t.OrderId).IsUnique();
            e.HasOne(t => t.Order).WithMany().HasForeignKey(t => t.OrderId).OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<Category>(e =>
        {
            e.HasIndex(c => c.Slug).IsUnique();
            e.HasMany(c => c.MenuItems)
             .WithOne(m => m.Category)
             .HasForeignKey(m => m.CategoryId)
             .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<MenuItem>(e =>
        {
            e.Property(m => m.Price).HasPrecision(10, 2);
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<ItemSize>(e =>
        {
            e.Property(s => s.PriceDelta).HasPrecision(10, 2);
            e.HasIndex(s => s.Name).IsUnique();
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<AddOn>(e =>
        {
            e.Property(a => a.Price).HasPrecision(10, 2);
            e.HasIndex(a => new { a.Group, a.Name }).IsUnique();
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<Order>(e =>
        {
            e.Property(o => o.Total).HasPrecision(10, 2);
            e.Property(o => o.Status).HasConversion<string>().HasMaxLength(20);
            e.Property(o => o.PaymentMethod).HasConversion<string>().HasMaxLength(20);
            e.Property(o => o.PaymentStatus).HasConversion<string>().HasMaxLength(20);
            e.HasIndex(o => new { o.UserId, o.CreatedAtUtc });
            e.HasIndex(o => new { o.Status, o.PickupAtUtc });
            e.HasIndex(o => new { o.PaymentStatus, o.PaymentMethod });
            e.HasOne(o => o.User).WithMany().HasForeignKey(o => o.UserId).OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<OrderItem>(e =>
        {
            e.Property(i => i.UnitPrice).HasPrecision(10, 2);
            e.HasOne(i => i.Order).WithMany(o => o.Items).HasForeignKey(i => i.OrderId).OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);
            e.HasOne(i => i.MenuItem).WithMany().HasForeignKey(i => i.MenuItemId).OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<OrderItemAddOn>(e =>
        {
            e.Property(a => a.Price).HasPrecision(10, 2);
            e.HasOne(a => a.OrderItem).WithMany(i => i.AddOns).HasForeignKey(a => a.OrderItemId).OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);
            e.HasOne(a => a.AddOn).WithMany().HasForeignKey(a => a.AddOnId).OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<InventoryItem>(e =>
        {
            e.HasIndex(i => i.MenuItemId).IsUnique();
            e.HasOne(i => i.MenuItem).WithOne().HasForeignKey<InventoryItem>(i => i.MenuItemId)
             .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);
            e.ToTable(t => t.HasCheckConstraint("CK_InventoryItems_Quantity", "`Quantity` >= 0"));
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<AuditLog>(e =>
        {
            e.HasIndex(a => a.CreatedAtUtc);
            e.HasIndex(a => a.UserId);
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<FavoriteItem>(e =>
        {
            e.HasIndex(f => new { f.UserId, f.MenuItemId }).IsUnique();
            e.HasOne(f => f.User).WithMany().HasForeignKey(f => f.UserId).OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);
            e.HasOne(f => f.MenuItem).WithMany().HasForeignKey(f => f.MenuItemId).OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<ProductReview>(e =>
        {
            e.HasIndex(r => new { r.UserId, r.OrderId, r.MenuItemId }).IsUnique();
            e.HasIndex(r => new { r.MenuItemId, r.CreatedAtUtc });
            e.HasOne(r => r.User).WithMany().HasForeignKey(r => r.UserId).OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);
            e.HasOne(r => r.MenuItem).WithMany().HasForeignKey(r => r.MenuItemId).OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);
            e.HasOne(r => r.Order).WithMany().HasForeignKey(r => r.OrderId).OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);
            e.ToTable(t => t.HasCheckConstraint("CK_ProductReviews_Rating", "`Rating` BETWEEN 1 AND 5"));
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<LoyaltyTransaction>(e =>
        {
            e.HasIndex(l => new { l.UserId, l.CreatedAtUtc });
            e.HasIndex(l => new { l.OrderId, l.Reason }).IsUnique();   // one earn row per order
            e.HasOne(l => l.User).WithMany().HasForeignKey(l => l.UserId).OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);
            e.HasOne(l => l.Order).WithMany().HasForeignKey(l => l.OrderId).OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.SetNull);
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<BaristaShift>(e =>
        {
            e.Property(s => s.TotalHours).HasPrecision(6, 2);
            e.Property(s => s.ShiftStatus).HasConversion<string>().HasMaxLength(20);
            e.HasIndex(s => new { s.BaristaUserId, s.ScheduledStart });
            e.HasIndex(s => s.ClockInAt);
            e.HasOne(s => s.BaristaUser).WithMany().HasForeignKey(s => s.BaristaUserId).OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);
        });

        // ---------- Seed data ----------

        modelBuilder.Entity<ItemSize>().HasData(
            new ItemSize { Id = 1, Name = "Small", PriceDelta = 0.00m, SortOrder = 1 },
            new ItemSize { Id = 2, Name = "Medium", PriceDelta = 0.50m, SortOrder = 2 },
            new ItemSize { Id = 3, Name = "Large", PriceDelta = 1.00m, SortOrder = 3 }
        );

        modelBuilder.Entity<AddOn>().HasData(
            new AddOn { Id = 1, Name = "Extra Espresso Shot", Price = 0.75m, Group = "Drinks" },
            new AddOn { Id = 2, Name = "Oat Milk", Price = 0.60m, Group = "Drinks" },
            new AddOn { Id = 3, Name = "Whipped Cream", Price = 0.40m, Group = "Drinks" },
            new AddOn { Id = 4, Name = "Vanilla Syrup", Price = 0.50m, Group = "Drinks" },
            new AddOn { Id = 5, Name = "Caramel Drizzle", Price = 0.50m, Group = "Drinks" },
            new AddOn { Id = 6, Name = "Warm It Up", Price = 0.00m, Group = "Food" }
        );

        modelBuilder.Entity<Category>().HasData(
            new Category { Id = 1, Slug = "coffee", Name = "Coffee", Group = "Drinks", ImageUrl = "" },
            new Category { Id = 2, Slug = "pastries", Name = "Pastries", Group = "Food", ImageUrl = "" }
        );

        modelBuilder.Entity<MenuItem>().HasData(
            new MenuItem { Id = 1, Name = "Caramel Latte", Description = "Espresso, steamed milk, and house caramel.", Price = 50.50m, BadgeText = "Bestseller", CategoryId = 1 },
            new MenuItem { Id = 2, Name = "Butter Croissant", Description = "Flaky, freshly baked all-butter croissant.", Price = 30.25m, BadgeText = "Bestseller", CategoryId = 2 },
            new MenuItem { Id = 4, Name = "Strawberry Crème Éclair", Description = "Delicate choux pastry filled with smooth vanilla cream and topped with rich strawberry glaze.", Price = 30.95m, ImageUrl = "/images/Strawberry Eclair.png", BadgeText = "Bestseller", CategoryId = 2 },
            new MenuItem { Id = 5, Name = "Golden Honey Puff", Description = "Flaky golden puff pastry filled with sweet honey custard and dusted with powdered sugar.", Price = 30.25m, BadgeText = "New", CategoryId = 2 },
            new MenuItem { Id = 6, Name = "Midnight Cocoa Twist", Description = "Buttery twisted pastry layered with dark cocoa filling and finished with chocolate drizzle.", Price = 35.75m, BadgeText = "Popular", CategoryId = 2 },
            new MenuItem { Id = 7, Name = "Roselle Berry Tart", Description = "Crispy buttery tart shell filled with creamy custard and topped with fresh mixed berries.", Price = 50.50m, BadgeText = "Chef's Pick", CategoryId = 2 },
            new MenuItem { Id = 8, Name = "Celestia Almond Croissant", Description = "Golden flaky croissant filled with almond cream and topped with toasted almond slices.", Price = 40.25m, BadgeText = "Special", CategoryId = 2 },
            new MenuItem { Id = 9, Name = "Caramel Cloud Latte", Description = "Smooth espresso blended with steamed milk and sweet caramel syrup, topped with creamy foam.", Price = 39.25m, BadgeText = "Bestseller", CategoryId = 1 },

        modelBuilder.Entity<InventoryItem>().HasData(

========================================
FILE: BrewStop\Data\DbInitializer.cs
========================================
using BrewStop.Models;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Identity;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace BrewStop.Data;

public static class DbInitializer
{
    public static async Task SeedAsync(IServiceProvider services)
    {
        using var scope = services.CreateScope();
        var sp = scope.ServiceProvider;
        var roles = sp.GetRequiredService<RoleManager<IdentityRole>>();
        var users = sp.GetRequiredService<UserManager<ApplicationUser>>();
        var db = sp.GetRequiredService<ApplicationDbContext>();
        var config = sp.GetRequiredService<IConfiguration>();
        var log = sp.GetRequiredService<ILoggerFactory>().CreateLogger("DbInitializer");

        foreach (var role in AppRoles.All)
            if (!await roles.RoleExistsAsync(role))
                await roles.CreateAsync(new IdentityRole(role));

        await EnsureUserAsync(users, log, config["Seed:AdminEmail"], config["Seed:AdminPassword"],
            "BrewStop Admin", "ADMIN-001", AppRoles.Admin);
        await EnsureUserAsync(users, log, config["Seed:BaristaEmail"], config["Seed:BaristaPassword"],
            "BrewStop Barista", "BARISTA-001", AppRoles.Barista);

        await EnsureTestItemsAsync(db);
    }

    private static async Task EnsureUserAsync(UserManager<ApplicationUser> users, ILogger log,
        string? email, string? password, string fullName, string campusId, string role)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(email) || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(password)) return;
        if (await users.FindByEmailAsync(email) is not null) return;

        var user = new ApplicationUser
        {
            UserName = email,
            Email = email,
            EmailConfirmed = true,
            FullName = fullName,
            CampusId = campusId
        };

        var result = await users.CreateAsync(user, password);
        if (result.Succeeded) await users.AddToRoleAsync(user, role);
        else log.LogError("{Role} seed failed: {Errors}", role, string.Join("; ", result.Errors.Select(e => e.Description)));
    }

    // TC-04: sold-out item (stock 0). TC-10: exactly one left (stock 1). Created once; admins can restock later.
    private static async Task EnsureTestItemsAsync(ApplicationDbContext db)
    {
        var coffee = await db.Categories.FirstOrDefaultAsync(c => c.Slug == "coffee");
        if (coffee is null) return;

        var wanted = new (string Name, string Description, decimal Price, int Stock)[]
        {
            ("Iced Spanish Latte", "Espresso over ice with sweetened condensed milk.", 48.50m, 0),
            ("Caramel Macchiato", "Vanilla milk marked with espresso and caramel drizzle.", 49.50m, 1)
        };

        foreach (var w in wanted)
        {
            if (await db.MenuItems.AnyAsync(m => m.Name == w.Name)) continue;

            var item = new MenuItem
            {
                Name = w.Name,
                Description = w.Description,
                Price = w.Price,
                BadgeText = "New",
                CategoryId = coffee.Id
            };
            db.MenuItems.Add(item);
            db.InventoryItems.Add(new InventoryItem { MenuItem = item, Quantity = w.Stock });
        }

        await db.SaveChangesAsync();
    }
}

FILE: BrewStop\Hub\OrderHub.cs

using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.SignalR;

namespace BrewStop.Hubs;

[Authorize]
public class OrderHub : Hub
{
    public override async Task OnConnectedAsync()
    {
        if (Context.UserIdentifier is { } uid)
            await Groups.AddToGroupAsync(Context.ConnectionId, $"user:{uid}");

        if (Context.User?.IsInRole("Barista") == true || Context.User?.IsInRole("Admin") == true)
            await Groups.AddToGroupAsync(Context.ConnectionId, "staff");

        await base.OnConnectedAsync();
    }
}

    // Filter (shop-local dates; default = last 7 days)

    // KPIs
    public decimal RevenueToday { get; set; }

    public decimal AverageOrderValue { get; set; }       // over the selected range

    public int PendingPickupCount { get; set; }          // Placed + Preparing + Ready, right now
    public decimal RangeRevenue { get; set; }
    public int RangeOrders { get; set; }

    // Charts
    public string[] DailyLabels { get; set; } = Array.Empty<string>();
    public decimal[] DailyRevenueData { get; set; } = Array.Empty<decimal>();
    public int[] DailyOrderCountData { get; set; } = Array.Empty<int>();
    public string[] PaymentMethodLabels { get; set; } = { "Cash", "GCash" };
    public int[] PaymentMethodCounts { get; set; } = new int[2];
    public string[] OrderStatusLabels { get; set; } = Array.Empty<string>();
    public int[] OrderStatusCounts { get; set; } = Array.Empty<int>();

    public List<TopSellingDrinkDto> TopSellingDrinks { get; set; } = new();

    // Shifts

public static class AdminOrderRules
{
    public static OrderStatus[] NextOptions(OrderStatus s) => s switch
    {
        OrderStatus.Placed => [OrderStatus.Preparing, OrderStatus.Cancelled],
        OrderStatus.Preparing => [OrderStatus.Ready, OrderStatus.Cancelled],
        OrderStatus.Ready => [OrderStatus.Claimed, OrderStatus.Cancelled],
        _ => []
    };
}

public class CartLineViewModel
{
    public string Key { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    public int MenuItemId { get; set; }
    public string Name { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    public string ImageUrl { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    public int? SizeId { get; set; }
    public string? SizeName { get; set; }
    public List<CartAddOnDto> AddOns { get; set; } = new();
    public decimal UnitPrice { get; set; }
    public int Quantity { get; set; }
    public decimal LineTotal => UnitPrice * Quantity;
    public int StockLeft { get; set; }
    public bool ExceedsStock { get; set; }
}

public class CartViewModel
{
    public List<CartLineViewModel> Lines { get; set; } = new();
    public decimal Total => Lines.Sum(l => l.LineTotal);
    public int ItemCount => Lines.Sum(l => l.Quantity);

    public bool HasStockProblem => Lines.Any(l => l.ExceedsStock);
}

using System.ComponentModel.DataAnnotations;

namespace BrewStop.Models;

public enum OrderStatus { Placed, Preparing, Ready, Claimed, Cancelled }
public enum PaymentMethod { Cash, GCash }
public enum PaymentStatus { Unpaid, Paid, Refunded }

public class Order
{
    public int Id { get; set; }

    [Required]
    public string UserId { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    public ApplicationUser User { get; set; } = null!;

    public OrderStatus Status { get; set; } = OrderStatus.Placed;

    public DateTime PickupAtUtc { get; set; }

    [MaxLength(200)]
    public string? Note { get; set; }

    [MaxLength(30)]
    public string? PromoCode { get; set; }
    public decimal DiscountAmount { get; set; }

    public decimal Total { get; set; } 

    public PaymentMethod PaymentMethod { get; set; } = PaymentMethod.Cash;
    public PaymentStatus PaymentStatus { get; set; } = PaymentStatus.Unpaid;

    [MaxLength(40)]
    public string? PaymentReference { get; set; }  

    public DateTime? PaidAtUtc { get; set; } 

    public DateTime CreatedAtUtc { get; set; } = DateTime.UtcNow;

    public List<OrderItem> Items { get; set; } = new();

public static class OrderDisplayExtensions
{
    
    public static string Label(this PaymentStatus s) => s switch
    {

        PaymentStatus.Paid => "Paid",
        PaymentStatus.Refunded => "Refunded",

    };

========================================
FILE: BrewStop\Models\ReviewQueries.cs
========================================
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace BrewStop.Data;

public static class ReviewQueries
{
    public static async Task<Dictionary<int, (int Count, double Avg)>> GetRatingStatsAsync(
        this ApplicationDbContext db, List<int>? menuItemIds = null, CancellationToken ct = default)
    {
        var q = db.ProductReviews.AsNoTracking();
        if (menuItemIds is not null) q = q.Where(r => menuItemIds.Contains(r.MenuItemId));

        var rows = await q.GroupBy(r => r.MenuItemId)
            .Select(g => new { Id = g.Key, Count = g.Count(), Avg = g.Average(x => (double)x.Rating) })
            .ToListAsync(ct);

        return rows.ToDictionary(r => r.Id, r => (r.Count, r.Avg));
    }
}

using BrewStop.Data;
using BrewStop.Models;

namespace BrewStop.Services;

public interface IAuditService
{
    /// <summary>Stages an entry; it is saved with the caller's next SaveChanges (so it joins a transaction).</summary>
    void Add(string? userId, string action, string? details = null);

    /// <summary>Stages and saves immediately.</summary>
    Task LogAsync(string? userId, string action, string? details = null, CancellationToken ct = default);
}

public class AuditService(ApplicationDbContext db, ILogger<AuditService> log) : IAuditService
{
    public void Add(string? userId, string action, string? details = null)
    {
        db.AuditLogs.Add(new AuditLog
        {
            UserId = userId,
            Action = action,
            Details = details is { Length: > 500 } ? details[..500] : details,
            CreatedAtUtc = DateTime.UtcNow
        });
        log.LogInformation("Audit {Action} by {UserId}: {Details}", action, userId ?? "system", details);
    }

    public async Task LogAsync(string? userId, string action, string? details = null, CancellationToken ct = default)
    {
        Add(userId, action, details);
        await db.SaveChangesAsync(ct);
    }
}


========================================
FILE: BrewStop\Services\CaptchaService.cs
========================================
using System.Security.Cryptography;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Caching.Memory;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Options;

namespace BrewStop.Services;

public class CaptchaOptions
{
    public string ImageFolder { get; set; } = "images/captcha";
    public int MinCoffee { get; set; } = 2;
    public int MaxCoffee { get; set; } = 4;
    public int ChallengeMinutes { get; set; } = 5;
    public int TokenMinutes { get; set; } = 10;
}

public sealed record CaptchaTile(string Id, string File, bool IsCoffee);
public sealed record CaptchaChallenge(string Id, string Action, IReadOnlyList<CaptchaTile> Tiles);

public interface ICaptchaService
{
    CaptchaChallenge? CreateChallenge(string action);
    string? GetTileFile(string challengeId, string tileId);
    string? Solve(string challengeId, IEnumerable<string> selectedTileIds, string action);
    Task<bool> VerifyAsync(HttpRequest request, string expectedAction, CancellationToken ct = default);
}

public class CaptchaService(
    IMemoryCache cache,
    IOptions<CaptchaOptions> options,
    IWebHostEnvironment env,
    ILogger<CaptchaService> log) : ICaptchaService
{
    public const string FormField = "bs-captcha-token";
    private static readonly string[] Ext = ["*.jpg", "*.jpeg", "*.png", "*.webp"];
    private readonly CaptchaOptions _o = options.Value;

    private static string ChKey(string id) => "captcha:ch:" + id;
    private static string TokKey(string t) => "captcha:tok:" + t;
    private static string NewId(int bytes) => Convert.ToHexString(RandomNumberGenerator.GetBytes(bytes));

    public CaptchaChallenge? CreateChallenge(string action)
    {
        var root = Path.Combine(env.WebRootPath, _o.ImageFolder);

        // FIX: Search the "coffee" subfolder AND the root folder for files starting with "coffee"
        var coffee = List(Path.Combine(root, "coffee"))
            .Concat(List(root).Where(f => Path.GetFileName(f).StartsWith("coffee", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)))
            .Distinct()
            .ToList();

        var other = List(Path.Combine(root, "other"));

        if (coffee.Count < _o.MinCoffee || other.Count == 0)
        {
            log.LogError("Captcha images missing in {Root}: coffee={C} (need >= {MinC}), other={O} (need >= 1)",
                root, coffee.Count, _o.MinCoffee, other.Count);
            return null;
        }

        var coffeeCount = Math.Min(RandomNumberGenerator.GetInt32(_o.MinCoffee, _o.MaxCoffee + 1), coffee.Count);
        var otherCount = 9 - coffeeCount;

        var otherPool = Shuffle(other);
        var otherPicks = Enumerable.Range(0, otherCount).Select(i => otherPool[i % otherPool.Count]);

        var tiles = Shuffle(coffee).Take(coffeeCount).Select(f => new CaptchaTile(NewId(8), f, true))
            .Concat(otherPicks.Select(f => new CaptchaTile(NewId(8), f, false)))
            .ToList();
        Shuffle(tiles);

        var ch = new CaptchaChallenge(NewId(16), action, tiles);
        cache.Set(ChKey(ch.Id), ch, TimeSpan.FromMinutes(_o.ChallengeMinutes));
        return ch;
    }

    public string? GetTileFile(string challengeId, string tileId) =>
        cache.TryGetValue(ChKey(challengeId), out CaptchaChallenge? ch)
            ? ch?.Tiles.FirstOrDefault(t => t.Id == tileId)?.File
            : null;

    public string? Solve(string challengeId, IEnumerable<string> selectedTileIds, string action)
    {
        if (!cache.TryGetValue(ChKey(challengeId), out CaptchaChallenge? ch) || ch is null) return null;
        cache.Remove(ChKey(challengeId));

        if (!string.Equals(ch.Action, action, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) return null;

        var picked = selectedTileIds.ToHashSet();
        var correct = ch.Tiles.Where(t => t.IsCoffee).Select(t => t.Id).ToHashSet();
        if (!picked.SetEquals(correct)) return null;

        var token = NewId(24);
        cache.Set(TokKey(token), ch.Action, TimeSpan.FromMinutes(_o.TokenMinutes));
        return token;
    }

    public Task<bool> VerifyAsync(HttpRequest request, string expectedAction, CancellationToken ct = default)
    {
        var token = request.HasFormContentType ? request.Form[FormField].ToString() : string.Empty;
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(token) || token.Length > 128 ||
            !cache.TryGetValue(TokKey(token), out string? action))
        {
            log.LogWarning("Captcha token missing/invalid for {Action}", expectedAction);
            return Task.FromResult(false);
        }

        cache.Remove(TokKey(token));
        return Task.FromResult(string.Equals(action, expectedAction, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
    }

    private static List<string> List(string dir) =>
        Directory.Exists(dir) ? Ext.SelectMany(e => Directory.EnumerateFiles(dir, e)).ToList() : [];

    private static List<T> Shuffle<T>(List<T> src)
    {
        for (var i = src.Count - 1; i > 0; i--)
        {
            var j = RandomNumberGenerator.GetInt32(i + 1);
            (src[i], src[j]) = (src[j], src[i]);
        }
        return src;
    }
}


========================================
FILE: BrewStop\Services\CartService.cs
========================================
using System.Text.Json;
using BrewStop.Data;
using BrewStop.Models;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace BrewStop.Services;

public record CartEntry(string Key, int MenuItemId, int? SizeId, int[] AddOnIds, int Quantity);

public interface ICartService
{
    int Count { get; }
    Task<(bool Ok, string? Error)> AddAsync(int menuItemId, int? sizeId, IEnumerable<int> addOnIds, int quantity);
    void SetQuantity(string key, int quantity);
    void Remove(string key);
    void Clear();
    Task<CartViewModel> GetAsync();
}

public class CartService(ApplicationDbContext db, IHttpContextAccessor http, IInventoryService inventory, IImageResolver images) : ICartService
{
    public const int MaxQuantity = 10;
    public const int MaxLines = 20;
    private const string SessionKey = "brewstop.cart";

    private ISession Session => http.HttpContext!.Session;

    private List<CartEntry> Load()
    {
        var json = Session.GetString(SessionKey);
        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(json)) return new();
        try { return JsonSerializer.Deserialize<List<CartEntry>>(json) ?? new(); }
        catch (JsonException) { return new(); }
    }

    private void Save(List<CartEntry> entries) =>
        Session.SetString(SessionKey, JsonSerializer.Serialize(entries));

    public int Count => Load().Sum(e => e.Quantity);

    public async Task<(bool Ok, string? Error)> AddAsync(
        int menuItemId, int? sizeId, IEnumerable<int> addOnIds, int quantity)
    {
        quantity = Math.Clamp(quantity, 1, MaxQuantity);

        var item = await db.MenuItems.AsNoTracking()
            .Include(m => m.Category)
            .FirstOrDefaultAsync(m => m.Id == menuItemId);
        if (item is null) return (false, "That item is no longer on the menu.");

        var stock = (await inventory.GetStockAsync(new[] { menuItemId })).GetValueOrDefault(menuItemId);
        if (stock <= 0) return (false, $"{item.Name} is sold out.");

        var inCart = Load().Where(e => e.MenuItemId == menuItemId).Sum(e => e.Quantity);
        if (inCart + quantity > stock)
            return (false, $"Only {stock} left in stock (you already have {inCart} in your cart).");

        var isDrink = item.Category.Group == "Drinks";

        int? validSizeId = null;
        if (isDrink)
        {
            if (sizeId is null) return (false, "Please choose a size.");
            if (!await db.ItemSizes.AnyAsync(s => s.Id == sizeId && s.IsActive))
                return (false, "That size is not available.");
            validSizeId = sizeId;
        }

        var ids = addOnIds.Distinct().OrderBy(i => i).ToArray();
        if (ids.Length > 0)
        {
            var group = item.Category.Group;

            var idList = ids.ToList();

            var valid = await db.AddOns.CountAsync(a => idList.Contains(a.Id) && a.IsActive && a.Group == group);

            if (valid != ids.Length) return (false, "One or more add-ons are not available for this item.");
        }

        var key = $"{menuItemId}:{validSizeId?.ToString() ?? "-"}:{string.Join(",", ids)}";
        var entries = Load();
        var index = entries.FindIndex(e => e.Key == key);

        if (index >= 0)
            entries[index] = entries[index] with { Quantity = Math.Min(MaxQuantity, entries[index].Quantity + quantity) };
        else
        {
            if (entries.Count >= MaxLines) return (false, "Your cart is full.");
            entries.Add(new CartEntry(key, menuItemId, validSizeId, ids, quantity));
        }

        Save(entries);
        return (true, null);
    }

    public void SetQuantity(string key, int quantity)
    {
        var entries = Load();
        var index = entries.FindIndex(e => e.Key == key);
        if (index < 0) return;

        if (quantity <= 0) entries.RemoveAt(index);
        else entries[index] = entries[index] with { Quantity = Math.Min(quantity, MaxQuantity) };

        Save(entries);
    }

    public void Remove(string key)
    {
        var entries = Load();
        if (entries.RemoveAll(e => e.Key == key) > 0) Save(entries);
    }

    public void Clear() => Session.Remove(SessionKey);

    public async Task<CartViewModel> GetAsync()
    {
        var vm = new CartViewModel();
        var entries = Load();
        if (entries.Count == 0) return vm;

        var itemIds = entries.Select(e => e.MenuItemId).Distinct().ToList();

        var stock = await inventory.GetStockAsync(itemIds);

        var items = await db.MenuItems.AsNoTracking()
            .Where(m => itemIds.Contains(m.Id))
            .ToDictionaryAsync(m => m.Id);
        var sizes = await db.ItemSizes.AsNoTracking().Where(s => s.IsActive).ToDictionaryAsync(s => s.Id);
        var addOns = await db.AddOns.AsNoTracking().Where(a => a.IsActive).ToDictionaryAsync(a => a.Id);

        var kept = new List<CartEntry>();
        foreach (var e in entries)
        {
            if (!items.TryGetValue(e.MenuItemId, out var item)) continue;

            ItemSize? size = null;
            if (e.SizeId is int sid && !sizes.TryGetValue(sid, out size)) continue;

            var lineAddOns = new List<CartAddOnDto>();
            var valid = true;
            foreach (var aid in e.AddOnIds)
            {
                if (addOns.TryGetValue(aid, out var a)) lineAddOns.Add(new CartAddOnDto(a.Id, a.Name, a.Price));
                else { valid = false; break; }
            }
            if (!valid) continue;

            kept.Add(e);
            vm.Lines.Add(new CartLineViewModel
            {
                Key = e.Key,
                MenuItemId = item.Id,
                Name = item.Name,
                ImageUrl = images.Resolve(item.ImageUrl, item.Name),
                SizeId = size?.Id,
                SizeName = size?.Name,
                AddOns = lineAddOns,
                UnitPrice = item.Price + (size?.PriceDelta ?? 0m) + lineAddOns.Sum(a => a.Price),
                Quantity = e.Quantity,
                StockLeft = stock.GetValueOrDefault(item.Id)
            });
        }

        var perItem = vm.Lines.GroupBy(l => l.MenuItemId).ToDictionary(g => g.Key, g => g.Sum(l => l.Quantity));
        foreach (var line in vm.Lines)
            line.ExceedsStock = perItem[line.MenuItemId] > line.StockLeft;

        if (kept.Count != entries.Count) Save(kept);
        return vm;
    }
}

using System.Net;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;

namespace BrewStop.Services;

public interface IEmailService
{
    Task SendAsync(string to, string subject, string htmlBody);
}

// Dev only: prints the email to the console. Replace with an SMTP version later.
public class LoggingEmailService(ILogger<LoggingEmailService> logger) : IEmailService
{
    public Task SendAsync(string to, string subject, string htmlBody)
    {
        var match = Regex.Match(htmlBody, "href=\"([^\"]+)\"");
        var link = match.Success ? WebUtility.HtmlDecode(match.Groups[1].Value) : "(no link)";

        logger.LogWarning("\n===== EMAIL to {To} =====\nSubject: {Subject}\nLINK: {Link}\n=========================",
            to, subject, link);
        return Task.CompletedTask;
    }
}

FILE: BrewStop\Services\ImageResolver.cs
========================================
namespace BrewStop.Services;

public interface IImageResolver
{
    string Resolve(string? imageUrl, string? name = null);
}

public class ImageResolver(IHttpContextAccessor http) : IImageResolver
{
    private const string Placeholder = "images/placeholder.svg";

    private static string U(string id) =>
        $"https://images.unsplash.com/photo-{id}?auto=format&fit=crop&w=800&q=80";

    // Key = menu item / category name (case-insensitive)
    private static readonly Dictionary<string, string> Catalog = new(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
    {
        // Categories
        ["Coffee"] = U("1561882468-9110e03e0f78"),
        ["Pastries"] = U("1699028057561-770e52cb572f"),

        // Drinks
        ["Caramel Latte"] = U("1534687941688-651ccaafbff8"),
        ["Caramel Cloud Latte"] = U("1563090308-5a7889e40542"),
        ["Velvet Mocha Brew"] = U("1598831745385-0c404c7034a9"),
        ["Vanilla Silk Macchiato"] = U("1631292007327-70297e83d78b"),
        ["Midnight Cold Brew"] = U("1517701550927-30cf4ba1dba5"),
        ["Hazelnut Dream Cappuccino"] = U("1561882468-9110e03e0f78"),

        // Pastries
        ["Butter Croissant"] = U("1555507036-ab1f4038808a"),
        ["Strawberry Crème Éclair"] = U("1721324390033-661a350668d6"),
        ["Golden Honey Puff"] = U("1587912001191-0cd4f14fd89e"),
        ["Midnight Cocoa Twist"] = U("1718897266472-5b7229ebdd3d"),
        ["Roselle Berry Tart"] = U("1649019937955-9687640aaaab"),
        ["Celestia Almond Croissant"] = U("1705856987588-1e6ae0b6061f"),
    };

    private string PathBase => http.HttpContext?.Request.PathBase.Value ?? string.Empty;

    public string Resolve(string? imageUrl, string? name = null)
    {
        // 0) File uploaded by an admin
        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(imageUrl) &&
            imageUrl.StartsWith("/uploads/menu/", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            return $"{PathBase}{imageUrl}";

        // 1) Curated photo by item name
        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(name) && Catalog.TryGetValue(name.Trim(), out var stock))
            return stock;

        // 2) Absolute http(s) URL stored in DB
        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(imageUrl) &&
            Uri.TryCreate(imageUrl, UriKind.Absolute, out var abs) &&
            (abs.Scheme == Uri.UriSchemeHttp || abs.Scheme == Uri.UriSchemeHttps))
            return imageUrl;

        // 3) Placeholder
        return $"{PathBase}/{Placeholder}";
    }
}

FILE: BrewStop\Services\InventoryService.cs
========================================
using BrewStop.Data;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace BrewStop.Services;

public record StockResult(bool Ok, List<int> ShortItemIds, List<int> SoldOutItemIds);

public interface IInventoryService
{
    Task<Dictionary<int, int>> GetStockAsync(IEnumerable<int> menuItemIds, CancellationToken ct = default);

    /// <summary>
    /// Atomically deducts stock. Call inside the caller's transaction and roll back when Ok is false.
    /// </summary>
    Task<StockResult> DeductAsync(IReadOnlyDictionary<int, int> quantities, CancellationToken ct = default);
}

public class InventoryService(ApplicationDbContext db) : IInventoryService
{
    public async Task<Dictionary<int, int>> GetStockAsync(IEnumerable<int> menuItemIds, CancellationToken ct = default)
    {
        var ids = menuItemIds.Distinct().ToList();
        return await db.InventoryItems.AsNoTracking()
            .Where(i => ids.Contains(i.MenuItemId))
            .ToDictionaryAsync(i => i.MenuItemId, i => i.Quantity, ct);
    }

    public async Task<StockResult> DeductAsync(IReadOnlyDictionary<int, int> quantities, CancellationToken ct = default)
    {
        var shortIds = new List<int>();

        // Fixed lock order (by item id) prevents deadlocks between concurrent checkouts.
        foreach (var (itemId, qty) in quantities.OrderBy(k => k.Key))
        {
            // One conditional UPDATE: succeeds only if enough stock remains, so stock can never go negative
            // and two buyers can never both take the last item (SRS-NFR-09).
            var rows = await db.InventoryItems
                .Where(i => i.MenuItemId == itemId && i.Quantity >= qty)
                .ExecuteUpdateAsync(s => s.SetProperty(i => i.Quantity, i => i.Quantity - qty), ct);

            if (rows == 0)
            {
                shortIds.Add(itemId);
                return new StockResult(false, shortIds, new List<int>());
            }
        }

        var ids = quantities.Keys.ToList();
        var soldOut = await db.InventoryItems.AsNoTracking()
            .Where(i => ids.Contains(i.MenuItemId) && i.Quantity == 0)
            .Select(i => i.MenuItemId)
            .ToListAsync(ct);

        return new StockResult(true, shortIds, soldOut);
    }
}

FILE: BrewStop\Services\PickupSlotService.cs
========================================
using System.Globalization;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Options;

namespace BrewStop.Services;

public class ShopOptions
{
    public string OpenTime { get; set; } = "08:00";
    public string CloseTime { get; set; } = "17:00";
    public int SlotMinutes { get; set; } = 10;
    public int LeadMinutes { get; set; } = 10;
    public string TimeZone { get; set; } = "Asia/Manila";
}

/// <param name="Utc">Slot start in UTC (what gets stored).</param>
public record PickupSlot(DateTime Utc, string Label)
{
    public string Value => Utc.ToString("yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm'Z'", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
}

public interface IPickupSlotService
{
    IReadOnlyList<PickupSlot> GetAvailableSlots();
    bool IsValid(DateTime utc);
    DateTime ToLocal(DateTime utc);
}

public class PickupSlotService(IOptions<ShopOptions> options) : IPickupSlotService
{
    private readonly ShopOptions _o = options.Value;
    private readonly TimeZoneInfo _tz = ResolveZone(options.Value.TimeZone);

    private static TimeZoneInfo ResolveZone(string id)
    {
        try { return TimeZoneInfo.FindSystemTimeZoneById(id); }
        catch (TimeZoneNotFoundException) { return TimeZoneInfo.CreateCustomTimeZone("PHT", TimeSpan.FromHours(8), "PHT", "PHT"); }
    }

    public DateTime ToLocal(DateTime utc) =>
        TimeZoneInfo.ConvertTimeFromUtc(DateTime.SpecifyKind(utc, DateTimeKind.Utc), _tz);

    public IReadOnlyList<PickupSlot> GetAvailableSlots()
    {
        var step = Math.Max(1, _o.SlotMinutes);
        var nowLocal = ToLocal(DateTime.UtcNow);
        var today = nowLocal.Date;

        var open = today + TimeOnly.Parse(_o.OpenTime, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture).ToTimeSpan();
        var close = today + TimeOnly.Parse(_o.CloseTime, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture).ToTimeSpan();

        var earliest = nowLocal.AddMinutes(_o.LeadMinutes);
        var start = earliest > open ? earliest : open;

        var minutes = (int)Math.Ceiling((start - today).TotalMinutes / step) * step;
        var t = today.AddMinutes(minutes);

        var slots = new List<PickupSlot>();
        while (t < close)
        {
            var utc = TimeZoneInfo.ConvertTimeToUtc(DateTime.SpecifyKind(t, DateTimeKind.Unspecified), _tz);
            slots.Add(new PickupSlot(utc, t.ToString("h:mm tt", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)));
            t = t.AddMinutes(step);
        }
        return slots;
    }

    public bool IsValid(DateTime utc) => GetAvailableSlots().Any(s => s.Utc == utc);
}


========================================
FILE: BrewStop\Services\PromotionService.cs
========================================
using BrewStop.Data;
using BrewStop.Models;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace BrewStop.Services;

public record PromoResult(bool Ok, string? Error, string? Code, decimal Discount, int? PromotionId = null);

public interface IPromotionService
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Validates the code server-side, computes the discount and atomically increments TimesUsed.
    /// Call inside the caller's transaction and roll back if the order fails afterwards.
    /// A blank code returns Ok with a zero discount.
    /// </summary>
    Task<PromoResult> ApplyPromotionAsync(string? code, decimal subtotal, CancellationToken ct = default);
}

public class PromotionService(ApplicationDbContext db) : IPromotionService
{
    private static PromoResult Fail(string msg) => new(false, msg, null, 0m);

    public async Task<PromoResult> ApplyPromotionAsync(string? code, decimal subtotal, CancellationToken ct = default)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(code)) return new PromoResult(true, null, null, 0m);

        var normalized = code.Trim().ToUpperInvariant();
        var now = DateTime.UtcNow;

        var promo = await db.Promotions.AsNoTracking().FirstOrDefaultAsync(p => p.Code == normalized, ct);
        if (promo is null || !promo.IsActive) return Fail("Invalid promo code.");
        if (promo.ExpiryDate < now) return Fail("This promo code has expired.");
        if (promo.MaxUses is not null && promo.TimesUsed >= promo.MaxUses) return Fail("This promo code has reached its usage limit.");

        var discount = promo.DiscountType == DiscountType.Percentage
            ? Math.Round(subtotal * promo.DiscountValue / 100m, 2, MidpointRounding.AwayFromZero)
            : promo.DiscountValue;
        discount = Math.Min(discount, subtotal);

        // Conditional UPDATE: two buyers can never both take the last use.
        var rows = await db.Promotions
            .Where(p => p.Id == promo.Id && p.IsActive && p.ExpiryDate >= now
                        && (p.MaxUses == null || p.TimesUsed < p.MaxUses))
            .ExecuteUpdateAsync(s => s.SetProperty(p => p.TimesUsed, p => p.TimesUsed + 1), ct);

        return rows == 0 ? Fail("This promo code is no longer available.")
                         : new PromoResult(true, null, promo.Code, discount, promo.Id);
    }
}


========================================
FILE: BrewStop\Services\QrTokenService.cs
========================================
using System.Security.Cryptography;
using System.Text;
using BrewStop.Data;
using BrewStop.Models;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Options;

namespace BrewStop.Services;

public class QrOptions
{
    public string SigningKey { get; set; } = string.Empty;
}

public enum ScanResult
{
    Ok, Invalid, Expired, AlreadyUsed, Revoked, WrongStatus
}

public interface IQrTokenService
{
    Task<string> IssueAsync(Order order, CancellationToken ct = default);
    Task<(ScanResult Result, int? OrderId)> ClaimAsync(string token, string baristaId, CancellationToken ct = default);
    Task RevokeAsync(int orderId, CancellationToken ct = default);
    Task<string?> GetTokenAsync(int orderId, CancellationToken ct = default);
}

public class QrTokenService(ApplicationDbContext db, IOptions<QrOptions> opt, IAuditService audit) : IQrTokenService
{
    private byte[] Key => string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(opt.Value.SigningKey)
        ? throw new InvalidOperationException("Qr:SigningKey is not configured (use user-secrets / env var Qr__SigningKey).")
        : Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(opt.Value.SigningKey);

    private static string B64(byte[] b) => Convert.ToBase64String(b).TrimEnd('=').Replace('+', '-').Replace('/', '_');
    private byte[] Sign(string payload) => HMACSHA256.HashData(Key, Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(payload));

    public async Task<string> IssueAsync(Order order, CancellationToken ct = default)
    {
        var nonce = Convert.ToHexString(RandomNumberGenerator.GetBytes(16));
        var expiry = order.PickupAtUtc.AddMinutes(30);
        var payload = $"{order.Id}|{nonce}|{new DateTimeOffset(expiry, TimeSpan.Zero).ToUnixTimeSeconds()}";
        var sig = B64(Sign(payload));

        db.OrderQrTokens.Add(new OrderQrToken { OrderId = order.Id, Nonce = nonce, Signature = sig, ExpiresAtUtc = expiry });
        await db.SaveChangesAsync(ct);
        return $"{B64(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(payload))}.{sig}";
    }

    public async Task<(ScanResult, int?)> ClaimAsync(string token, string baristaId, CancellationToken ct = default)
    {
        async Task<(ScanResult, int?)> Fail(ScanResult r, int? orderId, string why)
        {
            await audit.LogAsync(baristaId, "QrScanRejected", $"OrderId={orderId}; Reason={why}", ct);
            return (r, orderId);
        }

        var parts = token.Split('.');
        if (parts.Length != 2) return await Fail(ScanResult.Invalid, null, "Format");

        string payload;
        try
        {
            var p = parts[0].Replace('-', '+').Replace('_', '/');
            payload = Encoding.UTF8.GetString(Convert.FromBase64String(p.PadRight(p.Length + (4 - p.Length % 4) % 4, '=')));
        }
        catch { return await Fail(ScanResult.Invalid, null, "Encoding"); }

        var expected = Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(B64(Sign(payload)));
        if (!CryptographicOperations.FixedTimeEquals(expected, Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(parts[1])))
            return await Fail(ScanResult.Invalid, null, "Signature");

        var f = payload.Split('|');
        if (f.Length != 3 || !int.TryParse(f[0], out var orderId) || !long.TryParse(f[2], out var exp))
            return await Fail(ScanResult.Invalid, null, "Payload");

        if (DateTimeOffset.UtcNow.ToUnixTimeSeconds() > exp) return await Fail(ScanResult.Expired, orderId, "Expired");

        var t = await db.OrderQrTokens.AsNoTracking().Include(x => x.Order)
            .FirstOrDefaultAsync(x => x.OrderId == orderId && x.Nonce == f[1], ct);
        if (t is null) return await Fail(ScanResult.Invalid, orderId, "Nonce");
        if (t.IsRevoked) return await Fail(ScanResult.Revoked, orderId, "Revoked");
        if (t.IsUsed) return await Fail(ScanResult.AlreadyUsed, orderId, "AlreadyUsed");
        if (t.Order.Status != OrderStatus.Ready) return await Fail(ScanResult.WrongStatus, orderId, "Status=" + t.Order.Status);

        await using var tx = await db.Database.BeginTransactionAsync(ct);

        var rows = await db.OrderQrTokens
            .Where(x => x.Id == t.Id && !x.IsUsed && !x.IsRevoked)
            .ExecuteUpdateAsync(s => s
                .SetProperty(x => x.IsUsed, true)
                .SetProperty(x => x.ClaimedAtUtc, DateTime.UtcNow)
                .SetProperty(x => x.ClaimedBy, baristaId), ct);

        if (rows == 0) { await tx.RollbackAsync(ct); return await Fail(ScanResult.AlreadyUsed, orderId, "Race"); }

        await db.Orders.Where(o => o.Id == orderId)
            .ExecuteUpdateAsync(s => s
                .SetProperty(o => o.Status, OrderStatus.Claimed)
                .SetProperty(o => o.PaymentStatus, PaymentStatus.Paid)
                .SetProperty(o => o.PaidAtUtc, o => o.PaidAtUtc ?? DateTime.UtcNow), ct);

        audit.Add(baristaId, "QrScanAccepted", $"OrderId={orderId}");
        audit.Add(baristaId, "OrderStatusChanged", $"OrderId={orderId}; Ready->Claimed");
        await db.SaveChangesAsync(ct);
        await tx.CommitAsync(ct);
        return (ScanResult.Ok, orderId);
    }

    public Task RevokeAsync(int orderId, CancellationToken ct = default) =>
        db.OrderQrTokens.Where(x => x.OrderId == orderId)
            .ExecuteUpdateAsync(s => s.SetProperty(x => x.IsRevoked, true), ct);

    public async Task<string?> GetTokenAsync(int orderId, CancellationToken ct = default)
    {
        var t = await db.OrderQrTokens.AsNoTracking()
            .FirstOrDefaultAsync(x => x.OrderId == orderId && !x.IsRevoked && !x.IsUsed, ct);
        if (t is null) return null;

        var exp = new DateTimeOffset(DateTime.SpecifyKind(t.ExpiresAtUtc, DateTimeKind.Utc)).ToUnixTimeSeconds();
        var payload = $"{orderId}|{t.Nonce}|{exp}";
        return $"{B64(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(payload))}.{B64(Sign(payload))}";
    }
}

@model ActivityViewModel
@{
    Layout = "_Layout";
    ViewData["Title"] = "Account Activity";

    var inv = System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture;

    Dictionary<string, string> Parse(string? details)
    {
        var map = new Dictionary<string, string>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(details)) return map;
        foreach (var part in details.Split(';', StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries | StringSplitOptions.TrimEntries))
        {
            var i = part.IndexOf('=');
            if (i > 0) map[part[..i].Trim()] = part[(i + 1)..].Trim();
        }
        return map;
    }

    string Peso(string? raw) =>
        decimal.TryParse(raw, System.Globalization.NumberStyles.Number, inv, out var amt)
            ? "â‚±" + amt.ToString("N2", inv)
            : "â‚±0.00";

    (string Title, string Text, string Cat, string Icon, bool Warn) Describe(AuditLog a)
    {
        var d = Parse(a.Details);
        string V(string k) => d.TryGetValue(k, out var v) ? v : "";
        var order = V("OrderId");
        var via = V("Payment").Length > 0 ? $" via {V("Payment")}" : "";
        var status = V("Status").Length > 0 ? $" (Status: {V("Status")})" : "";

        return a.Action switch
        {
            "OrderPlaced" => ("Order placed", $"Order #{order} placed â€¢ {Peso(V("Total"))}{via}{status}", "orders", "fa-solid fa-bag-shopping", false),
            "OrderCancelled" => ("Order cancelled", $"Order #{order} was cancelled", "orders", "fa-solid fa-ban", true),
            "PromoApplied" => ("Promo code applied", $"Promo code {V("Code")} applied to Order #{order} ({Peso(V("Discount"))} off)", "promos", "fa-solid fa-ticket", false),
            "ReviewCreated" => ("Review submitted", $"Submitted a {V("Rating")}-star review for Order #{order}", "promos", "fa-solid fa-star", false),
            "ProfileUpdated" => ("Profile updated", "Updated account profile details", "account", "fa-solid fa-user-pen", false),
            "PasswordChanged" => ("Password changed", "Changed your account password", "account", "fa-solid fa-key", false),
            "PasswordSet" => ("Password set", "Set a password so you can also sign in with your email", "account", "fa-solid fa-key", false),
            "SignedOutAllDevices" => ("Signed out everywhere", "Signed out of all devices", "account", "fa-solid fa-laptop", false),
            "NotificationsUpdated" => ("Notifications updated", "Updated your notification preferences", "account", "fa-solid fa-bell", false),
            "ExternalLogin" => ("Signed in with Google", "Signed in using your Google account", "account", "fa-brands fa-google", false),
            "ExternalLoginLinked" => ("Google account linked", "Linked your Google account to BrewStop", "account", "fa-brands fa-google", false),
            "UserRegistered" => ("Account created", V("Provider").Length > 0 ? $"Created your BrewStop account with {V("Provider")}" : "Created your BrewStop account", "account", "fa-solid fa-user-plus", false),
            "GoogleOAuthRegistered" => ("Registration completed", "Completed registration by adding your Campus ID", "account", "fa-solid fa-id-card", false),
            "AccountDeleted" => ("Account deleted", "Your account was deleted and personal data erased", "account", "fa-solid fa-user-slash", true),
            _ => (a.Action, "", "account", "fa-solid fa-circle-info", false)
        };
    }

    string CatLabel(string c) => c switch { "orders" => "Orders", "promos" => "Promos & Reviews", _ => "Account & Security" };
    string CatBadge(string c) => c switch
    {
        "orders" => "bg-[#e8f3ee] text-[#1e3932]",
        "promos" => "bg-amber-50 text-amber-700",
        _ => "bg-gray-100 text-gray-600"
    };
}

<div class="tw">
    <div class="mx-auto max-w-6xl px-4 pt-24 pb-16">
        <h1 class="font-serif text-3xl font-bold text-cream">My Account</h1>
        <p class="mt-1 text-sm text-neutral-400">Account settings</p>

        <div class="mt-8 grid gap-8 lg:grid-cols-3">
            <aside class="lg:col-span-1">
                <partial name="_AccountSettingsNav" />
            </aside>

            <section class="rounded-3xl bg-white p-6 shadow-sm sm:p-10 lg:col-span-2">
                <h2 class="font-serif text-2xl font-semibold text-[#1e3932]">Account Activity</h2>
                <p class="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-500">Your most recent account activity (up to 50 events, UTC).</p>

                @if (Model.Entries.Count == 0)
                {
                    <p class="mt-6 rounded-xl bg-[#f9f9f9] px-4 py-6 text-center text-sm text-gray-500">No activity recorded yet.</p>
                }
                else
                {
                    <div class="mt-5 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
                        <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
                            <label for="activity-filter" class="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-gray-500">Filter</label>
                            <select id="activity-filter" aria-label="Filter activity by category"
                                    class="rounded-xl border border-solid border-gray-300 bg-white py-2 pl-3 pr-8 text-sm text-[#1e3932] shadow-sm focus:border-[#00754a] focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#00754a]/25">
                                <option value="all">All Activity</option>
                                <option value="orders">Orders</option>
                                <option value="promos">Promos &amp; Reviews</option>
                                <option value="account">Account &amp; Security</option>
                            </select>
                        </div>
                        <p id="activity-count" class="text-xs text-gray-400" aria-live="polite"></p>
                    </div>

                    <div id="activity-feed" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="Account activity feed"
                         class="activity-scroll mt-3 max-h-[420px] overflow-y-auto rounded-2xl border border-solid border-gray-200 bg-[#f9f9f9] [scrollbar-width:thin] [scrollbar-color:#b7d4c6_transparent]">
                        <ul>
                            @foreach (var a in Model.Entries)
                            {
                                var (title, text, cat, icon, warn) = Describe(a);
                                <li data-category="@cat" class="border-t border-solid border-gray-200 bg-white first:border-t-0">
                                    <div class="flex items-start gap-3 px-4 py-3.5">
                                        <span class="mt-0.5 flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full @(warn ? "bg-red-50 text-[#d62b1f]" : "bg-[#e8f3ee] text-[#00754a]")">
                                            <i class="@icon text-sm" aria-hidden="true"></i>
                                        </span>
                                        <div class="min-w-0 flex-1">
                                            <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-2 gap-y-1">
                                                <p class="text-sm font-semibold text-[#1e3932]">@title</p>
                                                <span class="rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide @CatBadge(cat)">@CatLabel(cat)</span>
                                            </div>
                                            @if (text.Length > 0)
                                            {
                                                <p class="mt-0.5 break-words text-sm text-gray-600">@text</p>
                                            }
                                            <time datetime="@a.CreatedAtUtc.ToString("o")" class="mt-1 block text-xs text-gray-400">
                                                @a.CreatedAtUtc.ToString("MMM d, yyyy h:mm tt") UTC
                                            </time>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                </li>
                            }
                        </ul>
                        <p id="activity-empty" class="hidden px-4 py-8 text-center text-sm text-gray-500">No activity in this category.</p>
                    </div>
                }
            </section>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>

@section Scripts {
    <style>
        .activity-scroll::-webkit-scrollbar {
            width: 8px;
        }

        .activity-scroll::-webkit-scrollbar-track {
            background: transparent;
        }

        .activity-scroll::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb {
            background: #b7d4c6;
            border-radius: 9999px;
        }

            .activity-scroll::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb:hover {
                background: #00754a;
            }
    </style>
    <script>
        (function() {
            var sel = document.getElementById('activity-filter');
            if (!sel) return;
            var feed = document.getElementById('activity-feed');
            var empty = document.getElementById('activity-empty');
            var count = document.getElementById('activity-count');
            var items = [].slice.call(feed.querySelectorAll('li[data-category]'));

            function apply() {
                var v = sel.value, n = 0;
                items.forEach(function(li) {
                    var show = v === 'all' || li.getAttribute('data-category') === v;
                    li.classList.toggle('hidden', !show);
                    if (show) n++;
                });
                empty.classList.toggle('hidden', n !== 0);
                count.textContent = 'Showing ' + n + ' of ' + items.length;
                feed.scrollTop = 0;
            }

            sel.addEventListener('change', apply);
            apply();
        })();
    </script>
}

<div class="tw">
    <div class="mx-auto max-w-6xl px-4 pt-24 pb-16">

                <h2 class="font-serif text-2xl font-semibold text-[#1e3932]">@(Model.HasPassword ? "Change Password" : "Set a Password")</h2>
                <p class="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-500">
                    @(Model.HasPassword
                        ? "Changing your password signs you out of all other devices."
                        : "You signed in with Google. Set a password to also sign in with your email.")
                </p>

                @if (TempData["Success"] is string ok)
                {
                    <p class="mt-5 rounded-xl border border-[#00754a]/20 bg-[#e8f3ee] px-4 py-3 text-sm text-[#1e3932]" role="status">@ok</p>
                }

                <form asp-action="ChangePassword" method="post" class="mt-6 space-y-6">
                    <div asp-validation-summary="ModelOnly" class="text-sm text-[#d62b1f]"></div>

                    @if (Model.HasPassword)
                    {
                        <div>
                            <label asp-for="CurrentPassword" class="@label"></label>
                            <div class="relative">
                                <input asp-for="CurrentPassword" autocomplete="current-password" class="@field" />
                                <button type="button" data-toggle-password aria-label="Show password" aria-pressed="false" class="@eyeBtn">
                                    <i class="fa-solid fa-eye" aria-hidden="true"></i>
                                </button>
                            </div>
                            <span asp-validation-for="CurrentPassword" class="@err"></span>
                        </div>
                    }
                    <div>
                        <label asp-for="NewPassword" class="@label"></label>
                        <div class="relative">
                            <input asp-for="NewPassword" autocomplete="new-password" class="@field" />
                            <button type="button" data-toggle-password aria-label="Show password" aria-pressed="false" class="@eyeBtn">
                                <i class="fa-solid fa-eye" aria-hidden="true"></i>
                            </button>
                        </div>
                        <span asp-validation-for="NewPassword" class="@err"></span>
                        <p class="mt-1 text-xs text-gray-400">At least 8 characters with an uppercase letter, a lowercase letter and a number.</p>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <label asp-for="ConfirmPassword" class="@label"></label>
                        <div class="relative">
                            <input asp-for="ConfirmPassword" autocomplete="new-password" class="@field" />
                            <button type="button" data-toggle-password aria-label="Show password" aria-pressed="false" class="@eyeBtn">
                                <i class="fa-solid fa-eye" aria-hidden="true"></i>
                            </button>
                        </div>
                        <span asp-validation-for="ConfirmPassword" class="@err"></span>
                    </div>

                    <button type="submit" class="@btn">@(Model.HasPassword ? "Update password" : "Set password")</button>
                </form>
            </section>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>

@section Scripts {
    <partial name="_ValidationScriptsPartial" />
    <style>
        /* hide Edge/IE native reveal icon so only the BrewStop toggle shows */
        input[type="password"]::-ms-reveal,
        input[type="password"]::-ms-clear {
            display: none;
        }
    </style>
    <script>
        document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
            var btn = e.target.closest('[data-toggle-password]');
            if (!btn) return;
            var input = btn.parentElement.querySelector('input');
            var icon = btn.querySelector('i');
            if (!input) return;
            var show = input.type === 'password';
            input.type = show ? 'text' : 'password';
            btn.setAttribute('aria-pressed', show ? 'true' : 'false');
            btn.setAttribute('aria-label', show ? 'Hide password' : 'Show password');
            if (icon) icon.className = show ? 'fa-solid fa-eye-slash' : 'fa-solid fa-eye';
            input.focus();
        });
    </script>
}

FILE: BrewStop\Views\Account\_AccountSettingsNav.cshtml

@{
    var current = (ViewContext.RouteData.Values["action"]?.ToString() ?? string.Empty);
    var items = new (string Action, string Label, string Icon)[]
    {
        ("Manage", "Personal Information", "fa-user"),
        ("ChangePassword", "Change Password", "fa-key"),
        ("Security", "Security Settings", "fa-shield-halved"),
        ("Notifications", "Notification Settings", "fa-bell"),
        ("Activity", "Account Activity", "fa-clock-rotate-left")
    };
    var deleteActive = string.Equals(current, "DeleteAccount", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
}

<nav aria-label="Account settings" class="rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 bg-[#f9f9f9] p-5">
    <p class="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-[#00754a]">BrewStop â€” Account Settings</p>
    <p class="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-500">Customer Profile â€” Manage your personal account</p>

    <ul class="mt-5 space-y-1">
        @foreach (var (action, label, icon) in items)
        {
            var active = string.Equals(current, action, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
            <li>
                <a asp-controller="Account" asp-action="@action"
                   aria-current="@(active ? "page" : null)"
                   class="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 text-sm transition @(active ? "bg-[#e8f3ee] font-semibold text-[#1e3932]" : "text-gray-600 hover:bg-white hover:text-[#1e3932]")">
                    <i class="fa-solid @icon w-4 text-center"></i>
                    <span class="flex-1">@label</span>
                    @if (active)
                    {
                        <span class="rounded-full bg-[#00754a] px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-white">Active</span>
                    }
                </a>
            </li>
        }

        <li>
            <form asp-controller="Account" asp-action="Logout" method="post">
                <button type="submit"
                        class="flex w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 text-left text-sm text-gray-600 transition hover:bg-white hover:text-[#1e3932]">
                    <i class="fa-solid fa-right-from-bracket w-4 text-center"></i>
                    <span class="flex-1">Logout</span>
                </button>
            </form>
        </li>

        <li class="mt-2 border-t border-gray-200 pt-3">
            <a asp-controller="Account" asp-action="DeleteAccount"
               aria-current="@(deleteActive ? "page" : null)"
               class="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium text-[#d62b1f] transition hover:bg-red-50 @(deleteActive ? "bg-red-50" : "")">
                <i class="fa-solid fa-trash-can w-4 text-center"></i>
                <span class="flex-1">Delete Account</span>
                <span class="rounded-full bg-red-100 px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-[#d62b1f]">Danger</span>
            </a>
        </li>
