            </form>

            <form method="post" action="/Admin/ToggleUserLock"
                  onsubmit="return confirm('@(Model.IsLocked ? "Unlock" : "Lock") this account?');">
                @Html.AntiForgeryToken()
                <input type="hidden" name="id" value="@Model.User.Id" />
                <button type="submit"
                        class="rounded-lg px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold @(Model.IsLocked ? "bg-green-500/20 text-green-300 hover:bg-green-500/30" : "border border-red-500/40 text-red-300 hover:bg-red-500/10")">
                    @(Model.IsLocked ? "Unlock Account" : "Lock Account")
                </button>
            </form>
        </div>

        <h2 class="mb-2 mt-6 font-serif text-lg font-bold">Order <span class="text-caramel">History</span></h2>
        <div class="overflow-x-auto rounded-xl border border-white/10 bg-card">
            <table class="min-w-full text-left text-sm">
                <thead class="bg-black/30 text-xs uppercase tracking-wider text-caramel">
                    <tr>
                        <th class="px-4 py-3">Order</th>
                        <th class="px-4 py-3">Placed</th>
                        <th class="px-4 py-3">Pickup</th>
                        <th class="px-4 py-3">Total</th>
                        <th class="px-4 py-3">Status</th>
                        <th class="px-4 py-3">Payment</th>
                        <th class="px-4 py-3"></th>
                    </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody class="divide-y divide-white/5">
                    @foreach (var o in Model.Orders)
                    {
                        <tr>
                            <td class="px-4 py-3">#@o.Id</td>
                            <td class="px-4 py-3">@o.CreatedAtUtc.ToLocalTime().ToString("MMM d, h:mm tt")</td>
                            <td class="px-4 py-3">@o.PickupAtUtc.ToLocalTime().ToString("MMM d, h:mm tt")</td>
                            <td class="px-4 py-3">?@o.Total.ToString("N2")</td>
                            <td class="px-4 py-3">@o.Status</td>
                            <td class="px-4 py-3">@o.PaymentStatus (@o.PaymentMethod)</td>
                            <td class="px-4 py-3 text-right"><a href="/Admin/OrderDetails/@o.Id" class="text-caramel no-underline hover:underline">View</a></td>
                        </tr>
                    }
                    @if (Model.Orders.Count == 0)
                    {
                        <tr><td colspan="7" class="px-4 py-8 text-center text-neutral-400">No orders yet.</td></tr>
                    }
                </tbody>
            </table>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>

FILE: BrewStop\Views\Admin\Customers.cshtml

@model AdminCustomersViewModel
@{
    Layout = "_AdminLayout";
    ViewData["Title"] = "Customer Management";

    // ---- View-only helpers (no controller / model changes) ----------------------------------
    // Tab bucket: Admin > Barista > Student. Faculty and role-less accounts are customers,
    // so they are grouped under the "Student" tab.
    bool InRole(AdminCustomerRow row, string role) =>
        row.Roles.Any(x => string.Equals(x, role, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));

    string Bucket(AdminCustomerRow row) =>
        InRole(row, "Admin") ? "admin" : InRole(row, "Barista") ? "barista" : "student";

    // Left-border accent: Inactive(locked)=crimson, Admin=amber, Barista=cyan, Active=green.
    string Accent(AdminCustomerRow row) =>
        row.IsLocked ? "locked" : InRole(row, "Admin") ? "admin" : InRole(row, "Barista") ? "barista" : "active";

    string RolePillClass(string role) =>
        string.Equals(role, "Admin", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ? "cm-role-admin"
        : string.Equals(role, "Barista", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ? "cm-role-barista"
        : "cm-role-customer";

    var totalCount = Model.Rows.Count;
    var studentCount = Model.Rows.Count(r => Bucket(r) == "student");
    var baristaCount = Model.Rows.Count(r => Bucket(r) == "barista");
    var adminCount = Model.Rows.Count(r => Bucket(r) == "admin");
    var rowIndex = 0;
}

            <span id="cmEmptyText">No users found.</span>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>

@section Scripts {
    <script>
        (function() {
            var tabsEl = document.getElementById('cmTabs');
            var pill = document.getElementById('cmPill');
            var meta = document.getElementById('cmMeta');
            var empty = document.getElementById('cmEmpty');
            var emptyText = document.getElementById('cmEmptyText');
            if (!tabsEl || !pill) return;

            var tabs = Array.prototype.slice.call(tabsEl.querySelectorAll('.cm-tab'));
            var rows = Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('#cmRows .cm-row'));
            var current = 'all';

            var labels = { all: 'All', student: 'Student', barista: 'Barista', admin: 'Admin' };

            // Live counts straight from the rendered rows
            function updateCounts() {
                var c = { all: rows.length, student: 0, barista: 0, admin: 0 };
                rows.forEach(function(r) { var k = r.getAttribute('data-role'); if (c[k] !== undefined) c[k]++; });
                Object.keys(c).forEach(function(k) {
                    var el = tabsEl.querySelector('[data-count="' + k + '"]');
                    if (el) el.textContent = c[k];
                });
                return c;
            }

            // Sliding active pill
            function movePill(btn, animate) {
                if (!btn) return;
                if (animate) pill.classList.add('cm-animate');
                pill.style.width = btn.offsetWidth + 'px';
                pill.style.transform = 'translateX(' + btn.offsetLeft + 'px)';
            }

            function applyFilter(filter) {
                current = filter;
                var shown = 0;
                rows.forEach(function(r) {
                    var match = filter === 'all' || r.getAttribute('data-role') === filter;
                    r.hidden = !match;
                    if (match) {
                        r.style.setProperty('--i', Math.min(shown, 12));
                        r.classList.remove('cm-enter');
                        void r.offsetWidth; // restart staggered entrance
                        r.classList.add('cm-enter');
                        shown++;
                    }
                });

                if (meta) meta.innerHTML = 'Showing <strong>' + shown + '</strong> of ' + rows.length + ' accounts';

                if (empty) {
                    var none = shown === 0;
                    empty.hidden = !none;
                    if (none && emptyText) {
                        emptyText.textContent = rows.length === 0
                            ? 'No users found.'
                            : 'No ' + labels[filter].toLowerCase() + ' accounts in these results.';
                    }
                }
            }

            function select(btn, focus) {
                tabs.forEach(function(t) {
                    var on = t === btn;
                    t.setAttribute('aria-selected', on ? 'true' : 'false');
                    t.setAttribute('tabindex', on ? '0' : '-1');
                });
                movePill(btn, true);
                applyFilter(btn.getAttribute('data-filter'));
                if (focus) btn.focus();
            }

            tabs.forEach(function(t, i) {
                t.addEventListener('click', function() { select(t, false); });
                t.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
                    var next = null;
                    if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') next = tabs[(i + 1) % tabs.length];
                    else if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') next = tabs[(i - 1 + tabs.length) % tabs.length];
                    else if (e.key === 'Home') next = tabs[0];
                    else if (e.key === 'End') next = tabs[tabs.length - 1];
                    if (next) { e.preventDefault(); select(next, true); }
                });
            });

            function activeTab() {
                return tabs.filter(function(t) { return t.getAttribute('aria-selected') === 'true'; })[0] || tabs[0];
            }

            updateCounts();
            movePill(activeTab(), false);          // initial placement without animation
            window.requestAnimationFrame(function() { pill.classList.add('cm-animate'); });

            // Keep the pill aligned if layout / fonts / viewport change
            window.addEventListener('resize', function() { pill.classList.remove('cm-animate'); movePill(activeTab(), false); });
            if (document.fonts && document.fonts.ready) {
                document.fonts.ready.then(function() { pill.classList.remove('cm-animate'); movePill(activeTab(), false); });
            }
            if (window.ResizeObserver) {
                new ResizeObserver(function() { movePill(activeTab(), false); }).observe(tabsEl);
            }
        })();
    </script>
}

    const string card = "rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-card p-5";
    const string h2 = "font-serif text-lg font-semibold text-cream";
    const string field = "rounded-lg border border-white/15 bg-transparent px-3 py-2 text-sm text-cream focus:border-caramel focus:outline-none [color-scheme:dark]";
}

@functions {
    static string StatusCls(OrderStatus s) => s switch
    {
        OrderStatus.Placed => "bg-sky-500/15 text-sky-300",
        OrderStatus.Preparing => "bg-amber-500/15 text-amber-300",
        OrderStatus.Ready => "bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-300",
        OrderStatus.Claimed => "bg-white/10 text-neutral-300",
        OrderStatus.Cancelled => "bg-red-500/15 text-red-300",
        _ => "bg-white/10 text-neutral-300"
    };

    static string PayCls(PaymentStatus s) => s switch
    {
        PaymentStatus.Paid => "bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-300",
        PaymentStatus.Refunded => "bg-white/10 text-neutral-300",
        _ => "bg-amber-500/15 text-amber-300"
    };
}

                            </td>

                            <td class="whitespace-nowrap py-3 text-xs text-neutral-400">@o.CreatedAt.ToString("MMM d · h:mm tt")</td>

@section Scripts {
    <script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/Chart.js/4.4.1/chart.umd.min.js"></script>
    <script>
        (function() {
            if (!window.Chart) return;

            var CARAMEL = '#d4a373', GRID = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.08)';
            Chart.defaults.color = '#d4d4d4';
            Chart.defaults.font.family = 'inherit';

            var labels = @Html.Raw(Json.Serialize(Model.DailyLabels));
            var revenue = @Html.Raw(Json.Serialize(Model.DailyRevenueData));
            var orders = @Html.Raw(Json.Serialize(Model.DailyOrderCountData));

            var payLabels = @Html.Raw(Json.Serialize(Model.PaymentMethodLabels));
            var payCounts = @Html.Raw(Json.Serialize(Model.PaymentMethodCounts));
            var stLabels = @Html.Raw(Json.Serialize(Model.OrderStatusLabels));
            var stCounts = @Html.Raw(Json.Serialize(Model.OrderStatusCounts));

            function peso(v) {
                return '\u20B1' + Number(v).toLocaleString('en-PH', { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 });
            }

            // ---- Trend line chart with Revenue / Orders toggle ----
            var metric = 'revenue';
            var trend = new Chart(document.getElementById('trendChart'), {
                type: 'line',
                data: {
                    labels: labels,
                    datasets: [{
                        label: 'Revenue (\u20B1)',
                        data: revenue,
                        borderColor: CARAMEL,
                        backgroundColor: 'rgba(212,163,115,0.15)',
                        fill: true,
                        tension: 0.35,
                        borderWidth: 2,
                        pointRadius: labels.length > 31 ? 0 : 3,
                        pointHoverRadius: 5
                    }]
                },
                options: {
                    maintainAspectRatio: false,
                    interaction: { mode: 'index', intersect: false },
                    plugins: {
                        legend: { display: false },
                        tooltip: {
                            callbacks: {
                                label: function(c) {
                                    return metric === 'revenue' ? ' ' + peso(c.parsed.y) : ' ' + c.parsed.y + ' order' + (c.parsed.y === 1 ? '' : 's');
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    },
                    scales: {
                        x: { grid: { display: false }, ticks: { maxTicksLimit: 10 } },
                        y: {
                            beginAtZero: true,
                            grid: { color: GRID },
                            ticks: {
                                precision: 0,
                                callback: function(v) { return metric === 'revenue' ? '\u20B1' + v : v; }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            });

            function setMetric(m) {
                metric = m;
                var ds = trend.data.datasets[0];
                ds.data = m === 'revenue' ? revenue : orders;
                ds.label = m === 'revenue' ? 'Revenue (\u20B1)' : 'Orders';
                trend.update();
                document.querySelectorAll('[data-metric]').forEach(function(b) {
                    b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', b.dataset.metric === m ? 'true' : 'false');
                });
            }
            document.querySelectorAll('[data-metric]').forEach(function(b) {
                b.addEventListener('click', function() { setMetric(b.dataset.metric); });
            });

            // ---- Donut with Payment / Status toggle ----
            var PAY_COLORS = ['#d4a373', '#38bdf8'];
            var ST_COLORS = { Placed: '#38bdf8', Preparing: '#fbbf24', Ready: '#34d399', Claimed: '#a8a29e', Cancelled: '#f87171' };
            var mix = new Chart(document.getElementById('mixChart'), {
                type: 'doughnut',
                data: {
                    labels: payLabels,
                    datasets: [{ data: payCounts, backgroundColor: PAY_COLORS, borderColor: '#241411', borderWidth: 2 }]
                },
                options: {
                    maintainAspectRatio: false,
                    cutout: '62%',
                    plugins: {
                        legend: { position: 'bottom', labels: { boxWidth: 12, padding: 14 } },
                        tooltip: {
                            callbacks: {
                                label: function(c) {
                                    var total = c.dataset.data.reduce(function(a, b) { return a + b; }, 0);
                                    var pct = total ? Math.round(c.parsed * 100 / total) : 0;
                                    return ' ' + c.label + ': ' + c.parsed + ' (' + pct + '%)';
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            });

            function setMix(kind) {
                var ds = mix.data.datasets[0];
                if (kind === 'payment') {
                    mix.data.labels = payLabels; ds.data = payCounts; ds.backgroundColor = PAY_COLORS;
                } else {
                    mix.data.labels = stLabels; ds.data = stCounts;
                    ds.backgroundColor = stLabels.map(function(l) { return ST_COLORS[l] || '#a8a29e'; });
                }
                var total = ds.data.reduce(function(a, b) { return a + b; }, 0);
                document.getElementById('mixEmpty').classList.toggle('hidden', total > 0);
                mix.update();
                document.querySelectorAll('[data-mix]').forEach(function(b) {
                    b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', b.dataset.mix === kind ? 'true' : 'false');
                });
            }
            document.querySelectorAll('[data-mix]').forEach(function(b) {
                b.addEventListener('click', function() { setMix(b.dataset.mix); });
            });
        })();
    </script>
}

}

<div class="tw">
    <div class="mx-auto max-w-4xl">
        <div class="mb-5 flex items-center justify-between">
            <h1 class="font-serif text-2xl font-bold">Order <span class="text-caramel">#@Model.Id</span></h1>

                <i class="fa-solid fa-arrow-left mr-1"></i> Back to Orders
            </a>
        </div>

        @if (TempData["Success"] is string ok)
        {
            <div class="mb-3 rounded-lg border border-green-500/30 bg-green-500/10 p-3 text-green-300">@ok</div>
        }
        @if (TempData["Error"] is string err)
        {
            <div class="mb-3 rounded-lg border border-red-500/30 bg-red-500/10 p-3 text-red-300">@err</div>
        }

        <div class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 rounded-xl border border-white/10 bg-card p-5 sm:grid-cols-2">
            <div><div class="text-xs uppercase text-neutral-400">Customer</div>@Model.User.FullName</div>
            <div><div class="text-xs uppercase text-neutral-400">Campus ID</div>@Model.User.CampusId</div>
            <div><div class="text-xs uppercase text-neutral-400">Pickup Slot</div>@Model.PickupAtUtc.ToLocalTime().ToString("ddd, MMM d · h:mm tt")</div>
            <div><div class="text-xs uppercase text-neutral-400">Status</div><span class="font-semibold text-caramel">@Model.Status</span></div>
            <div class="sm:col-span-2"><div class="text-xs uppercase text-neutral-400">Order Note</div>@(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Model.Note) ? "—" : Model.Note)</div>
        </div>

        <div class="mt-4 overflow-x-auto rounded-xl border border-white/10 bg-card">
            <table class="min-w-full text-left text-sm">
                <thead class="bg-black/30 text-xs uppercase tracking-wider text-caramel">
                    <tr>
                        <th class="px-4 py-3">Item</th>
                        <th class="px-4 py-3">Size</th>
                        <th class="px-4 py-3">Add-ons</th>
                        <th class="px-4 py-3">Unit Price</th>
                        <th class="px-4 py-3">Qty</th>
                    </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody class="divide-y divide-white/5">
                    @foreach (var i in Model.Items)
                    {
                        <tr>
                            <td class="px-4 py-3">@i.ItemName</td>
                            <td class="px-4 py-3">@(i.SizeName ?? "—")</td>
                            <td class="px-4 py-3">
                                @if (i.AddOns.Count == 0)
                                {
                                    <span>—</span>
                                }
                                else
                                {

                                    @string.Join(", ", i.AddOns.Select(a => $"{a.Name} (+?{a.Price:N2})"))
                                }
                            </td>
                            <td class="px-4 py-3">?@i.UnitPrice.ToString("N2")</td>
                            <td class="px-4 py-3">@i.Quantity</td>
                        </tr>
                    }
                </tbody>
                <tfoot>
                    <tr class="border-t border-white/10">
                        <td colspan="4" class="px-4 py-3 text-right font-semibold">Total</td>
                        <td class="px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-caramel">?@Model.Total.ToString("N2")</td>
                    </tr>
                </tfoot>
            </table>
        </div>

        @if (next.Length > 0)
        {
            <div class="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-3">
                @foreach (var s in next)
                {
                    <form method="post" action="/Admin/UpdateOrderStatus"
                          onsubmit="@(s == OrderStatus.Cancelled ? "return confirm('Cancel this order? Stock will be restored.');" : "return true;")">
                        @Html.AntiForgeryToken()
                        <input type="hidden" name="id" value="@Model.Id" />
                        <input type="hidden" name="status" value="@s" />
                        <button type="submit"
                                class="rounded-lg px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold @(s == OrderStatus.Cancelled ? "border border-red-500/40 text-red-300 hover:bg-red-500/10" : "bg-caramel text-espresso hover:opacity-90")">
                            @(s == OrderStatus.Cancelled ? "Cancel Order" : $"Mark as {s}")
                        </button>
                    </form>
                }
            </div>
        }
    </div>
</div>

                </tr>

                            <span class="rounded-full px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold @(pending ? "bg-amber-500/15 text-amber-300" : o.Status == OrderStatus.Cancelled ? "bg-red-500/15 text-red-300" : "bg-green-500/15 text-green-300")">@o.Status</span>
                        </td>
                        <td class="px-4 py-3 text-right">
                            <a href="/Admin/OrderDetails/@o.Id" class="text-caramel no-underline hover:underline">View</a>
                        </td>

<div class="tw">
    <div class="pay">
        <h1 class="mb-5 font-serif text-2xl font-bold">Payment <span class="text-caramel">Management</span></h1>

        @if (TempData["Success"] is string ok)
        {
            <div class="mb-3 rounded-lg border border-green-500/30 bg-green-500/10 p-3 text-green-300">@ok</div>
        }
        @if (TempData["Error"] is string err)
        {
            <div class="mb-3 rounded-lg border border-red-500/30 bg-red-500/10 p-3 text-red-300">@err</div>
        }

        <div class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-5">
            <div class="kpi kpi-caramel">
                <div class="kpi-label">Today's Collected</div>
                <div class="kpi-value num">?@Model.TodayCollected.ToString("N2")</div>
            </div>
            <div class="kpi kpi-amber">
                <div class="kpi-label">Unpaid (Pending Pickup)</div>
                <div class="kpi-value num">?@Model.UnpaidPending.ToString("N2")</div>
            </div>
            <div class="kpi kpi-cash">
                <div class="kpi-label">Cash Total</div>
                <div class="kpi-value num">?@Model.CashTotal.ToString("N2")</div>
            </div>
            <div class="kpi kpi-gcash">
                <div class="kpi-label">GCash Total</div>
                <div class="kpi-value num">?@Model.GCashTotal.ToString("N2")</div>
            </div>
            <div class="kpi kpi-red">
                <div class="kpi-label">Refunded Total</div>
                <div class="kpi-value num">?@Model.RefundedTotal.ToString("N2")</div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <form method="get" action="/Admin/Payments" class="filters">
            <div>
                <label for="f-status">Payment Status</label>
                <select id="f-status" name="paymentStatus" class="field">
                    @foreach (var s in new[] { "All", "Paid", "Unpaid", "Refunded" })
                    {
                        <option value="@s" selected="@(Model.PaymentStatusFilter == s)">@s</option>
                    }
                </select>
            </div>
            <div>
                <label for="f-method">Method</label>
                <select id="f-method" name="method" class="field">
                    @foreach (var m in new[] { "All", "Cash", "GCash" })
                    {
                        <option value="@m" selected="@(Model.MethodFilter == m)">@m</option>
                    }
                </select>
            </div>
            <div>
                <label for="f-date">Date</label>
                <input id="f-date" type="date" name="date" value="@Model.Date" class="field" />
            </div>
            <button type="submit" class="btn-apply">Apply</button>
            <a href="/Admin/Payments" class="btn-reset">Reset</a>
        </form>

        <div class="tbl-scroll">
            <div class="tbl" role="table" aria-label="Payments">
                <div class="grid-row tbl-head" role="row">
                    <div role="columnheader">Order</div>
                    <div role="columnheader">Customer</div>
                    <div role="columnheader">Placed</div>
                    <div role="columnheader">Order Status</div>
                    <div role="columnheader">Method</div>
                    <div role="columnheader" class="text-right" style="padding-right:.5rem">Amount</div>
                    <div role="columnheader">Payment</div>
                    <div role="columnheader"></div>
                </div>

                @{
                    var rowIndex = 0;
                }
                @foreach (var o in Model.Orders)
                {
                    var fullName = o.User?.FullName ?? string.Empty;
                    var initial = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(fullName) ? "?" : fullName.Trim()[0].ToString().ToUpperInvariant();
                    var campusId = o.User?.CampusId ?? string.Empty;
                    var avIdx = Math.Abs((campusId + fullName).Aggregate(0, (h, c) => unchecked(h * 31 + c))) % 5;
                    var rowCls = o.PaymentStatus == PaymentStatus.Paid ? "row-paid"
                               : o.PaymentStatus == PaymentStatus.Refunded ? "row-refunded" : "row-unpaid";
                    var payCls = o.PaymentStatus == PaymentStatus.Paid ? "pill-paid"
                               : o.PaymentStatus == PaymentStatus.Refunded ? "pill-refunded" : "pill-unpaid";
                    var methodCls = o.PaymentMethod == PaymentMethod.GCash ? "pill-gcash" : "pill-cash";

                    <div class="grid-row row-card @rowCls" role="row" style="--i:@(Math.Min(rowIndex++, 20))">
                        <div role="cell">
                            <a href="/Admin/OrderDetails/@o.Id" class="order-badge num">#@o.Id</a>
                        </div>
                        <div role="cell" class="cust">
                            <span class="avatar av-@avIdx" aria-hidden="true">@initial</span>
                            <div class="min-w-0">
                                <div class="cust-name">@o.User.FullName</div>
                                <div class="cust-id">@o.User.CampusId</div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        <div role="cell">
                            <div class="cell-muted num">@o.CreatedAtUtc.ToLocalTime().ToString("MMM d, h:mm tt")</div>
                        </div>
                        <div role="cell"><span class="pill pill-order">@o.Status</span></div>
                        <div role="cell"><span class="pill @methodCls">@o.PaymentMethod</span></div>
                        <div role="cell" class="amount num">?@o.Total.ToString("N2")</div>
                        <div role="cell">
                            <span class="pill @payCls"><span class="dot"></span>@o.PaymentStatus</span>
                        </div>
                        <div role="cell" class="actions">
                            @if (o.PaymentStatus == PaymentStatus.Unpaid && o.Status != OrderStatus.Cancelled)
                            {
                                <form method="post" action="/Admin/UpdatePaymentStatus" class="inline">
                                    @Html.AntiForgeryToken()
                                    <input type="hidden" name="id" value="@o.Id" />
                                    <input type="hidden" name="status" value="Paid" />
                                    <input type="hidden" name="fp" value="@(Model.PaymentStatusFilter == "All" ? "" : Model.PaymentStatusFilter)" />
                                    <input type="hidden" name="fm" value="@(Model.MethodFilter == "All" ? "" : Model.MethodFilter)" />
                                    <input type="hidden" name="fd" value="@Model.Date" />
                                    <button type="submit" class="act act-pay">Mark Paid</button>
                                </form>
                            }
                            else if (o.PaymentStatus == PaymentStatus.Paid)
                            {
                                <form method="post" action="/Admin/UpdatePaymentStatus" class="inline"
                                      onsubmit="return confirm('Refund order #@o.Id?');">
                                    @Html.AntiForgeryToken()
                                    <input type="hidden" name="id" value="@o.Id" />
                                    <input type="hidden" name="status" value="Refunded" />
                                    <input type="hidden" name="fp" value="@(Model.PaymentStatusFilter == "All" ? "" : Model.PaymentStatusFilter)" />
                                    <input type="hidden" name="fm" value="@(Model.MethodFilter == "All" ? "" : Model.MethodFilter)" />
                                    <input type="hidden" name="fd" value="@Model.Date" />
                                    <button type="submit" class="act act-refund">Refund</button>
                                </form>
                            }
                        </div>
                    </div>
                }
                @if (Model.Orders.Count == 0)
                {
                    <div class="empty"><i class="fa-solid fa-receipt"></i>No transactions found.</div>
                }
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>

                    <div class="grid-row row-card @rowCls" role="row" style="--i:@(Math.Min(rowIndex++, 20))">

                                <span class="tag-expired">Expired</span>
                            }
                        </div>
                        <div role="cell" class="usage num"><b>@p.TimesUsed</b> / @(p.MaxUses?.ToString() ?? "8")</div>
                        <div role="cell">
                            <form method="post" action="/Admin/TogglePromotion" class="status-form">
                                @Html.AntiForgeryToken()
                                <input type="hidden" name="id" value="@p.Id" />
                                <button type="submit" class="status-btn @(p.IsActive ? "status-on" : "status-off")">
                                    <span class="dot"></span>@(p.IsActive ? "Active" : "Inactive")
                                </button>
                            </form>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                }
                @if (Model.Promotions.Count == 0)
                {
                    <div class="empty"><i class="fa-solid fa-ticket"></i>No promotions yet.</div>
                }
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>

<script>
    (function() {
        function flash(btn) {
            btn.classList.add('copied');
            clearTimeout(btn._t);
            btn._t = setTimeout(function() { btn.classList.remove('copied'); }, 1400);
        }
        function fallbackCopy(text) {
            var ta = document.createElement('textarea');
            ta.value = text; ta.setAttribute('readonly', '');
            ta.style.position = 'fixed'; ta.style.opacity = '0';
            document.body.appendChild(ta); ta.select();
            try { document.execCommand('copy'); } catch (e) { }
            document.body.removeChild(ta);
        }
        document.querySelectorAll('.promo .code-badge').forEach(function(btn) {
            btn.addEventListener('click', function() {
                var code = btn.getAttribute('data-code') || '';
                if (navigator.clipboard && window.isSecureContext) {
                    navigator.clipboard.writeText(code).then(function() { flash(btn); }, function() { fallbackCopy(code); flash(btn); });
                } else {
                    fallbackCopy(code); flash(btn);
                }
            });
        });
    })();
</script>

        @{
            void SalesTable(string title, string col, List<AdminSalesRow> rows)
            {
                <h2 class="rp-h2">@title</h2>
                <div class="rp-scroll">
                    <div class="rp-table" role="table" aria-label="@title">

                            <div role="columnheader" class="rp-num">Orders</div>
                            <div role="columnheader" class="rp-num">Gross Sales</div>
                            <div role="columnheader" class="rp-num">Discounts</div>
                            <div role="columnheader" class="rp-num">Net Revenue</div>
                        </div>
                        @for (var i = 0; i < rows.Count; i++)
                        {
                            var r = rows[i];
                            <div class="rp-row rp-cols-sales" role="row" style="--i:@i">
                                <div role="cell"><span class="rp-badge">@r.Label</span></div>
                                <div role="cell" class="rp-num">@r.Orders</div>
                                <div role="cell" class="rp-num">?@r.Gross.ToString("N2")</div>
                                <div role="cell" class="rp-num @(r.Discounts > 0 ? "rp-disc" : "rp-muted")">?@r.Discounts.ToString("N2")</div>
                                <div role="cell" class="rp-num"><span class="rp-net">?@r.Net.ToString("N2")</span></div>
                            </div>
                        }
                        @if (rows.Count == 0)
                        {
                            <div class="rp-empty">No sales in this range.</div>
                        }
                    </div>
                </div>
            }

            void ProductTable(string title, List<AdminProductRow> rows)
            {
                <div>
                    <h3 class="rp-h3">@title</h3>

                                <div role="columnheader">Product</div>
                                <div role="columnheader" class="rp-num">Units</div>
                                <div role="columnheader" class="rp-num">Revenue</div>
                            </div>
                            @for (var i = 0; i < rows.Count; i++)
                            {
                                <div class="rp-row rp-cols-product" role="row" style="--i:@i">
                                    <div role="cell"><span class="rp-rank">@(i + 1)</span></div>
                                    <div role="cell" class="rp-wrap">@rows[i].Name</div>
                                    <div role="cell" class="rp-num">@rows[i].Units</div>
                                    <div role="cell" class="rp-num"><span class="rp-net">?@rows[i].Revenue.ToString("N2")</span></div>
                                </div>
                            }
                            @if (rows.Count == 0)
                            {
                                <div class="rp-empty">No products in this range.</div>
                            }
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            }
        }

<script>
    (function() {
        var form = document.getElementById('rp-filter-form');
        var apply = document.getElementById('rp-apply');
        if (form && apply) {
            form.addEventListener('submit', function() { apply.classList.add('is-loading'); });
        }
        // Keep End Date >= Start Date in the picker (UI hint only; server logic untouched)
        var start = form && form.querySelector('input[name="startDate"]');
        var end = form && form.querySelector('input[name="endDate"]');
        if (start && end) {
            var sync = function() { if (start.value) end.min = start.value; };
            start.addEventListener('change', sync); sync();
        }
        // Reset entrance animation if user returns via back/forward cache
        window.addEventListener('pageshow', function(ev) {
            if (!ev.persisted) return;
            document.querySelectorAll('.rp-row').forEach(function(r) {
                r.style.animation = 'none'; void r.offsetWidth; r.style.animation = '';
            });
            if (apply) apply.classList.remove('is-loading');
        });
    })();
</script>

@inject BrewStop.Services.IImageResolver Images
@model BrewStop.Models.MenuItem
@{
    var isEdit = Model.Id > 0;
    ViewData["Title"] = isEdit ? "Edit Menu Item" : "New Menu Item";
    var preview = isEdit ? Images.Resolve(Model.ImageUrl, Model.Name) : "";
    var hasUpload = Model.ImageUrl?.StartsWith("/uploads/menu/") == true;
    var input = "mt-1 w-full rounded-lg border border-white/10 bg-card p-2.5 text-cream placeholder-neutral-500 focus:border-caramel focus:outline-none";
}

<div class="tw">
    <div class="mx-auto max-w-xl p-6 text-cream">
        <h1 class="mb-5 font-serif text-2xl font-bold">@ViewData["Title"]</h1>

        <form method="post" enctype="multipart/form-data" class="space-y-4">
            @Html.AntiForgeryToken()
            <input type="hidden" asp-for="Id" />
            <div asp-validation-summary="ModelOnly" class="text-sm text-red-400"></div>

            <div>
                <label asp-for="Name" class="block text-sm font-medium"></label>
                <input asp-for="Name" class="@input" />
                <span asp-validation-for="Name" class="text-sm text-red-400"></span>
            </div>
            <div>
                <label asp-for="Description" class="block text-sm font-medium"></label>
                <textarea asp-for="Description" rows="3" class="@input"></textarea>
                <span asp-validation-for="Description" class="text-sm text-red-400"></span>
            </div>
            <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                <div>
                    <label asp-for="Price" class="block text-sm font-medium"></label>
                    <input asp-for="Price" type="number" step="0.01" min="0" class="@input" />
                    <span asp-validation-for="Price" class="text-sm text-red-400"></span>
                </div>
                <div>
                    <label asp-for="CategoryId" class="block text-sm font-medium"></label>
                    <select asp-for="CategoryId" asp-items="(SelectList)ViewBag.Categories" class="@input [&>option]:bg-card"></select>
                    <span asp-validation-for="CategoryId" class="text-sm text-red-400"></span>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div>
                <label class="block text-sm font-medium">Image</label>
                <label for="image" id="dropzone"
                       class="mt-1 flex cursor-pointer flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border-2 border-dashed border-caramel/40 bg-card p-6 text-center hover:border-caramel">
                    <img id="preview" src="@preview" alt="" class="@(isEdit ? "" : "hidden") h-40 w-40 rounded-lg object-cover" />
                    <i class="fa-solid fa-cloud-arrow-up text-3xl text-caramel"></i>
                    <span class="text-sm">Click to upload or drag &amp; drop</span>
                    <span class="text-xs text-neutral-400">JPG, PNG or WEBP · max 2 MB</span>
                </label>
                <input type="file" id="image" name="image" accept="image/png,image/jpeg,image/webp" class="hidden" />
                @if (isEdit && hasUpload)
                {
                    <label class="mt-2 flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-neutral-300">
                        <input type="checkbox" name="removeImage" value="true" /> Remove current image
                    </label>
                }
            </div>

            <div>
                <label asp-for="BadgeText" class="block text-sm font-medium"></label>
                <input asp-for="BadgeText" class="@input" />
            </div>

            <div class="flex gap-2  ">
                <button class="rounded-lg bg-caramel px-5 py-2 font-semibold text-espresso hover:opacity-90">Save</button>
                <a asp-action="Index" class="rounded-lg border border-white/20 px-5 py-2 text-cream hover:border-caramel hover:text-caramel">Cancel</a>
            </div>
        </form>
    </div>
</div>

@section Scripts {
    <partial name="_ValidationScriptsPartial" />
    <script>
        const fileInput = document.getElementById('image');
        const preview = document.getElementById('preview');
        const zone = document.getElementById('dropzone');

        function showFile(f) {
            if (!f) return;
            if (f.size > 2 * 1024 * 1024) { alert('Image must be 2 MB or smaller.'); fileInput.value = ''; return; }
            preview.src = URL.createObjectURL(f);
            preview.classList.remove('hidden');
        }

        fileInput.addEventListener('change', () => showFile(fileInput.files[0]));
        ['dragenter', 'dragover'].forEach(e => zone.addEventListener(e, ev => { ev.preventDefault(); zone.classList.add('border-caramel'); }));
        ['dragleave', 'drop'].forEach(e => zone.addEventListener(e, ev => { ev.preventDefault(); zone.classList.remove('border-caramel'); }));
        zone.addEventListener('drop', ev => {
            if (ev.dataTransfer.files.length) { fileInput.files = ev.dataTransfer.files; showFile(fileInput.files[0]); }
        });
    </script>
}

@inject BrewStop.Services.IImageResolver Images

@{
    ViewData["Title"] = "Manage Menu";
    var stock = (Dictionary<int, int>)ViewBag.Stock;
    var n = 0;
    var cAvail = Model.Count(x => stock.GetValueOrDefault(x.Id, 0) > 5);
    var cLow = Model.Count(x => { var q = stock.GetValueOrDefault(x.Id, 0); return q > 0 && q <= 5; });
    var cSold = Model.Count(x => stock.GetValueOrDefault(x.Id, 0) == 0);
}

<script>
    (function() {
        var seg = document.getElementById('mm-seg');
        var ind = document.getElementById('mm-ind');
        var list = document.getElementById('mm-list');
        var empty = document.getElementById('mm-empty');
        var shown = document.getElementById('mm-shown');
        var live = document.getElementById('mm-live');
        if (!seg || !ind || !list) return;

        var tabs = Array.prototype.slice.call(seg.querySelectorAll('.mm-tab'));
        var rows = Array.prototype.slice.call(list.querySelectorAll('.mm-row'));
        var current = 'all';

        function moveIndicator(tab, instant) {
            if (instant) ind.style.transition = 'none';
            ind.style.width = tab.offsetWidth + 'px';
            ind.style.transform = 'translateX(' + tab.offsetLeft + 'px)';
            if (instant) { void ind.offsetWidth; ind.style.transition = ''; }
        }

        function apply(filter) {
            current = filter;
            var visible = 0;
            rows.forEach(function(r) {
                var match = filter === 'all' || r.getAttribute('data-status') === filter;
                r.hidden = !match;
                if (match) {
                    r.style.setProperty('--i', visible++);
                    r.style.animation = 'none'; void r.offsetWidth; r.style.animation = '';
                }
            });
            if (shown) shown.textContent = visible;
            if (empty) empty.hidden = visible !== 0;
            if (live) { live.classList.remove('bump'); void live.offsetWidth; live.classList.add('bump'); }
        }

        function select(tab, focus) {
            tabs.forEach(function(t) {
                var on = t === tab;
                t.setAttribute('aria-selected', on ? 'true' : 'false');
                t.tabIndex = on ? 0 : -1;
            });
            moveIndicator(tab, false);
            if (focus) tab.focus();
            apply(tab.getAttribute('data-filter'));
        }

        tabs.forEach(function(t, i) {
            t.addEventListener('click', function() { select(t, false); });
            t.addEventListener('keydown', function(ev) {
                var k = ev.key, next = null;
                if (k === 'ArrowRight') next = tabs[(i + 1) % tabs.length];
                else if (k === 'ArrowLeft') next = tabs[(i - 1 + tabs.length) % tabs.length];
                else if (k === 'Home') next = tabs[0];
                else if (k === 'End') next = tabs[tabs.length - 1];
                if (next) { ev.preventDefault(); select(next, true); }
            });
        });

        // Thumbnail fallback (icon shows through when image fails)
        list.querySelectorAll('.mm-thumb img').forEach(function(img) {
            var broken = function() { img.parentNode.classList.add('is-broken'); };
            img.addEventListener('error', broken);
            if (img.complete && img.naturalWidth === 0) broken();
        });

        function init() { moveIndicator(seg.querySelector('[aria-selected="true"]'), true); }
        window.addEventListener('resize', function() {
            moveIndicator(seg.querySelector('[aria-selected="true"]'), true);
        });
        window.addEventListener('load', init);
        if (document.fonts && document.fonts.ready) document.fonts.ready.then(init);
        init();
    })();
</script>

FILE: BrewStop\Views\Barista\Dashboard.cshtml

@model BrewStop.Models.BaristaDashboardViewModel
@inject BrewStop.Services.IPickupSlotService Slots
@{
    ViewData["Title"] = "Barista Dashboard";
    var nowUtc = DateTime.UtcNow;
    string Ago(DateTime utc)
    {
        var m = (int)Math.Max(0, (nowUtc - DateTime.SpecifyKind(utc, DateTimeKind.Utc)).TotalMinutes);
        return m < 1 ? "just now" : m < 60 ? $"{m} min ago" : m < 1440 ? $"{m / 60} h ago" : $"{m / 1440} d ago";
    }
    var tiles = new (string Label, int Value, string Icon, string Href, string Tone)[]
    {
        ("Waiting in queue", Model.Placed, "fa-list-ol", "/Barista/Queue", "text-[#FFD9A0]"),
        ("Preparing", Model.Preparing, "fa-mug-hot", "/Barista/Preparation", "text-emerald-300"),
        ("Ready for pickup", Model.Ready, "fa-bag-shopping", "/Barista/Status", "text-[#FFD9A0]"),
        ("Orders today", Model.OrdersToday, "fa-receipt", "/Barista/Status", "text-[#FFD9A0]"),
        ("Picked up today", Model.ClaimedToday, "fa-circle-check", "/Barista/Status", "text-emerald-300"),
        ("Cancelled today", Model.CancelledToday, "fa-ban", "/Barista/Notifications", "text-red-300"),
    };
}

<div class="tw kds-page">
    <div class="mx-auto max-w-6xl px-4 pt-6 pb-8">

                <h1 class="font-serif text-3xl font-bold text-[#FFD9A0]">Dashboard</h1>
                <p class="mt-1 text-sm text-[#FFD9A0]/70">@Model.LocalNow.ToString("dddd, MMMM d, yyyy") · daily order statistics</p>
            </div>

            {
                <a href="/Barista/Shifts" class="rounded-full border border-solid border-emerald-400/50 bg-emerald-400/10 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-emerald-200 no-underline">
                    <i class="fa-solid fa-clock mr-1.5"></i>Clocked in since @Slots.ToLocal(Model.OpenShift.ClockInAt!.Value).ToString("h:mm tt")
                </a>
            }
            else
            {
                <a href="/Barista/Shifts" class="rounded-full border border-solid border-[#644234] bg-[#422C22] px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-[#FFD9A0] no-underline transition hover:border-[#FFD9A0] hover:bg-[#5C3B2E]">
                    <i class="fa-regular fa-clock mr-1.5"></i>Not clocked in
                </a>
            }
        </div>

        @if (Model.LowStockCount > 0)
        {
            <a href="/Barista/Notifications" class="mt-4 flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl border border-solid border-yellow-500/40 bg-yellow-500/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-yellow-200 no-underline">
                <i class="fa-solid fa-triangle-exclamation"></i>
                <span>@Model.LowStockCount item(s) are low or out of stock. Tell the shop admin to restock.</span>
            </a>
        }

        <div class="mt-5 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 lg:grid-cols-3">
            @foreach (var t in tiles)
            {

                    <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                        <p class="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-[#815F51]">@t.Label</p>
                        <i class="fa-solid @t.Icon @t.Tone"></i>
                    </div>
                    <p class="mt-2 font-serif text-4xl font-extrabold text-[#FFD9A0]">@t.Value</p>
                </a>
            }
        </div>

        <div class="mt-6 grid gap-5 lg:grid-cols-3">
            <section class="min-w-0 rounded-2xl border border-solid border-[#644234] bg-[#36241C] p-5 lg:col-span-1">
                <h2 class="font-serif text-lg font-bold text-[#FFD9A0]">Most ordered today</h2>
                @if (Model.TopItems.Count == 0)
                {
                    <p class="mt-3 text-sm text-[#815F51]">No orders yet today.</p>
                }
                else
                {
                    var max = Model.TopItems.Max(i => i.Quantity);
                    <ul class="mt-4 space-y-3">
                        @foreach (var i in Model.TopItems)
                        {
                            var pct = (int)Math.Round(100.0 * i.Quantity / max);
                            <li>
                                <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 text-sm">
                                    <span class="truncate text-[#FFD9A0]">@i.Name</span>
                                    <span class="shrink-0 font-bold text-[#FFD9A0]">@(i.Quantity)x</span>
                                </div>
                                <div class="mt-1 h-2 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-[#4D3328]">
                                    <div class="h-full rounded-full bg-[#FFD9A0]" style="width:@(pct)%"></div>
                                </div>
                            </li>
                        }
                    </ul>
                }
            </section>

            <section class="min-w-0 rounded-2xl border border-solid border-[#644234] bg-[#36241C] p-5 lg:col-span-2">
                <h2 class="font-serif text-lg font-bold text-[#FFD9A0]">Recent activity</h2>
                @if (Model.Recent.Count == 0)
                {
                    <p class="mt-3 text-sm text-[#815F51]">No recent order activity.</p>
                }
                else
                {
                    <ul class="kds-scroll mt-3 max-h-[420px] overflow-y-auto pr-1">
                        @foreach (var a in Model.Recent)
                        {
                            <li class="flex items-start gap-3 border-t border-solid border-[#644234] py-3 first:border-t-0">
                                <span class="mt-0.5 grid size-9 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full bg-[#4D3328] text-[#FFD9A0]"><i class="fa-solid @a.Icon text-sm"></i></span>
                                <div class="min-w-0 flex-1">
                                    <p class="text-sm font-semibold text-[#FFD9A0]">@a.Title</p>
                                    <p class="break-words text-xs text-[#FFD9A0]/70">@a.Text</p>
                                </div>
                                <time datetime="@DateTime.SpecifyKind(a.AtUtc, DateTimeKind.Utc).ToString("o")" class="shrink-0 text-xs text-[#815F51]">@Ago(a.AtUtc)</time>
                            </li>
                        }
                    </ul>
                }
            </section>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>

@model List<BrewStop.Models.BaristaAlertDto>
@inject BrewStop.Services.IPickupSlotService Slots
@{
    ViewData["Title"] = "Notifications";
    var nowUtc = DateTime.UtcNow;
    string Ago(DateTime utc)
    {
        var m = (int)Math.Max(0, (nowUtc - DateTime.SpecifyKind(utc, DateTimeKind.Utc)).TotalMinutes);
        return m < 1 ? "just now" : m < 60 ? $"{m} min ago" : m < 1440 ? $"{m / 60} h ago" : $"{m / 1440} d ago";
    }
    (string Icon, string Tone, string Label) Look(BrewStop.Models.BaristaAlertDto a) => a.Kind switch
    {
        "NewOrder" => ("fa-bag-shopping", "bg-[#FFD9A0]/15 text-[#FFD9A0]", "New order"),
        "Cancelled" => ("fa-ban", "bg-red-500/15 text-red-300", "Cancelled"),
        "Note" => ("fa-triangle-exclamation", "bg-amber-400/15 text-amber-300", "Special note"),
        _ => (a.Urgent ? "fa-circle-exclamation" : "fa-box-open", a.Urgent ? "bg-red-500/15 text-red-300" : "bg-yellow-500/15 text-yellow-300", "Low stock")
    };
    const string tab = "kds-tab flex shrink-0 items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-solid border-[#644234] bg-[#422C22] px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-[#FFD9A0] hover:bg-[#5C3B2E]";
}

        </p>

        <div class="kds-scroll mt-4 flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto pb-1" role="group" aria-label="Filter notifications">

            <button type="button" data-kind-filter="NewOrder" aria-pressed="false" class="@tab">New orders</button>
            <button type="button" data-kind-filter="Cancelled" aria-pressed="false" class="@tab">Cancellations</button>
            <button type="button" data-kind-filter="LowStock" aria-pressed="false" class="@tab">Low stock</button>
            <button type="button" data-kind-filter="Note" aria-pressed="false" class="@tab">Special notes</button>
        </div>

        <div id="alerts-feed" class="kds-scroll mt-4 max-h-[calc(100vh-17rem)] min-h-[280px] overflow-y-auto rounded-2xl border border-solid border-[#644234] bg-[#36241C]">
            <p data-alerts-empty hidden="@(Model.Count > 0 ? "hidden" : null)" class="px-4 py-14 text-center text-sm text-[#815F51]">You're all caught up. No notifications.</p>
            <ul>
                @foreach (var a in Model)
                {
                    var look = Look(a);
                    <li data-kind="@a.Kind" class="border-t border-solid border-[#644234] transition-colors first:border-t-0 hover:bg-[#5C3B2E]">
                        <div class="flex items-start gap-3 px-4 py-3.5">
                            <span class="mt-0.5 grid size-10 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full @look.Tone"><i class="fa-solid @look.Icon"></i></span>
                            <div class="min-w-0 flex-1">
                                <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                                    <p class="text-sm font-bold text-[#FFD9A0]">@a.Title</p>
                                    <span class="rounded-full bg-[#201A17] px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-[#FFD9A0]">@look.Label</span>
                                </div>
                                <p class="mt-0.5 break-words text-sm @(a.Kind == "Note" ? "font-semibold text-amber-200" : "text-[#FFD9A0]/80")">@a.Detail</p>
                                @if (a.OrderId is > 0 && a.Kind is "NewOrder")
                                {
                                    <a href="/Barista/Queue" class="mt-1 inline-block text-xs font-semibold text-[#FFD9A0] no-underline hover:underline">Open Order Queue &rarr;</a>
                                }
                            </div>
                            @if (a.AtUtc is not null)
                            {
                                <time datetime="@DateTime.SpecifyKind(a.AtUtc.Value, DateTimeKind.Utc).ToString("o")" class="shrink-0 text-xs text-[#815F51]"
                                      title="@Slots.ToLocal(a.AtUtc.Value).ToString("MMM d, h:mm tt")">@Ago(a.AtUtc.Value)</time>
                            }
                        </div>
                    </li>
                }
            </ul>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>

@section Scripts {
    <style>
        [data-kind][hidden], [data-alerts-empty][hidden] {
            display: none !important;
        }
    </style>
    <script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/microsoft-signalr/8.0.7/signalr.min.js"></script>
    <script>
        (function() {
            var feed = document.getElementById('alerts-feed');
            var live = document.getElementById('kds-live'), liveText = document.getElementById('kds-live-text');
            var filter = 'all';

            function apply() {
                var shown = 0;
                [].slice.call(feed.querySelectorAll('[data-kind]')).forEach(function(li) {
                    var on = filter === 'all' || li.getAttribute('data-kind') === filter;
                    li.hidden = !on;
                    if (on) shown++;
                });
                var e = feed.querySelector('[data-alerts-empty]');
                if (e) e.hidden = shown !== 0;
                [].slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('[data-kind-filter]')).forEach(function(b) {
                    b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', b.getAttribute('data-kind-filter') === filter ? 'true' : 'false');
                });
                var total = feed.querySelectorAll('[data-kind]').length;
                var c = document.querySelector('[data-kcount="all"]');
                if (c) c.textContent = total;
            }

            document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
                var b = e.target.closest('[data-kind-filter]');
                if (!b) return;
                filter = b.getAttribute('data-kind-filter');
                apply();
            });

            var busy = false;
            function refresh() {
                if (busy) return;
                busy = true;
                fetch('/Barista/Notifications', { credentials: 'same-origin' })
                    .then(function(r) { return r.ok ? r.text() : null; })
                    .then(function(html) {
                        if (!html) return;
                        var nf = new DOMParser().parseFromString(html, 'text/html').getElementById('alerts-feed');
                        if (!nf) return;
                        var top = feed.scrollTop;
                        feed.innerHTML = nf.innerHTML;
                        feed.scrollTop = top;
                        apply();
                        window.dispatchEvent(new Event('barista:changed'));
                    })
                    .catch(function() { })
                    .then(function() { busy = false; });
            }

            function setLive(on) {
                live.classList.toggle('kds-live-on', on);
                liveText.textContent = on ? 'Live' : 'Reconnecting…';
            }

            if (window.signalR) {
                var conn = new signalR.HubConnectionBuilder().withUrl('/hubs/orders').withAutomaticReconnect().build();
                conn.on('QueueChanged', refresh);
                conn.onreconnecting(function() { setLive(false); });
                conn.onreconnected(function() { setLive(true); refresh(); });
                conn.onclose(function() { setLive(false); });
                conn.start().then(function() { setLive(true); }).catch(function() { setLive(false); });
            }
            setInterval(function() { if (!document.hidden) refresh(); }, 30000);
            apply();
        })();
    </script>
}

@section Scripts {
    <style>
        .tw .rg-pill[aria-pressed="true"] {
            background-color: #FFD9A0 !important;
            border-color: #FFD9A0 !important;
            color: #201A17 !important;
        }

        [data-rg-card][hidden], [data-rg-section][hidden], #rg-empty[hidden] {
            display: none !important;
        }
    </style>
    <script>
        (function() {
            var input = document.getElementById('rg-search');
            var cards = [].slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('[data-rg-card]'));
            var sections = [].slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('[data-rg-section]'));
            var empty = document.getElementById('rg-empty');
            var cat = 'all';

            function apply() {
                var q = input.value.trim().toLowerCase(), shown = 0;
                cards.forEach(function(c) {
                    var sec = c.closest('[data-rg-section]');
                    var ok = (!q || c.getAttribute('data-search').indexOf(q) !== -1) &&
                        (cat === 'all' || sec.getAttribute('data-rg-section') === cat);
                    c.hidden = !ok;
                    if (ok) shown++;
                });
                sections.forEach(function(s) { s.hidden = !s.querySelector('[data-rg-card]:not([hidden])'); });
                empty.hidden = shown !== 0;
                [].slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('[data-rg-cat]')).forEach(function(b) {
                    b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', b.getAttribute('data-rg-cat') === cat ? 'true' : 'false');
                });
            }

            input.addEventListener('input', apply);
            document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
                var b = e.target.closest('[data-rg-cat]');
                if (!b) return;
                cat = b.getAttribute('data-rg-cat');
                apply();
            });
            apply();
        })();
    </script>
}

                                    <td class="py-2.5 pr-3">@L(s.ClockInAt!.Value, "h:mm tt")</td>
                                    <td class="py-2.5 pr-3">@L(s.ClockOutAt!.Value, "h:mm tt")</td>
                                    <td class="py-2.5 pr-3">@(Unscheduled(s) ? "Unscheduled" : L(s.ScheduledStart, "h:mm tt") + " – " + L(s.ScheduledEnd, "h:mm tt"))</td>
                                    <td class="py-2.5 text-right font-bold text-[#FFD9A0]">@((s.TotalHours ?? 0m).ToString("0.00"))</td>
                                </tr>
                            }
                        </tbody>
                    </table>
                </div>
            }
        </section>
    </div>
</div>

@section Scripts {
    <script>
        (function() {
            var el = document.getElementById('shift-elapsed');
            if (!el) return;
            var start = new Date(el.getAttribute('data-clockin')).getTime();
            function pad(n) { return (n < 10 ? '0' : '') + n; }
            function tick() {
                var s = Math.max(0, Math.floor((Date.now() - start) / 1000));
                el.textContent = pad(Math.floor(s / 3600)) + ':' + pad(Math.floor(s % 3600 / 60)) + ':' + pad(s % 60);
            }
            tick();
            setInterval(tick, 1000);
        })();
    </script>
}

@model List<BrewStop.Models.Order>
@using BrewStop.Models
@{
    ViewData["Title"] = "Order Status";
    var placed = Model.Where(o => o.Status == OrderStatus.Placed).ToList();
    var preparing = Model.Where(o => o.Status == OrderStatus.Preparing).ToList();
    var ready = Model.Where(o => o.Status == OrderStatus.Ready).ToList();
    var columns = new (OrderStatus Status, string Title, string Icon, List<Order> Orders)[]
    {
        (OrderStatus.Placed, "Placed / New", "fa-bell-concierge", placed),
        (OrderStatus.Preparing, "Preparing", "fa-mug-hot", preparing),
        (OrderStatus.Ready, "Ready for Pickup", "fa-bag-shopping", ready)
    };
    const string tab = "kds-tab flex shrink-0 items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-solid border-[#644234] bg-[#422C22] px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-[#FFD9A0] hover:bg-[#5C3B2E]";
    const string cnt = "rounded-full bg-[#201A17] px-2 py-0.5 text-xs text-[#FFD9A0]";
}

<div class="tw kds-page">
    <div class="mx-auto w-full max-w-[1600px] px-4 pt-6 pb-6">
        <div class="flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-4">
            <div>
                <h1 class="font-serif text-3xl font-bold text-[#FFD9A0]">Order Status</h1>
                <p class="mt-1 text-sm text-[#FFD9A0]/70">
                    <span id="kds-live" class="inline-flex items-center gap-2"><span class="kds-dot"></span><span id="kds-live-text">Connecting…</span></span>
                </p>
            </div>

            <form asp-action="Scan" method="post" data-kds-form class="flex w-full flex-wrap items-center gap-2 sm:w-auto">
                <input id="kds-scan-input" name="token" autocomplete="off" maxlength="512" placeholder="Scan or paste pickup QR token"
                       class="min-w-0 flex-1 rounded-xl border border-solid border-[#644234] bg-[#422C22] px-4 py-2.5 text-sm text-[#FFD9A0] placeholder:text-[#815F51] focus:border-[#FFD9A0] focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#FFD9A0]/30 sm:w-80 sm:flex-none" />
                <button type="submit" class="rounded-full bg-[#FFD9A0] px-5 py-2.5 text-sm font-bold text-[#201A17] transition hover:brightness-110">
                    <i class="fa-solid fa-qrcode mr-1.5"></i>Verify pickup
                </button>
            </form>
        </div>

        <p id="kds-scan-hint" class="mt-2 rounded-xl border border-solid border-[#FFD9A0] bg-[#FFD9A0]/10 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-[#FFD9A0]" hidden></p>

        <div id="kds-flash" class="mt-3" aria-live="polite">
            @if (TempData["Success"] is string ok)
            {
                <p class="rounded-xl border border-solid border-emerald-400/30 bg-emerald-500/10 p-3 text-sm text-emerald-300">@ok</p>
            }
            @if (TempData["Error"] is string err)
            {
                <p class="rounded-xl border border-solid border-red-400/30 bg-red-500/10 p-3 text-sm text-red-300">@err</p>
            }
        </div>

        <div class="mt-4 flex flex-col gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-solid border-[#644234] bg-[#36241C] p-3 lg:flex-row lg:items-center lg:justify-between">
            <div class="kds-scroll flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto pb-1" role="group" aria-label="Filter orders by status">
                <button type="button" data-filter="all" aria-pressed="true" class="@tab">All <span data-count="all" class="@cnt">@Model.Count</span></button>
                <button type="button" data-filter="Placed" aria-pressed="false" class="@tab">Placed / New <span data-count="Placed" class="@cnt">@placed.Count</span></button>
                <button type="button" data-filter="Preparing" aria-pressed="false" class="@tab">Preparing <span data-count="Preparing" class="@cnt">@preparing.Count</span></button>
                <button type="button" data-filter="Ready" aria-pressed="false" class="@tab">Ready for Pickup <span data-count="Ready" class="@cnt">@ready.Count</span></button>
            </div>
            <div class="relative w-full lg:w-80">
                <i class="fa-solid fa-magnifying-glass pointer-events-none absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-sm text-[#815F51]" aria-hidden="true"></i>
                <input id="kds-search" type="search" autocomplete="off" placeholder="Search order # or customer name" aria-label="Search orders"
                       class="w-full rounded-xl border border-solid border-[#644234] bg-[#201A17] py-2.5 pl-10 pr-3 text-sm text-[#FFD9A0] placeholder:text-[#815F51] focus:border-[#FFD9A0] focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#FFD9A0]/30" />
            </div>
        </div>

        <div id="kds-board" class="kds-board mt-4" data-url="/Barista/Status">
            @foreach (var col in columns)
            {
                <section data-col="@col.Status" class="kds-col flex min-h-0 flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-solid border-[#644234] bg-[#36241C]">
                    <header class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 border-b border-solid border-[#644234] bg-[#36241C] px-4 py-3">
                        <h2 class="min-w-0 truncate font-serif text-lg font-bold text-[#FFD9A0]"><i class="fa-solid @col.Icon mr-2" aria-hidden="true"></i>@col.Title</h2>
                        <span data-col-count class="shrink-0 rounded-full bg-[#201A17] px-3 py-0.5 text-sm font-bold text-[#FFD9A0]">@col.Orders.Count</span>
                    </header>
                    <div data-col-body class="kds-col-body kds-scroll min-h-0 flex-1 space-y-3 p-3">
                        <p data-empty hidden="@(col.Orders.Count > 0 ? "hidden" : null)" class="rounded-xl border border-dashed border-[#644234] px-4 py-8 text-center text-sm text-[#815F51]">No orders here</p>
                        @foreach (var o in col.Orders)
                        {
                            <partial name="_OrderCard" model="o" />
                        }
                    </div>
                </section>
            }
        </div>
    </div>
</div>

    <partial name="_KdsAssets" />

<script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/microsoft-signalr/8.0.7/signalr.min.js"></script>
<script>
    (function() {
        var board = document.getElementById('kds-board');
        if (!board) return;
        var flash = document.getElementById('kds-flash');
        var search = document.getElementById('kds-search');
        var live = document.getElementById('kds-live');
        var liveText = document.getElementById('kds-live-text');
        var scanInput = document.getElementById('kds-scan-input');
        var scanHint = document.getElementById('kds-scan-hint');
        var url = board.getAttribute('data-url') || window.location.pathname;
        var state = { filter: 'all', q: '' };
        var flashTimer = null;

        if (document.querySelector('[data-filter]')) {
            try { state.filter = sessionStorage.getItem('kds-filter') || 'all'; } catch (e) { }
        }

        function applyFilter() {
            var q = state.q.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/^#/, '');
            var counts = { all: 0, Placed: 0, Preparing: 0, Ready: 0 };
            var visible = { Placed: 0, Preparing: 0, Ready: 0 };
            var shown = 0;

            [].slice.call(board.querySelectorAll('[data-card]')).forEach(function(c) {
                var st = c.getAttribute('data-status');
                counts.all++; counts[st]++;
                var hay = (c.getAttribute('data-order') + ' ' + c.getAttribute('data-name') + ' ' + c.getAttribute('data-campus')).toLowerCase();
                var show = (!q || hay.indexOf(q) !== -1) && (state.filter === 'all' || state.filter === st);
                c.hidden = !show;
                if (show) { visible[st]++; shown++; }
            });

            var cols = [].slice.call(board.querySelectorAll('[data-col]')), n = 0;
            cols.forEach(function(col) {
                var st = col.getAttribute('data-col');
                var show = state.filter === 'all' || state.filter === st;
                col.hidden = !show;
                if (show) n++;
                var empty = col.querySelector('[data-empty]');
                if (empty) empty.hidden = visible[st] !== 0;
                var cnt = col.querySelector('[data-col-count]');
                if (cnt) cnt.textContent = counts[st];
            });
            if (cols.length) board.style.setProperty('--kds-cols', Math.max(n, 1));

            var ea = board.querySelector('[data-empty-all]');
            if (ea) {
                ea.hidden = shown !== 0;
                ea.textContent = counts.all === 0 ? ea.getAttribute('data-none') : ea.getAttribute('data-nomatch');
            }

            [].slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('[data-count]')).forEach(function(el) {
                el.textContent = counts[el.getAttribute('data-count')];
            });
            [].slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('[data-filter]')).forEach(function(b) {
                b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', b.getAttribute('data-filter') === state.filter ? 'true' : 'false');
            });
        }

        function tick() {
            var now = Date.now();
            [].slice.call(board.querySelectorAll('[data-since]')).forEach(function(el) {
                var m = Math.max(0, Math.floor((now - new Date(el.getAttribute('data-since')).getTime()) / 60000));
                el.textContent = m < 1 ? 'Just now' : (m < 60 ? m + ' min' : Math.floor(m / 60) + 'h ' + (m % 60) + 'm');
                if (el.parentElement) el.parentElement.setAttribute('data-level', m >= 15 ? 'late' : (m >= 10 ? 'warn' : 'ok'));
            });
        }

        function showFlash(html) {
            if (!flash || !html || !html.trim()) return;
            flash.innerHTML = html;
            clearTimeout(flashTimer);
            flashTimer = setTimeout(function() { flash.innerHTML = ''; }, 6000);
        }

        function swapFrom(html) {
            var doc = new DOMParser().parseFromString(html, 'text/html');
            var nb = doc.getElementById('kds-board');
            if (!nb) return false;

            var top = board.scrollTop, scroll = {};
            [].slice.call(board.querySelectorAll('[data-col]')).forEach(function(c) {
                var b = c.querySelector('[data-col-body]');
                if (b) scroll[c.getAttribute('data-col')] = b.scrollTop;
            });

            board.innerHTML = nb.innerHTML;
            board.scrollTop = top;

            [].slice.call(board.querySelectorAll('[data-col]')).forEach(function(c) {
                var b = c.querySelector('[data-col-body]');
                var s = scroll[c.getAttribute('data-col')];
                if (b && s) b.scrollTop = s;
            });

            var nf = doc.getElementById('kds-flash');
            if (nf) showFlash(nf.innerHTML);
            applyFilter();
            tick();
            window.dispatchEvent(new Event('barista:changed'));
            return true;
        }

        var refreshing = false, pending = false;
        function refresh() {
            if (refreshing) { pending = true; return; }
            refreshing = true;
            fetch(url, { credentials: 'same-origin', headers: { 'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest' } })
                .then(function(r) { if (!r.ok) throw new Error('refresh failed'); return r.text(); })
                .then(swapFrom)
                .catch(function() { })
                .then(function() { refreshing = false; if (pending) { pending = false; refresh(); } });
        }

        // One-tap actions: POST (antiforgery token included), then swap in the re-rendered page board.
        document.addEventListener('submit', function(e) {
            var f = e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('form[data-kds-form]') : null;
            if (!f || !window.fetch) return;
            e.preventDefault();
            var btn = f.querySelector('button[type="submit"]');
            if (btn) btn.disabled = true;

            fetch(f.action, { method: 'POST', body: new FormData(f), credentials: 'same-origin' })
                .then(function(r) { return r.text(); })
                .then(function(html) {
                    if (!swapFrom(html)) { window.location.reload(); return; }
                    if (f.querySelector('input[name="token"]')) {
                        f.reset();
                        if (scanHint) scanHint.hidden = true;
                        [].slice.call(board.querySelectorAll('.kds-card-focus')).forEach(function(c) { c.classList.remove('kds-card-focus'); });
                        if (scanInput) scanInput.focus();
                    }
                    if (btn) btn.disabled = false;
                })
                .catch(function() { window.location.reload(); });
        });

        board.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
            var b = e.target.closest('[data-verify]');
            if (!b || !scanInput) return;
            [].slice.call(board.querySelectorAll('.kds-card-focus')).forEach(function(c) { c.classList.remove('kds-card-focus'); });
            var card = b.closest('[data-card]');
            if (card) card.classList.add('kds-card-focus');
            if (scanHint) {
                scanHint.textContent = 'Scan the pickup QR for Order #' + b.getAttribute('data-verify') + ' to complete it.';
                scanHint.hidden = false;
            }
            scanInput.scrollIntoView({ block: 'center', behavior: 'smooth' });
            scanInput.focus();
        });

        document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
            var t = e.target.closest('[data-filter]');
            if (!t) return;
            state.filter = t.getAttribute('data-filter');
            try { sessionStorage.setItem('kds-filter', state.filter); } catch (err) { }
            applyFilter();
        });

        if (search) search.addEventListener('input', function() { state.q = search.value; applyFilter(); });

        function setLive(on) {
            if (!live) return;
            live.classList.toggle('kds-live-on', on);
            liveText.textContent = on ? 'Live — updates automatically' : 'Reconnecting…';
        }

        if (window.signalR) {
            var conn = new signalR.HubConnectionBuilder().withUrl('/hubs/orders').withAutomaticReconnect().build();
            conn.on('QueueChanged', refresh);
            conn.on('OrderStatusChanged', refresh);
            conn.onreconnecting(function() { setLive(false); });
            conn.onreconnected(function() { setLive(true); refresh(); });
            conn.onclose(function() { setLive(false); });
            conn.start().then(function() { setLive(true); }).catch(function() { setLive(false); });
        }

        setInterval(function() { if (!document.hidden) refresh(); }, 20000);
        setInterval(tick, 30000);

        applyFilter();
        tick();
    })();
</script>
