@model BrewStop.Models.Order
@inject BrewStop.Services.IPickupSlotService Slots
@using BrewStop.Models
@{
    var o = Model;
    var nowUtc = DateTime.UtcNow;
    var todayLocal = Slots.ToLocal(nowUtc).Date;
    var inv = System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture;
    var modRx = new System.Text.RegularExpressions.Regex(@"\b(ice|iced|sugar|sweet\w*|hot|warm|cold|less|no|decaf)\b",
        System.Text.RegularExpressions.RegexOptions.IgnoreCase);
    var ret = ViewContext.RouteData.Values["action"]?.ToString();
    if (ret is not ("Queue" or "Preparation" or "Status")) ret = "Status";

    var pickup = Slots.ToLocal(o.PickupAtUtc);
    var st = o.Status.ToString();
    var created = DateTime.SpecifyKind(o.CreatedAtUtc, DateTimeKind.Utc);
    var mins = (int)Math.Max(0, (nowUtc - created).TotalMinutes);
    var wait = mins < 1 ? "Just now" : mins < 60 ? $"{mins} min" : $"{mins / 60}h {mins % 60}m";
    var level = mins >= 15 ? "late" : mins >= 10 ? "warn" : "ok";
}
<article data-card data-status="@st" data-order="@o.Id" data-name="@o.User.FullName" data-campus="@o.User.CampusId"
         class="kds-card kds-top-@st rounded-2xl border border-solid border-[#644234] bg-[#422C22] shadow-lg shadow-black/30 transition-colors hover:bg-[#5C3B2E]">

    <div class="kds-head p-4 pb-3">
        <div class="min-w-0">
            <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                <span class="font-serif text-3xl font-extrabold leading-none text-[#FFD9A0]">#@o.Id</span>
                <span class="kds-b-@st rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-xs font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wide">@st</span>
            </div>
            <p class="mt-2 truncate text-base font-semibold text-[#FFD9A0]">@o.User.FullName</p>
            <p class="truncate text-xs text-[#FFD9A0]/60">Campus ID: @o.User.CampusId</p>
        </div>
        <div class="kds-pickup min-w-0 text-right">
            <p class="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-[#815F51]">Pickup</p>
            <p class="text-2xl font-extrabold leading-tight text-[#FFD9A0]">@pickup.ToString("h:mm tt")</p>
            @if (pickup.Date != todayLocal)
            {
                <p class="text-xs text-[#FFD9A0]/60">@pickup.ToString("ddd, MMM d")</p>
            }
            <p class="kds-wait mt-1 text-xs" data-level="@level">
                <i class="fa-regular fa-clock" aria-hidden="true"></i>
                <span data-since="@created.ToString("o")" class="font-bold">@wait</span>
                <span class="text-[#FFD9A0]/60">· received @Slots.ToLocal(o.CreatedAtUtc).ToString("h:mm tt")</span>
            </p>
        </div>
    </div>

    <ul class="space-y-2 px-4 pb-3">
        @foreach (var i in o.Items)
        {
            var temp = i.ItemName.StartsWith("Iced", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ? "Iced"
                     : i.ItemName.StartsWith("Hot", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ? "Hot" : null;
            var mods = i.AddOns.Where(a => modRx.IsMatch(a.Name)).Select(a => a.Name).ToList();
            var adds = i.AddOns.Where(a => !modRx.IsMatch(a.Name)).Select(a => a.Name).ToList();
            <li class="flex items-start gap-3 rounded-xl border border-solid border-[#644234] bg-[#4D3328] p-3">
                <span class="flex h-11 min-w-[3rem] shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-[#FFD9A0] px-2 text-xl font-extrabold leading-none text-[#201A17]">@(i.Quantity)x</span>
                <div class="min-w-0 flex-1">
                    <p class="break-words text-base font-bold leading-tight text-[#FFD9A0]">@i.ItemName</p>
                    @if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(i.SizeName) || temp is not null)
                    {
                        <div class="mt-1.5 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
                            @if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(i.SizeName))
                            {
                                <span class="rounded-md border border-solid border-[#FFD9A0] px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-extrabold uppercase text-[#FFD9A0]">@i.SizeName</span>
                            }
                            @if (temp is not null)
                            {
                                <span class="rounded-md border border-solid @(temp == "Iced" ? "border-sky-300/70 bg-sky-400/10 text-sky-200" : "border-orange-300/70 bg-orange-400/10 text-orange-200") px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-bold uppercase">@temp</span>
                            }
                        </div>
                    }
                    @if (mods.Count > 0)
                    {
                        <div class="mt-1.5 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
                            <span class="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-[#815F51]">Modifiers</span>
                            @foreach (var m in mods)
                            {
                                <span class="rounded-md border border-solid border-amber-300/70 bg-amber-400/10 px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold text-amber-200">@m</span>
                            }
                        </div>
                    }
                    @if (adds.Count > 0)
                    {
                        <div class="mt-1.5 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
                            <span class="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-[#815F51]">Add-ons</span>
                            @foreach (var a in adds)
                            {
                                <span class="rounded-md bg-[#644234] px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold text-[#FFD9A0]">+ @a</span>
                            }
                        </div>
                    }
                </div>
            </li>
        }
    </ul>

    @if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(o.Note))
    {
        <div class="mx-4 mb-3 rounded-xl border-2 border-solid border-amber-400 bg-amber-400/15 p-3">
            <p class="text-[11px] font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider text-amber-300">
                <i class="fa-solid fa-triangle-exclamation mr-1" aria-hidden="true"></i>Special instructions
            </p>
            <p class="mt-1 break-words text-base font-semibold text-amber-100">@o.Note</p>
        </div>
    }

    <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 border-t border-solid border-[#644234] px-4 pt-3 text-xs">
        <span class="rounded-md border border-solid border-[#644234] bg-[#4D3328] px-2 py-0.5 font-semibold @(o.PaymentStatus == PaymentStatus.Paid ? "text-emerald-300" : "text-[#FFD9A0]")">
            @o.PaymentMethod &middot; @o.PaymentStatus
        </span>
        <span class="text-sm font-extrabold text-[#FFD9A0]">&#8369;@o.Total.ToString("N2", inv)</span>
    </div>

    <div class="p-4">
        @if (o.Status == OrderStatus.Placed)
        {
            <form asp-controller="Barista" asp-action="Advance" asp-route-id="@o.Id" method="post" data-kds-form>
                <input type="hidden" name="returnTo" value="@ret" />
                <button type="submit" class="h-12 w-full rounded-xl bg-[#FFD9A0] text-base font-extrabold text-[#201A17] transition hover:brightness-110 disabled:opacity-60">
                    <i class="fa-solid fa-circle-check mr-2" aria-hidden="true"></i>Accept Order
                </button>
            </form>
        }
        else if (o.Status == OrderStatus.Preparing)
        {
            <form asp-controller="Barista" asp-action="Advance" asp-route-id="@o.Id" method="post" data-kds-form>
                <input type="hidden" name="returnTo" value="@ret" />
                <button type="submit" class="h-12 w-full rounded-xl bg-[#00754a] text-base font-extrabold text-white transition hover:bg-[#008a58] disabled:opacity-60">
                    <i class="fa-solid fa-check mr-2" aria-hidden="true"></i>Mark Ready / Completed
                </button>
            </form>
        }
        else
        {
            <button type="button" data-verify="@o.Id" class="h-12 w-full rounded-xl border-2 border-solid border-[#FFD9A0] bg-transparent text-base font-extrabold text-[#FFD9A0] transition hover:bg-[#FFD9A0] hover:text-[#201A17]">
                <i class="fa-solid fa-qrcode mr-2" aria-hidden="true"></i>Complete / Verify QR
            </button>
        }
    </div>
</article>

FILE: BrewStop\Views\Barista\_OrderList.cshtml

@model List<BrewStop.Models.Order>
<div class="tw kds-page">
    <div class="mx-auto w-full max-w-[1600px] px-4 pt-6 pb-6">

@model BrewStop.Models.CustomizeViewModel
@inject BrewStop.Services.IImageResolver Images
@using System.Globalization

@{
    ViewData["Title"] = "Customize " + Model.Item.Name;
    var inv = CultureInfo.InvariantCulture;
}

<div class="tw">
    <div class="mx-auto max-w-3xl px-4 pt-24 pb-12">
        <a asp-controller="Menu" asp-action="Index" class="text-sm text-caramel hover:underline">&larr; Back to menu</a>

        @if (TempData["CartError"] is string err)
        {
            <div class="mt-4 rounded-lg border border-red-500/40 bg-red-500/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-200">@err</div>
        }

        <form asp-controller="Cart" asp-action="Add" method="post" id="customizeForm"
              data-base="@Model.Item.Price.ToString(inv)"
              class="mt-4 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-card">
            <input type="hidden" name="MenuItemId" value="@Model.Item.Id" />

            <div class="flex flex-col gap-5 p-6 sm:flex-row">
                
                <img src="@Images.Resolve(Model.Item.ImageUrl, Model.Item.Name)" alt="@Model.Item.Name" class="h-40 w-full rounded-xl object-cover sm:w-40" />

                <div>
                    <h1 class="font-serif text-2xl font-bold text-cream">@Model.Item.Name</h1>
                    <p class="mt-1 text-sm text-neutral-300">@Model.Item.Description</p>
                    <p class="mt-2 text-lg font-semibold text-caramel">From &#8369;@Model.Item.Price.ToString("N2")</p>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div class="space-y-6 border-t border-white/10 p-6">
                @if (Model.IsDrink)
                {
                    <fieldset>
                        <legend class="mb-2 text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-cream">Size</legend>
                        <div class="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3">
                            @foreach (var s in Model.Sizes)
                            {
                                <label class="cursor-pointer">
                                    <input type="radio" name="SizeId" value="@s.Id" data-delta="@s.PriceDelta.ToString(inv)"
                                           class="peer sr-only" @(s.Id == Model.DefaultSizeId ? "checked" : "") />
                                    <span class="block rounded-xl border border-white/15 px-3 py-3 text-center text-sm text-cream peer-checked:border-caramel peer-checked:bg-caramel/10 peer-focus-visible:ring-2 peer-focus-visible:ring-caramel">
                                        @s.Name
                                        <span class="block text-xs text-neutral-400">@(s.PriceDelta == 0 ? "Base price" : "+\u20B1" + s.PriceDelta.ToString("N2"))</span>
                                    </span>
                                </label>
                            }
                        </div>
                    </fieldset>
                }

                @if (Model.AddOns.Count > 0)
                {
                    <fieldset>
                        <legend class="mb-2 text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-cream">Add-ons</legend>
                        <div class="space-y-2">
                            @foreach (var a in Model.AddOns)
                            {
                                <label class="flex cursor-pointer items-center justify-between rounded-xl border border-white/15 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-cream has-[:checked]:border-caramel has-[:checked]:bg-caramel/10">
                                    <span class="flex items-center gap-3">
                                        <input type="checkbox" name="AddOnIds" value="@a.Id" data-price="@a.Price.ToString(inv)" class="h-4 w-4 accent-[#d4a373]" />
                                        @a.Name
                                    </span>
                                    <span class="text-neutral-300">+&#8369;@a.Price.ToString("N2")</span>
                                </label>
                            }
                        </div>
                    </fieldset>
                }

                <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
                    <label for="Quantity" class="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-cream">Quantity</label>
                    <input id="Quantity" name="Quantity" type="number" min="1" max="10" value="1"
                           class="w-24 rounded-lg border border-white/15 bg-transparent px-3 py-2 text-center text-cream" />
                </div>
            </div>

            <div class="flex items-center justify-between border-t border-white/10 bg-black/20 p-6">
                <div>
                    <p class="text-xs uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-400">Estimated total</p>
                    <p class="text-2xl font-bold text-caramel">&#8369;<span id="liveTotal">@Model.Item.Price.ToString("N2")</span></p>
                </div>
                <button type="submit" class="rounded-full bg-caramel px-6 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-black hover:bg-cream">
                    Add to cart
                </button>
            </div>
        </form>
    </div>
</div>

@section Scripts {
    <script>
        (function() {
            const form = document.getElementById('customizeForm');
            const out = document.getElementById('liveTotal');
            const base = parseFloat(form.dataset.base);
            function update() {
                let unit = base;
                const size = form.querySelector('input[name="SizeId"]:checked');
                if (size) unit += parseFloat(size.dataset.delta);
                form.querySelectorAll('input[name="AddOnIds"]:checked').forEach(c => unit += parseFloat(c.dataset.price));
                const qty = Math.min(10, Math.max(1, parseInt(form.querySelector('#Quantity').value, 10) || 1));
                out.textContent = (unit * qty).toLocaleString('en-US', { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 });
            }
            form.addEventListener('change', update);
            form.addEventListener('input', update);
            update();
        })();
    </script>
}

@model BrewStop.Models.CartPageViewModel

    const string actionBtn = "inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full border px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold transition";
}

<div class="tw">

            {
                <p class="mt-5 rounded-xl border border-emerald-500/30 bg-emerald-500/10 p-3 text-sm text-emerald-300" role="status">@cartOk</p>

            {

                        Nothing brewing yet. Pick a drink or a fresh pastry and skip the queue at pickup.
                    </p>

                       class="mt-7 inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-r from-caramel to-[#e9c46a] px-8 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-black shadow-lg shadow-caramel/20 transition hover:brightness-110">
                        Explore Menu <i class="fa-solid fa-arrow-right text-xs"></i>
                    </a>
                </div>
            }
            else
            {
                @if (Model.HasStockProblem)
                {
                    <div class="mt-5 flex items-start gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-red-500/40 bg-red-500/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-200" role="alert">
                        <i class="fa-solid fa-triangle-exclamation mt-0.5"></i>
                        <span>
                            @(Model.HasSoldOut
                                ? "Some items are out of stock. Remove them or adjust quantities to continue to checkout."
                                : "Some items exceed the stock we have left. Lower the quantity to continue to checkout.")
                        </span>
                    </div>
                }

                <div class="mt-6 grid items-start gap-6 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_360px]">

                    <div class="min-w-0 space-y-10">

                        <!-- Cart items -->
                        <ul class="space-y-4">
                            @foreach (var l in Model.Lines)
                            {
                                var soldOut = l.StockLeft <= 0;
                                var itemQty = Model.Lines.Where(x => x.MenuItemId == l.MenuItemId).Sum(x => x.Quantity);
                                var plusDisabled = l.Quantity >= maxQty || itemQty >= l.StockLeft;

                                var hasDesc = Model.Descriptions.TryGetValue(l.MenuItemId, out var desc) && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(desc);

                                                        }

                                                        <input type="hidden" name="key" value="@l.Key" />
                                                        <input type="hidden" name="quantity" value="@(l.Quantity + 1)" />

                                                                data-focus-id="inc-@l.Key"
                                                                aria-label="Increase quantity of @l.Name"
                                                                class="@stepBtn">
                                                            <i class="fa-solid fa-plus text-xs"></i>
                                                        </button>

                                    </form>
                                </div>

                            else
                            {
                                <form asp-controller="Cart" asp-action="ApplyPromo" method="post" data-cart-form class="mt-3 flex gap-2">
                                    <label for="promoCode" class="sr-only">Promo code</label>

                    Remove
                </button>
            </div>
        </dialog>
    </div>
</div>

@section Scripts {
    <script>
        (function () {
            var dialog = document.getElementById('remove-dialog');
            var nameEl = document.getElementById('remove-name');
            var live = document.getElementById('cart-live');
            var pending = null;
            var busy = false;

            function root() { return document.getElementById('cart-root'); }

            function syncHeaderBadge(n) {
                var link = document.querySelector('a[aria-label^="Cart ("]');
                if (!link) return;
                link.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Cart (' + n + ' items)');
                var badge = link.querySelector('span');
                if (!badge) return;
                badge.textContent = n;
                badge.style.display = n > 0 ? '' : 'none';
            }

            async function submitCartForm(form) {
                if (busy) return;
                busy = true;

                var r = root();
                var active = document.activeElement;
                var focusId = active && active.dataset ? active.dataset.focusId : null;
                r.classList.add('opacity-60');

                try {
                    var res = await fetch(form.action, {
                        method: 'POST',
                        body: new FormData(form),
                        credentials: 'same-origin'
                    });
                    if (!res.ok) { HTMLFormElement.prototype.submit.call(form); return; }

                    var html = await res.text();
                    var fresh = new DOMParser().parseFromString(html, 'text/html').getElementById('cart-root');
                    if (!fresh) { window.location.assign(res.url); return; }

                    r.innerHTML = fresh.innerHTML;
                    r.dataset.count = fresh.dataset.count;
                    syncHeaderBadge(parseInt(fresh.dataset.count, 10) || 0);

                    if (focusId) {
                        var next = Array.prototype.find.call(r.querySelectorAll('[data-focus-id]'), function (el) {
                            return el.dataset.focusId === focusId && !el.disabled;
                        });
                        if (next) next.focus();
                    }
                    live.textContent = 'Cart updated';
                } catch (err) {
                    HTMLFormElement.prototype.submit.call(form);
                } finally {
                    r.classList.remove('opacity-60');
                    busy = false;
                }
            }

            async function toggleFavorite(form) {
                var btn = form.querySelector('[data-fav-btn]');
                if (btn.disabled) return;
                btn.disabled = true;

                try {
                    var res = await fetch(form.action, {
                        method: 'POST',
                        body: new FormData(form),
                        credentials: 'same-origin',
                        headers: { 'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest' }
                    });
                    var type = res.headers.get('content-type') || '';
                    if (!res.ok || type.indexOf('application/json') === -1) {
                        HTMLFormElement.prototype.submit.call(form);
                        return;
                    }

                    var data = await res.json();
                    document.querySelectorAll('[data-fav-form]').forEach(function (f) {
                        var idInput = f.querySelector('input[name="menuItemId"]');
                        if (!idInput || idInput.value !== String(data.menuItemId)) return;
                        var b = f.querySelector('[data-fav-btn]');
                        b.dataset.saved = data.saved ? 'true' : 'false';
                        b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', data.saved ? 'true' : 'false');
                    });
                    live.textContent = data.saved ? 'Saved to favorites' : 'Removed from favorites';
                } catch (err) {
                    HTMLFormElement.prototype.submit.call(form);
                } finally {
                    btn.disabled = false;
                }
            }

            document.addEventListener('submit', function (e) {
                var form = e.target;
                if (!(form instanceof HTMLFormElement)) return;

                if (form.hasAttribute('data-fav-form')) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    toggleFavorite(form);
                    return;
                }

                if (!form.hasAttribute('data-cart-form')) return;
                e.preventDefault();

                var itemName = form.getAttribute('data-confirm-remove');
                if (itemName !== null) {
                    if (dialog && typeof dialog.showModal === 'function') {
                        pending = form;
                        nameEl.textContent = itemName;
                        dialog.showModal();
                        document.getElementById('remove-cancel').focus();
                    } else if (window.confirm('Remove ' + itemName + ' from your cart?')) {
                        submitCartForm(form);
                    }
                    return;
                }

                submitCartForm(form);
            });

            document.getElementById('remove-cancel').addEventListener('click', function () {
                pending = null;
                dialog.close();
            });

            document.getElementById('remove-confirm').addEventListener('click', function () {
                var form = pending;
                pending = null;
                dialog.close();
                if (form) submitCartForm(form);
            });

            dialog.addEventListener('close', function () { pending = null; });
        })();
    </script>
}

@model BrewStop.Models.CheckoutViewModel
@inject BrewStop.Services.IPickupSlotService PickupSlots
@{
    ViewData["Title"] = "Checkout";

    var methods = (ViewData["Methods"] as IReadOnlyList<string>) ?? Array.Empty<string>();
    var chosen = ViewData["MethodChosen"] is true;
    var accepted = ViewData["AcceptTerms"] is true;
    var contact = ViewData["Contact"] as BrewStop.Controllers.CheckoutContact ?? new BrewStop.Controllers.CheckoutContact();

    string Norm(string s) => new string(s.Where(char.IsLetterOrDigit).ToArray()).ToLowerInvariant();

    var catalog = new Dictionary<string, (string Label, string Icon, string Hint, string Info)>
    {
        ["cash"] = ("Cash", "fa-solid fa-money-bill-wave", "Pay at the counter",
            "Pay the exact total at the counter when you pick up your order."),
        ["gcash"] = ("GCash", "fa-solid fa-mobile-screen-button", "Digital wallet",
            "Pay with your GCash wallet and enter your reference number below (optional). The barista verifies it at pickup."),
        ["maya"] = ("Maya", "fa-solid fa-wallet", "Digital wallet",
            "Pay with your Maya wallet and show the confirmation to the barista at pickup."),
        ["qrph"] = ("QR Ph", "fa-solid fa-qrcode", "Scan with any bank or e-wallet app",
            "Scan the QR Ph code at the counter using any participating bank or e-wallet app."),
        ["visa"] = ("Visa", "fa-brands fa-cc-visa", "Secure card payment",
            "You will complete payment on our secure payment gateway. BrewStop never collects or stores your card number."),
        ["mastercard"] = ("Mastercard", "fa-brands fa-cc-mastercard", "Secure card payment",
            "You will complete payment on our secure payment gateway. BrewStop never collects or stores your card number.")
    };

    var offered = methods
        .Select(m => (Name: m, Key: Norm(m)))
        .Where(m => catalog.ContainsKey(m.Key))
        .ToList();
    var selectedKey = chosen ? Norm(Model.Input.PaymentMethod.ToString()) : "";
    var isGcashSelected = selectedKey == "gcash";

    var slotGroups = Model.Slots.GroupBy(s => PickupSlots.ToLocal(s.Utc).Date).OrderBy(g => g.Key).ToList();
    var selectedSlot = Model.Slots.FirstOrDefault(s => s.Value == Model.Input.PickupSlot);
    DateTime? activeDate = selectedSlot is not null ? PickupSlots.ToLocal(selectedSlot.Utc).Date : slotGroups.FirstOrDefault()?.Key;
    var slotIndex = 0;

    const string cardCls = "rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-card p-5 sm:p-6";
    const string h2 = "font-serif text-xl font-bold text-cream";
    const string label = "mb-2 block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-cream/80";
    const string field = "w-full rounded-xl border border-white/10 bg-espresso px-3 py-3 text-cream placeholder:text-neutral-500 focus:border-caramel focus:outline-none";
    const string chip = "block rounded-xl border border-white/10 bg-espresso py-2.5 text-center text-sm text-cream transition hover:border-caramel peer-checked:border-caramel peer-checked:bg-caramel peer-checked:font-semibold peer-checked:text-espresso peer-focus-visible:ring-2 peer-focus-visible:ring-caramel";
    const string err = "mt-1 block text-sm text-red-300";
}

                    </button>
                    <p class="mt-3 text-center text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-500">
                        <i class="fa-solid fa-lock mr-1"></i> Card numbers are never collected or stored by BrewStop
                    </p>
                </div>

                <div class="rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-card/60 p-5 text-sm text-neutral-300">
                    <p class="flex items-center gap-2 font-semibold text-cream"><i class="fa-solid fa-mug-hot text-caramel"></i> Ready when you are</p>
                    <p class="mt-2 text-xs leading-relaxed text-neutral-400">Your order goes straight to our baristas. Show your QR code at the counter when it's ready.</p>
                </div>
            </aside>
        </form>
    </div>
</div>

@section Scripts {
    <partial name="_ValidationScriptsPartial" />
    <script>
        (function () {
            var form = document.getElementById('checkout-form');
            var btn = document.getElementById('place-btn');
            var spin = document.getElementById('place-spin');
            var terms = document.getElementById('terms');
            var subtotal = @Model.Cart.Total.ToString("0.00", System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
            var CHOSEN = @(chosen ? "true" : "false");
            var locked = false;

            function fmt(n) { return Number(n).toLocaleString('en-US', { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 }); }

            /* ---------- Payment collapsible ---------- */
            var radios = [].slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('input[name="Input.PaymentMethod"]'));
            var infos = [].slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('[data-pay-info]'));
            var toggle = document.getElementById('pay-toggle');
            var panel = document.getElementById('pay-panel');
            var chev = document.getElementById('pay-chevron');
            var ptitle = document.getElementById('pay-title');
            var psub = document.getElementById('pay-sub');
            var pchange = document.getElementById('pay-change');
            var picon = document.getElementById('pay-icon');

            if (!CHOSEN) radios.forEach(function (r) { r.checked = false; });

            function selected() { return radios.filter(function (r) { return r.checked; })[0] || null; }

            function setOpen(open) {
                panel.style.gridTemplateRows = open ? '1fr' : '0fr';
                panel.inert = !open;
                toggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', open ? 'true' : 'false');
                chev.style.transform = open ? 'rotate(180deg)' : '';
            }

            function renderMethod() {
                var r = selected();
                radios.forEach(function (x) {
                    var dot = x.parentElement.querySelector('[data-dot]');
                    if (dot) dot.classList.toggle('hidden', !x.checked);
                });
                infos.forEach(function (i) { i.classList.toggle('hidden', !r || i.dataset.payInfo !== r.dataset.method); });
                if (r) {
                    ptitle.textContent = 'Payment Method';
                    psub.textContent = r.dataset.label;
                    picon.innerHTML = '<i class="' + r.dataset.icon + '"></i>';
                    pchange.classList.remove('hidden');
                } else {
                    ptitle.textContent = 'Select Payment Method';
                    psub.textContent = 'Choose your preferred payment option';
                    picon.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-wallet"></i>';
                    pchange.classList.add('hidden');
                }
            }

            toggle.addEventListener('click', function () { setOpen(toggle.getAttribute('aria-expanded') !== 'true'); });
            radios.forEach(function (r) {
                r.addEventListener('change', function () {
                    renderMethod();
                    setTimeout(function () { setOpen(false); }, 250);
                    refresh();
                });
            });
            renderMethod();
            setOpen(false);

            /* ---------- Pickup date -> slot groups ---------- */
            var dateInput = document.getElementById('pickup-date');
            var groups = [].slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('[data-slot-date]'));
            var empty = document.getElementById('slot-empty');
            if (dateInput) {
                dateInput.addEventListener('change', function () {
                    var any = false;
                    groups.forEach(function (g) {
                        var show = g.dataset.slotDate === dateInput.value;
                        g.classList.toggle('hidden', !show);
                        if (show) any = true;
                        else g.querySelectorAll('input').forEach(function (i) { i.checked = false; });
                    });
                    empty.classList.toggle('hidden', any);
                    refresh();
                });
            }
            form.querySelectorAll('input[name="Input.PickupSlot"]').forEach(function (r) { r.addEventListener('change', refresh); });

            /* ---------- Promo (read-only preview; server re-validates on submit) ---------- */
            var promo = document.getElementById('Input_PromoCode');
            var applyBtn = document.getElementById('promo-apply');
            var msg = document.getElementById('promo-msg');
            var row = document.getElementById('promo-row');
            var rowLabel = document.getElementById('promo-row-label');
            var rowValue = document.getElementById('promo-row-value');
            var totalEl = document.getElementById('total-value');
            var placeTotal = document.getElementById('place-total');

            function setTotal(t) { totalEl.textContent = fmt(t); placeTotal.textContent = fmt(t); }
            function showMsg(text, ok) {
                msg.textContent = text;
                msg.className = 'mt-2 text-sm ' + (ok ? 'text-emerald-300' : 'text-red-300');
            }
            function clearPromo() {
                row.classList.add('hidden'); row.classList.remove('flex');
                msg.className = 'mt-2 hidden text-sm';
                setTotal(subtotal);
            }

            promo.addEventListener('input', clearPromo);
            applyBtn.addEventListener('click', function () {
                var code = promo.value.trim();
                if (!code) { clearPromo(); showMsg('Enter a promo code.', false); return; }
                var token = form.querySelector('input[name="__RequestVerificationToken"]');
                applyBtn.disabled = true;
                fetch('@Url.Action("PreviewPromo", "Checkout")', {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: {
                        'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded',
                        'RequestVerificationToken': token ? token.value : ''
                    },
                    body: 'code=' + encodeURIComponent(code)
                })
                    .then(function (res) { if (!res.ok) throw new Error(); return res.json(); })
                    .then(function (d) {
                        if (d.ok) {
                            rowLabel.textContent = 'Promo ' + d.code;
                            rowValue.textContent = '\u2212\u20B1' + fmt(d.discount);
                            row.classList.remove('hidden'); row.classList.add('flex');
                            setTotal(d.total);
                            showMsg(d.message, true);
                        } else {
                            clearPromo();
                            showMsg(d.message, false);
                        }
                    })
                    .catch(function () { clearPromo(); showMsg('Could not check the promo code. Please try again.', false); })
                    .then(function () { applyBtn.disabled = false; });
            });

            /* ---------- Note counter ---------- */
            var note = document.getElementById('Input_Note');
            var cnt = document.getElementById('note-count');
            function count() { cnt.textContent = note.value.length; }
            note.addEventListener('input', count);
            count();

            /* ---------- Submit state ---------- */
            function valid() {
                return !locked
                    && !!form.querySelector('input[name="Input.PickupSlot"]:checked')
                    && !!selected()
                    && terms.checked;
            }
            function refresh() { btn.disabled = !valid(); }
            terms.addEventListener('change', refresh);
            refresh();

            form.addEventListener('submit', function (e) {
                if (locked || !valid()) { e.preventDefault(); return; }
                if (window.jQuery && jQuery.fn.valid && !jQuery(form).valid()) return;
                locked = true;
                btn.disabled = true;
                spin.classList.remove('hidden');
                document.getElementById('place-label').textContent = 'Placing order\u2026';
            });

            window.addEventListener('pageshow', function (e) {
                if (e.persisted) {
                    locked = false;
                    spin.classList.add('hidden');
                    document.getElementById('place-label').innerHTML = 'Place Order &mdash; &#8369;<span id="place-total">' + fmt(subtotal) + '</span>';
                    placeTotal = document.getElementById('place-total');
                    refresh();
                }
            });
        })();
    </script>
}

FILE: BrewStop\Views\Checkout\_CheckoutSteps.cshtml

@model int
@{
    var steps = new[] { "Cart", "Checkout", "Confirmation" };
}
<ol class="mx-auto flex max-w-xl items-start" aria-label="Checkout progress">
    @for (var i = 0; i < steps.Length; i++)
    {
        var state = i < Model ? "done" : i == Model ? "active" : "todo";
        <li data-state="@state" class="group relative flex flex-1 flex-col items-center text-center">
            @if (i > 0)
            {
                <span class="absolute right-1/2 top-4 h-px w-full bg-white/15 group-data-[state=done]:bg-caramel group-data-[state=active]:bg-caramel"></span>
            }
            <span class="relative z-10 grid size-8 place-items-center rounded-full border border-white/20 bg-espresso text-xs font-bold text-neutral-500 group-data-[state=active]:border-caramel group-data-[state=active]:bg-caramel group-data-[state=active]:text-espresso group-data-[state=done]:border-caramel group-data-[state=done]:text-caramel">
                <i class="fa-solid fa-check hidden group-data-[state=done]:inline"></i>
                <span class="group-data-[state=done]:hidden">@(i + 1)</span>
            </span>
            @if (i == 0)
            {
                <a href="@Url.Action("Index", "Cart")" class="mt-2 text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-500 no-underline group-data-[state=done]:text-caramel hover:text-cream sm:text-xs">@steps[i]</a>
            }
            else
            {
                <span class="mt-2 text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-500 group-data-[state=active]:text-cream group-data-[state=done]:text-caramel sm:text-xs">@steps[i]</span>
            }
        </li>
    }
</ol>

                        var shown = o.Items.Take(3).ToList();
                        var more = o.Items.Count - shown.Count;
                        var statusMsg = o.Status switch
                        {
                            OrderStatus.Placed => "We've got your order. A barista will start on it shortly.",
                            OrderStatus.Preparing => "Your order is being handcrafted right now.",
                            OrderStatus.Ready => "Ready for pickup! Show your QR code at the counter.",

                        };

                        <article class="rounded-3xl border border-caramel/30 bg-card p-5 shadow-lg shadow-black/20 sm:p-6">

                                    <partial name="~/Views/Order/_StatusBadge.cshtml" model="o" />
                                </div>
                                <p class="shrink-0 text-xl font-bold text-caramel">&#8369;@o.Total.ToString("N2")</p>

                                <i class="fa-solid fa-bell-concierge mt-0.5 text-caramel"></i>
                                <span>@statusMsg</span>
                            </p>

                            <div class="mt-4 flex gap-4">
                                <div class="flex shrink-0 -space-x-4 self-start">
                                    @foreach (var i in shown)
                                    {
                                        <img src="@Images.Resolve(i.MenuItem?.ImageUrl, i.ItemName)" alt="@i.ItemName" loading="lazy"
                                             class="size-16 rounded-2xl object-cover ring-2 ring-card sm:size-20" />
                                    }
                                    @if (more > 0)
                                    {
                                        <span class="grid size-16 place-items-center rounded-2xl bg-espresso text-xs font-bold text-caramel ring-2 ring-card sm:size-20">+@more</span>
                                    }
                                </div>

                                <ul class="min-w-0 flex-1 space-y-2">
                                    @foreach (var i in o.Items)
                                    {
                                        <li class="min-w-0">
                                            <p class="truncate text-sm font-semibold text-cream">@i.Quantity&times; @i.ItemName</p>
                                            @if (i.SizeName is not null || i.AddOns.Count > 0)
                                            {
                                                <div class="mt-1 flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                                                    @if (i.SizeName is not null)
                                                    {
                                                        <span class="rounded-full bg-white/5 px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] text-neutral-300">@i.SizeName</span>
                                                    }
                                                    @foreach (var a in i.AddOns)
                                                    {
                                                        <span class="rounded-full bg-caramel/10 px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] text-caramel">+ @a.Name</span>
                                                    }
                                                </div>
                                            }
                                        </li>
                                    }
                                </ul>
                            </div>

                            <dl class="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-x-6 gap-y-1 text-xs text-neutral-400">

</div>

@section Scripts {
    @if (Model.ActiveCount > 0)
    {
        <partial name="~/Views/Order/_OrderLive.cshtml" />
        <script>
            (function() {
                var MSG = {
                    Placed: "We've got your order. A barista will start on it shortly.",
                    Preparing: 'Your order is being handcrafted right now.',
                    Ready: 'Ready for pickup! Show your QR code at the counter.',
                    Claimed: 'Picked up. Enjoy your coffee!'
                };
                document.querySelectorAll('[data-order-status]').forEach(function(badge) {
                    new MutationObserver(function() {
                        var wrap = document.querySelector('[data-order-message="' + badge.dataset.orderStatus + '"]');
                        var text = MSG[badge.dataset.status];
                        var span = wrap ? wrap.querySelector('span') : null;
                        if (span && text) span.textContent = text;
                    }).observe(badge, { attributes: true, attributeFilter: ['data-status'] });
                });
            })();
        </script>
    }
}

@section Scripts {
    <script>
        async function startOrderHubConnection() {
            const badge = document.getElementById("privacy-connection-badge");
            try {
                const connection = new signalR.HubConnectionBuilder()
                    .withUrl("/hubs/orders")
                    .withAutomaticReconnect()
                    .build();

                connection.on("OrderStatusChanged", update => {
                    console.log("[Privacy Page] Live Status update:", update);
                });

                await connection.start();
                console.log("SignalR Connection Successful on Privacy Page!");
                badge.className = "badge bg-dark text-white rounded-pill px-3 py-2 d-flex align-items-center gap-2 shadow-sm";
                badge.innerHTML = '<span class="pulse-dot"></span> Active Stream';
            } catch (err) {
                console.error("SignalR Connection Error: ", err);
                badge.className = "badge bg-danger text-white rounded-pill px-3 py-2 shadow-sm";
                badge.innerText = "Offline";
            }
        }

        document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", startOrderHubConnection);
    </script>
}

    const string label = "mb-1 block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-400";
    const string field = "w-full rounded-xl border border-white/10 bg-espresso px-4 py-2.5 text-sm text-cream placeholder:text-neutral-500 focus:border-caramel focus:outline-none";
    const string err = "mt-1 block text-xs text-red-400";
    var faqs = new (string Q, string A)[]
    {
        ("How do I place a pickup order?", "Browse the menu, customize your drink, add it to your cart, choose a 10-minute pickup slot at checkout and submit. You'll get a QR code to show at the counter."),
        ("How do I pay?", "Choose Cash or GCash at checkout. Payment is settled at the counter when you pick up your order; there is no online payment gateway."),
        ("Can I cancel my order?", "Yes, but only while the order is still Placed. Once the barista starts preparing it, it can no longer be cancelled."),
        ("My QR code was rejected. What now?", "Each QR code works only once and is invalid after cancellation or expiry. Ask the barista to look up your order number."),
        ("How do I reorder a previous drink?", "Open My Orders, then press Reorder on a past order. Items that are sold out are skipped and you'll be told which ones."),
        ("How do I change my password or delete my account?", "Open the profile icon in the top bar. You'll find Change Password and Delete Account there.")
    };
}

<style>
    .faq summary::-webkit-details-marker {
        display: none;
    }

    .faq details[open] .faq-chev {
        transform: rotate(180deg);
    }

    .faq-chev {
        transition: transform .2s ease;
    }
</style>

<div class="tw">

            <div class="mt-4 space-y-3">
                @foreach (var f in faqs)
                {
                    <details class="rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-card px-5 py-4">

                            <span>@f.Q</span>
                            <i class="fa-solid fa-chevron-down faq-chev text-caramel" aria-hidden="true"></i>
                        </summary>
                        <p class="mt-3 text-sm leading-relaxed text-neutral-300">@f.A</p>
                    </details>
                }
            </div>
        </section>

        <section id="contact" class="mt-12 scroll-mt-24" aria-labelledby="contact-h">
            <h2 id="contact-h" class="font-serif text-xl font-semibold text-caramel">Contact us</h2>

            @if (TempData["Success"] is string ok)
            {
                <p class="mt-4 rounded-xl border border-emerald-500/30 bg-emerald-500/10 p-3 text-sm text-emerald-300" role="status">@ok</p>
            }

            <form asp-action="Support" method="post" class="mt-4 space-y-4 rounded-3xl border border-white/10 bg-card p-6">
                <div asp-validation-summary="ModelOnly" class="text-sm text-red-400"></div>
                <div class="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
                    <div>
                        <label asp-for="Name" class="@label"></label>
                        <input asp-for="Name" maxlength="100" autocomplete="name" class="@field" />
                        <span asp-validation-for="Name" class="@err"></span>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <label asp-for="Email" class="@label"></label>
                        <input asp-for="Email" type="email" maxlength="150" autocomplete="email" class="@field" />
                        <span asp-validation-for="Email" class="@err"></span>
                    </div>
                </div>
                <div>
                    <label asp-for="Topic" class="@label"></label>
                    <select asp-for="Topic" class="@field">
                        <option>Order issue</option>
                        <option>Payment question</option>
                        <option>Account help</option>
                        <option>Feedback</option>
                        <option>Other</option>
                    </select>
                    <span asp-validation-for="Topic" class="@err"></span>
                </div>
                <div>
                    <label asp-for="Message" class="@label"></label>
                    <textarea asp-for="Message" rows="5" maxlength="1000" class="@field"></textarea>
                    <span asp-validation-for="Message" class="@err"></span>
                </div>
                <button type="submit" class="rounded-full bg-caramel px-7 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-black transition hover:bg-cream">Send message</button>
            </form>
        </section>
    </div>
</div>

========================================
FILE: BrewStop\Views\Menu\Details.cshtml
========================================
@model BrewStop.Models.MenuItemDetailsViewModel
@{
    var item = Model.Item;
    ViewData["Title"] = item.Name;
    var returnUrl = Context.Request.Path + Context.Request.QueryString;
    var total = Model.Item.ReviewCount;
    var isBarista = User.IsInRole(AppRoles.Barista);
    var refSizes = (ViewBag.RefSizes as List<BrewStop.Models.ItemSize>) ?? new List<BrewStop.Models.ItemSize>();
    var refAddOns = (ViewBag.RefAddOns as List<BrewStop.Models.AddOn>) ?? new List<BrewStop.Models.AddOn>();
    var refGroup = (ViewBag.RefGroup as string) ?? "Drinks";
}

<div class="tw">
    <div class="mx-auto max-w-5xl px-4 pt-24 pb-16">
        <a asp-action="Index" class="text-sm font-semibold text-caramel hover:text-cream">&larr; Back to menu</a>

        <div class="mt-4 grid gap-8 md:grid-cols-2">
            <div class="relative aspect-[4/3] overflow-hidden rounded-3xl border border-white/10 bg-espresso">
                <img src="@item.ImageUrl" alt="@item.Name" referrerpolicy="no-referrer"
                     class="size-full object-cover @(item.IsAvailable ? "" : "grayscale opacity-60")"
                     onerror="this.onerror=null;this.src='@Url.Content("~/images/placeholder.svg")'" />
                @if (!item.IsAvailable)
                {
                    <span class="absolute left-3 top-3 rounded-full bg-red-600 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-white">Out of stock</span>
                }
                @if (Model.CanFavorite)
                {
                    <form asp-controller="Favorites" asp-action="Toggle" method="post" data-fav-form class="absolute bottom-3 right-3">
                        <input type="hidden" name="menuItemId" value="@item.Id" />
                        <input type="hidden" name="returnUrl" value="@returnUrl" />
                        <button type="submit" aria-pressed="@(item.IsFavorite ? "true" : "false")" aria-label="Favorite @item.Name"
                                class="grid size-10 place-items-center rounded-full bg-black/60 text-lg backdrop-blur transition hover:bg-black/80 @(item.IsFavorite ? "text-rose-400" : "text-white")">
                            <i class="@(item.IsFavorite ? "fa-solid" : "fa-regular") fa-heart"></i>
                        </button>
                    </form>
                }
            </div>

            <div class="flex flex-col">
                <p class="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-caramel">@item.CategoryName</p>
                <h1 class="mt-1 font-serif text-3xl font-bold text-cream">@item.Name</h1>
                <div class="mt-2"><partial name="_RatingBadge" model="item" /></div>
                <p class="mt-4 text-2xl font-bold text-caramel">&#8369;@item.Price.ToString("N2")</p>
                <p class="mt-4 text-sm leading-relaxed text-neutral-300">@item.Description</p>

                <p class="mt-4 text-xs font-semibold @(!item.IsAvailable ? "text-red-300" : item.Stock <= 5 ? "text-yellow-300" : "text-emerald-300")">
                    @if (!item.IsAvailable)
                    {
                        <text>Out of stock</text>
                    }
                    else if (item.Stock <= 5)
                    {
                        <text>Only @item.Stock left</text>
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        <text>In stock</text>
                    }
                </p>

                <div class="mt-auto pt-6">
                    @if (isBarista)
                    {
                        <partial name="_BaristaReference" model="(refGroup, refSizes, refAddOns)" />
                    }
                    else if (item.IsAvailable)
                    {
                        <a asp-controller="Cart" asp-action="Customize" asp-route-id="@item.Id"
                           class="block rounded-full bg-caramel px-6 py-3 text-center text-sm font-semibold text-black transition hover:bg-cream">
                            <i class="fa-solid fa-mug-hot me-1"></i> Customize &amp; Order
                        </a>
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        <button type="button" disabled aria-disabled="true"
                                class="w-full cursor-not-allowed rounded-full bg-white/10 px-6 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-neutral-500">
                            Unavailable
                        </button>
                    }
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <section class="mt-12 rounded-3xl border border-white/10 bg-card p-5 sm:p-6" aria-labelledby="reviews-h">
            <h2 id="reviews-h" class="font-serif text-xl font-semibold text-cream">Customer reviews</h2>

            @if (total == 0)
            {
                <p class="mt-3 text-sm text-neutral-400">No reviews yet. Order this and be the first to rate it!</p>
            }
            else
            {
                <div class="mt-4 grid gap-6 sm:grid-cols-[160px_1fr]">
                    <div class="text-center sm:text-left">
                        <p class="text-5xl font-bold text-cream">@item.RatingText</p>
                        <p class="mt-1 text-amber-400" aria-hidden="true">@(new string('?', (int)Math.Round(item.AverageRating)))<span class="text-neutral-600">@(new string('?', 5 - (int)Math.Round(item.AverageRating)))</span></p>
                        <p class="mt-1 text-xs text-neutral-400">@total review@(total == 1 ? "" : "s")</p>
                    </div>
                    <ul class="space-y-1.5">
                        @for (var star = 5; star >= 1; star--)
                        {
                            var count = Model.Distribution[star - 1];
                            var pct = (int)Math.Round(100.0 * count / total);
                            <li class="flex items-center gap-3 text-xs text-neutral-300">
                                <span class="w-10 shrink-0">@star &#9733;</span>
                                <span class="h-2 flex-1 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-white/10">
                                    <span class="block h-full rounded-full bg-amber-400" style="width:@(pct)%"></span>
                                </span>
                                <span class="w-6 shrink-0 text-right text-neutral-400">@count</span>
                            </li>
                        }
                    </ul>
                </div>

                <ul class="mt-6 divide-y divide-white/10 border-t border-white/10">
                    @foreach (var r in Model.Reviews)
                    {
                        <li class="py-4">
                            <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
                                <p class="text-sm font-semibold text-cream">@r.Author</p>
                                <p class="text-xs text-neutral-500">@r.CreatedAtUtc.ToString("MMM d, yyyy")</p>
                            </div>
                            <p class="mt-0.5 text-sm text-amber-400" aria-label="@r.Rating out of 5 stars">
                                @(new string('?', r.Rating))<span class="text-neutral-600">@(new string('?', 5 - r.Rating))</span>
                            </p>
                            @if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(r.Comment))
                            {
                                <p class="mt-1 whitespace-pre-line text-sm text-neutral-300">@r.Comment</p>
                            }
                            <p class="mt-1 text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-emerald-300/80">
                                <i class="fa-solid fa-circle-check me-1"></i>Verified purchase
                            </p>
                        </li>
                    }
                </ul>
            }
        </section>
    </div>
</div>

FILE: BrewStop\Views\Menu\Index.cshtml

@using System.Globalization
@using System.Text.Json
@model BrewStop.Models.MenuViewModel
@{
    ViewData["Title"] = "Menu";
    var isCustomer = User.IsInRole(AppRoles.Student) || User.IsInRole(AppRoles.Faculty);
    var isBarista = User.IsInRole(AppRoles.Barista);
    var refSizes = (ViewBag.RefSizes as List<BrewStop.Models.ItemSize>) ?? new List<BrewStop.Models.ItemSize>();
    var refAddOns = (ViewBag.RefAddOns as List<BrewStop.Models.AddOn>) ?? new List<BrewStop.Models.AddOn>();
    var returnUrl = Context.Request.Path + Context.Request.QueryString;
    var inv = CultureInfo.InvariantCulture;
    var activeIsDrinks = Model.ActiveGroup == "Drinks";

    var hasDrinkAddOns = Model.AddOns.Any(a => string.Equals(a.Group?.Trim(), "Drinks", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
    var hasPastryAddOns = Model.AddOns.Any(a => string.Equals(a.Group?.Trim(), "Pastries", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));

    var optionsJson = JsonSerializer.Serialize(new
    {
        sizes = Model.Sizes.Select(s => new { id = s.Id, name = s.Name, price = s.Price }),
        addOns = Model.AddOns.Select(a => new { id = a.Id, name = a.Name, price = a.Price, group = a.Group })
    }, new JsonSerializerOptions { PropertyNamingPolicy = JsonNamingPolicy.CamelCase });

    const string tabBase = "relative z-10 flex flex-1 flex-col items-start gap-0.5 rounded-2xl px-5 py-3 text-left transition-colors duration-300 focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-caramel";
}

<div class="tw">

    @* ============ HERO ============ *@
    <header class="relative isolate overflow-hidden bg-espresso">
        @if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(Model.HeroImageUrl))
        {
            <img src="@Model.HeroImageUrl" alt="@Model.HeroImageAlt" referrerpolicy="no-referrer"
                 class="absolute inset-0 -z-10 size-full object-cover opacity-60"
                 onerror="this.onerror=null;this.src='@Url.Content("~/images/placeholder.svg")'" />
        }
        <div class="absolute inset-0 -z-10 bg-gradient-to-t from-[#1a0f0d] via-[#1a0f0d]/80 to-[#1a0f0d]/30"></div>
        <div class="absolute inset-0 -z-10 bg-gradient-to-r from-[#1a0f0d]/90 via-transparent to-transparent"></div>

        <div class="mx-auto max-w-6xl px-4 pb-14 pt-32 sm:pt-40">
            <p class="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.3em] text-caramel">BrewStop Caf&eacute;</p>
            <h1 class="mt-3 font-serif text-5xl font-bold leading-tight text-cream sm:text-6xl">Our Menu</h1>
            <p class="mt-3 max-w-xl text-lg text-[#f5efe6]/80">Find your perfect everyday brew.</p>
        </div>
    </header>

    <div class="bg-espresso">
        <div class="mx-auto max-w-6xl px-4 pb-20">

            @if (TempData["MenuError"] is string menuErr)
            {
                <div class="mt-6 flex items-start gap-3 rounded-xl border border-yellow-500/40 bg-yellow-500/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-yellow-200">
                    <i class="fa-solid fa-triangle-exclamation mt-0.5"></i><span>@menuErr</span>
                </div>
            }

            @* ============ CONTROL BAR ============ *@
            <div class="sticky top-0 z-20 -mx-4 border-b border-white/10 bg-[#1a0f0d]/95 px-4 py-4 backdrop-blur">
                <div class="flex flex-col gap-4 lg:flex-row lg:items-center lg:justify-between">

                    <div id="menu-tabs" role="tablist" aria-label="Menu sections"
                         class="relative flex w-full rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-card p-1 lg:max-w-xl">
                        <span id="tab-indicator" aria-hidden="true"
                              class="absolute left-0 top-1 bottom-1 z-0 rounded-2xl bg-caramel shadow-lg transition-all duration-300 ease-out"
                              style="width:0;transform:translateX(0)"></span>
                        <button type="button" role="tab" data-tab="drinks" id="tab-drinks" aria-controls="panel-drinks"
                                aria-selected="@(activeIsDrinks ? "true" : "false")"
                                class="@tabBase @(activeIsDrinks ? "text-black" : "text-cream")">
                            <span class="font-serif text-lg font-semibold"><i class="fa-solid fa-mug-hot me-1.5 text-sm"></i>Drinks</span>
                            <span class="text-xs opacity-80">Coffee, espresso and refreshing brews</span>
                        </button>
                        <button type="button" role="tab" data-tab="pastries" id="tab-pastries" aria-controls="panel-pastries"
                                aria-selected="@(activeIsDrinks ? "false" : "true")"
                                class="@tabBase @(activeIsDrinks ? "text-cream" : "text-black")">
                            <span class="font-serif text-lg font-semibold"><i class="fa-solid fa-cookie-bite me-1.5 text-sm"></i>Pastries</span>
                            <span class="text-xs opacity-80">Freshly baked sweet treats</span>
                        </button>
                    </div>

                    <form id="menu-form" method="get" asp-action="Index" role="search"
                          class="flex flex-1 flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row lg:max-w-xl">
                        <input type="hidden" name="group" id="f-group" value="@Model.ActiveGroup" />
                        <div class="relative flex-1">
                            <label for="menu-search" class="sr-only">Search the menu</label>
                            <i class="fa-solid fa-magnifying-glass pointer-events-none absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-sm text-caramel"></i>
                            <input id="menu-search" type="search" name="searchTerm" value="@Model.SearchTerm" maxlength="100"
                                   autocomplete="off" placeholder="Search drinks or pastries..."
                                   class="w-full rounded-full border border-white/15 bg-card py-3 pl-11 pr-4 text-sm text-cream placeholder:text-neutral-500 transition focus:border-caramel focus:outline-none" />
                        </div>
                        <div class="relative sm:w-52">
                            <label for="menu-sort" class="sr-only">Sort by</label>
                            <select id="menu-sort" name="sort"
                                    class="w-full appearance-none rounded-full border border-white/15 bg-card py-3 pl-4 pr-10 text-sm text-cream transition focus:border-caramel focus:outline-none">
                                <option value="popularity" selected="@(Model.Sort == "popularity")">Popularity (Best Sellers)</option>
                                <option value="price_asc" selected="@(Model.Sort == "price_asc")">Price: Low to High</option>
                                <option value="price_desc" selected="@(Model.Sort == "price_desc")">Price: High to Low</option>
                            </select>
                            <i class="fa-solid fa-chevron-down pointer-events-none absolute right-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-xs text-caramel"></i>
                        </div>
                        <noscript>
                            <button type="submit" class="rounded-full bg-caramel px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-black">Apply</button>
                        </noscript>
                    </form>
                </div>
            </div>

            @* ============ DRINKS PANEL ============ *@
            <section id="panel-drinks" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="tab-drinks" data-panel="drinks"
                     class="@(activeIsDrinks ? "" : "hidden") pt-10 transition-opacity duration-300">
                <div class="mb-6 flex items-end justify-between gap-3">
                    <div>
                        <h2 class="font-serif text-3xl font-bold text-cream">Drinks</h2>
                        <p class="mt-1 text-sm text-neutral-400">Coffee, espresso and refreshing brews</p>
                    </div>
                    <span class="rounded-full bg-white/5 px-3 py-1 text-xs text-neutral-300" data-count>@Model.Drinks.Count items</span>
                </div>

                <div data-grid class="grid gap-6 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
                    @foreach (var item in Model.Drinks)
                    {
                        var itemAddOns = refAddOns.Where(a => string.Equals(a.Group?.Trim(), item.Group?.Trim(), StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)).ToList();
                        <article data-card data-id="@item.Id" data-group="drinks"
                                 data-name="@item.Name" data-desc="@item.Description"
                                 data-price="@item.Price.ToString("0.00", inv)" data-img="@item.ImageUrl"
                                 data-stock="@item.Stock" data-badge="@item.BadgeText"
                                 data-pop="@item.Popularity.ToString("0.000", inv)"
                                 data-search="@((item.Name + " " + item.Description + " " + item.CategoryName).ToLowerInvariant())"
                                 class="group relative flex flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-3xl border border-white/10 bg-card transition duration-300 hover:-translate-y-1 hover:border-caramel/50 hover:shadow-2xl hover:shadow-black/40">

                            <div class="relative aspect-[4/5] overflow-hidden bg-espresso">
                                @if (isBarista)
                                {
                                    <img src="@item.ImageUrl" alt="@item.Name" loading="lazy" referrerpolicy="no-referrer"
                                         class="size-full object-cover transition duration-700 group-hover:scale-105 @(item.IsAvailable ? "" : "grayscale opacity-60")"
                                         onerror="this.onerror=null;this.src='@Url.Content("~/images/placeholder.svg")'" />
                                }
                                else
                                {
                                    <button type="button" data-open aria-label="View @item.Name" class="block size-full text-left">
                                        <img src="@item.ImageUrl" alt="@item.Name" loading="lazy" referrerpolicy="no-referrer"
                                             class="size-full object-cover transition duration-700 group-hover:scale-105 @(item.IsAvailable ? "" : "grayscale opacity-60")"
                                             onerror="this.onerror=null;this.src='@Url.Content("~/images/placeholder.svg")'" />
                                    </button>
                                }
                                <div class="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-[#1a0f0d] via-[#1a0f0d]/20 to-transparent"></div>

                                <div class="absolute left-3 top-3 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                                    @if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(item.BadgeText))
                                    {
                                        <span class="rounded-full bg-black/60 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-caramel backdrop-blur">
                                            <i class="fa-solid fa-star me-1 text-[10px]"></i>@item.BadgeText
                                        </span>
                                    }
                                </div>

                                <div class="absolute right-3 top-3">
                                    @if (item.IsAvailable)
                                    {
                                        <span class="rounded-full bg-emerald-500/20 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-emerald-300 backdrop-blur">Available</span>
                                    }
                                    else
                                    {
                                        <span class="rounded-full bg-red-600 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-white">Sold Out</span>
                                    }
                                </div>

                                @if (isCustomer)
                                {
                                    <form asp-controller="Favorites" asp-action="Toggle" method="post" data-fav-form
                                          class="absolute bottom-3 right-3">
                                        <input type="hidden" name="menuItemId" value="@item.Id" />
                                        <input type="hidden" name="returnUrl" value="@returnUrl" />
                                        <button type="submit" aria-pressed="@(item.IsFavorite ? "true" : "false")"
                                                aria-label="Favorite @item.Name"
                                                class="grid size-10 place-items-center rounded-full bg-black/60 text-base backdrop-blur transition hover:scale-110 hover:bg-black/80 @(item.IsFavorite ? "text-rose-400" : "text-white")">
                                            <i class="@(item.IsFavorite ? "fa-solid" : "fa-regular") fa-heart"></i>
                                        </button>
                                    </form>
                                }
                                else if (User.Identity?.IsAuthenticated != true)
                                {
                                    <a asp-controller="Account" asp-action="Login" asp-route-returnUrl="@returnUrl"
                                       aria-label="Sign in to save @item.Name"
                                       class="absolute bottom-3 right-3 grid size-10 place-items-center rounded-full bg-black/60 text-base text-white backdrop-blur transition hover:scale-110 hover:bg-black/80">
                                        <i class="fa-regular fa-heart"></i>
                                    </a>
                                }

                                <div class="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-3 left-3 flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                                    @if (Model.Sizes.Count > 0)
                                    {
                                        <span class="rounded-full border border-caramel/40 bg-black/50 px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-semibold text-caramel backdrop-blur">
                                            <i class="fa-solid fa-ruler-vertical me-1"></i>Sizes
                                        </span>
                                    }
                                    @if (hasDrinkAddOns)
                                    {
                                        <span class="rounded-full border border-caramel/40 bg-black/50 px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-semibold text-caramel backdrop-blur">
                                            <i class="fa-solid fa-plus me-1"></i>Add-Ons
                                        </span>
                                    }
                                </div>
                            </div>

                            <div class="flex flex-1 flex-col p-5">
                                <div class="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                                    <h3 class="font-serif text-xl font-semibold text-cream">
                                        @if (isBarista)
                                        {
                                            <a asp-action="Details" asp-route-id="@item.Id" class="transition hover:text-caramel">@item.Name</a>
                                        }
                                        else
