<script id="menu-options" type="application/json">@Html.Raw(optionsJson)</script>
<script>
    (function() {
        var tabs = document.querySelectorAll('#menu-tabs [data-tab]');
        var indicator = document.getElementById('tab-indicator');
        var groupInput = document.getElementById('f-group');
        var sort = document.getElementById('menu-sort');
        var form = document.getElementById('menu-form');
        var search = document.getElementById('menu-search');

        function moveIndicator(tab) {
            if (!tab || !indicator) return;
            indicator.style.width = tab.offsetWidth + 'px';
            indicator.style.transform = 'translateX(' + tab.offsetLeft + 'px)';
        }

        function activate(name) {
            tabs.forEach(function(t) {
                var on = t.dataset.tab === name;
                t.setAttribute('aria-selected', on ? 'true' : 'false');
                t.classList.toggle('text-black', on);
                t.classList.toggle('text-cream', !on);
                var panel = document.getElementById('panel-' + t.dataset.tab);
                if (panel) panel.classList.toggle('hidden', !on);
                if (on) moveIndicator(t);
            });
            if (groupInput) groupInput.value = name === 'drinks' ? 'Drinks' : 'Pastries';
        }

        tabs.forEach(function(t) {
            t.addEventListener('click', function() { activate(t.dataset.tab); });
        });

        var current = document.querySelector('#menu-tabs [aria-selected="true"]');
        if (current) activate(current.dataset.tab);
        window.addEventListener('resize', function() {
            var c = document.querySelector('#menu-tabs [aria-selected="true"]');
            if (c) moveIndicator(c);
        });

        if (sort && form) sort.addEventListener('change', function() { form.submit(); });

        /* live search */
        if (search) {
            search.addEventListener('input', function() {
                var q = search.value.trim().toLowerCase();
                document.querySelectorAll('[data-panel]').forEach(function(panel) {
                    var shown = 0;
                    panel.querySelectorAll('[data-card]').forEach(function(card) {
                        var hit = !q || (card.dataset.search || '').indexOf(q) !== -1;
                        card.classList.toggle('hidden', !hit);
                        if (hit) shown++;
                    });
                    var count = panel.querySelector('[data-count]');
                    if (count) count.textContent = shown + ' items';
                    var empty = panel.querySelector('[data-empty]');
                    if (empty) empty.classList.toggle('hidden', shown !== 0);
                });
            });
        }

        /* customize modal */
        var modal = document.getElementById('menu-modal');
        if (!modal) return;

        var opts = { sizes: [], addOns: [] };
        try { opts = JSON.parse(document.getElementById('menu-options').textContent) || opts; } catch (e) { }

        var el = {
            id: document.getElementById('mm-id'),
            img: document.getElementById('mm-img'),
            badge: document.getElementById('mm-badge'),
            title: document.getElementById('mm-title'),
            desc: document.getElementById('mm-desc'),
            sizesWrap: document.getElementById('mm-sizes-wrap'),
            sizes: document.getElementById('mm-sizes'),
            addonsWrap: document.getElementById('mm-addons-wrap'),
            addons: document.getElementById('mm-addons'),
            qty: document.getElementById('mm-qty'),
            total: document.getElementById('mm-total'),
            form: document.getElementById('mm-form'),
            close: document.getElementById('mm-close')
        };
        var base = 0;
        var maxQty = 10;
        var lastFocus = null;

        function peso(n) {
            return n.toLocaleString('en-US', { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 });
        }

        function esc(s) {
            var d = document.createElement('div');
            d.textContent = s == null ? '' : String(s);
            return d.innerHTML;
        }

        function recalc() {
            var unit = base;
            var size = el.form.querySelector('input[name="SizeId"]:checked');
            if (size) unit += parseFloat(size.dataset.delta) || 0;
            el.form.querySelectorAll('input[name="AddOnIds"]:checked').forEach(function(c) {
                unit += parseFloat(c.dataset.price) || 0;
            });
            var qty = Math.min(maxQty, Math.max(1, parseInt(el.qty.value, 10) || 1));
            el.total.textContent = peso(unit * qty);
        }

        function openModal(card) {
            var d = card.dataset;
            var isDrink = d.group === 'drinks';
            var stock = parseInt(d.stock, 10);
            base = parseFloat(d.price) || 0;
            maxQty = !isNaN(stock) && stock > 0 ? Math.min(10, stock) : 10;

            el.id.value = d.id;
            el.title.textContent = d.name || '';
            el.desc.textContent = d.desc || '';
            el.img.src = d.img || '';
            el.img.alt = d.name || '';
            el.badge.textContent = d.badge || '';
            el.badge.classList.toggle('hidden', !d.badge);
            el.qty.max = String(maxQty);
            el.qty.value = 1;

            el.sizes.innerHTML = '';
            if (isDrink && opts.sizes.length) {
                opts.sizes.forEach(function(s, i) {
                    var label = document.createElement('label');
                    label.className = 'cursor-pointer';
                    label.innerHTML =
                        '<input type="radio" name="SizeId" value="' + s.id + '" data-delta="' + s.price + '" class="peer sr-only"' + (i === 0 ? ' checked' : '') + ' />' +
                        '<span class="block rounded-xl border border-white/15 px-3 py-3 text-center text-sm text-cream peer-checked:border-caramel peer-checked:bg-caramel/10 peer-focus-visible:ring-2 peer-focus-visible:ring-caramel">' +
                        esc(s.name) +
                        '<span class="block text-xs text-neutral-400">' + (s.price > 0 ? '+\u20B1' + peso(s.price) : 'Base price') + '</span></span>';
                    el.sizes.appendChild(label);
                });
                el.sizesWrap.classList.remove('hidden');
            } else {
                el.sizesWrap.classList.add('hidden');
            }

            el.addons.innerHTML = '';
            var list = opts.addOns.filter(function(a) { return (a.group || '').trim().toLowerCase() === d.group; });
            if (list.length) {
                list.forEach(function(a) {
                    var label = document.createElement('label');
                    label.className = 'flex cursor-pointer items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-xl border border-white/10 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm text-cream transition hover:border-caramel/50';
                    label.innerHTML =
                        '<span class="flex items-center gap-3"><input type="checkbox" name="AddOnIds" value="' + a.id + '" data-price="' + a.price + '" class="size-4 accent-[#d4a373]" />' + esc(a.name) + '</span>' +
                        '<span class="text-xs text-neutral-400">' + (a.price > 0 ? '+\u20B1' + peso(a.price) : 'Free') + '</span>';
                    el.addons.appendChild(label);
                });
                el.addonsWrap.classList.remove('hidden');
            } else {
                el.addonsWrap.classList.add('hidden');
            }

            recalc();
            lastFocus = document.activeElement;
            modal.classList.remove('hidden');
            modal.classList.add('flex');
            document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
            el.close.focus();
        }

        function closeModal() {
            modal.classList.add('hidden');
            modal.classList.remove('flex');
            document.body.style.overflow = '';
            if (lastFocus && lastFocus.focus) lastFocus.focus();
        }

        document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
            var trigger = e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('[data-open]') : null;
            if (!trigger) return;
            var card = trigger.closest('[data-card]');
            if (!card) return;
            var stock = parseInt(card.dataset.stock, 10);
            if (!isNaN(stock) && stock <= 0) return;
            openModal(card);
        });

        el.close.addEventListener('click', closeModal);
        modal.addEventListener('click', function(e) { if (e.target === modal) closeModal(); });
        document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
            if (e.key === 'Escape' && !modal.classList.contains('hidden')) closeModal();
        });
        el.form.addEventListener('change', recalc);
        el.form.addEventListener('input', recalc);
    })();
</script>

FILE: BrewStop\Views\Menu\_BaristaReference.cshtml

@model (string Group, List<BrewStop.Models.ItemSize> Sizes, List<BrewStop.Models.AddOn> AddOns)
@{
    var isDrink = string.Equals(Model.Group?.Trim(), "Drinks", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
    var groupAddOns = Model.AddOns
        .Where(a => string.Equals(a.Group?.Trim(), Model.Group?.Trim(), StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
        .ToList();
}

<div class="space-y-1.5 rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-espresso/60 p-3 text-xs text-neutral-300">
    @if (isDrink && Model.Sizes.Count > 0)
    {
        <p><span class="font-semibold text-caramel">Sizes:</span> @string.Join(", ", Model.Sizes.Select(s => s.Name))</p>
    }
    @if (groupAddOns.Count > 0)
    {
        <p><span class="font-semibold text-caramel">Add-ons:</span> @string.Join(", ", groupAddOns.Select(a => a.Name))</p>
    }
    @if ((!isDrink || Model.Sizes.Count == 0) && groupAddOns.Count == 0)
    {
        <p class="text-neutral-400">Standard item (no size or add-on options)</p>
    }
</div>

@model BrewStop.Models.MenuItemDto

            <i class="fa-solid fa-star text-[10px]"></i> @Model.RatingText

@{
    ViewData["Title"] = "Order placed";
    ViewData["LiveReload"] = false;
    var pickupLocal = Slots.ToLocal(Model.PickupAtUtc);
    var subtotal = Model.Items.Sum(i => i.UnitPrice * i.Quantity);
    var payHint = Model.PaymentMethod switch
    {
        PaymentMethod.Cash => "Pay the total at the counter when you pick up.",
        PaymentMethod.GCash => "The barista verifies your GCash payment at pickup.",
        _ => "Complete your payment at pickup."
    };
    const string cardCls = "rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-card p-5 sm:p-6";
    const string h2 = "font-serif text-xl font-bold text-cream";
}

                        </div>
                        <div>
                            <dt class="text-neutral-400">Pickup time</dt>
                            <dd class="text-cream">@pickupLocal.ToString("h:mm tt")</dd>

                        {

                                <dt class="text-neutral-400">GCash reference</dt>
                                <dd class="font-mono text-cream">@Model.PaymentReference</dd>
                            </div>

                        <i class="fa-solid fa-circle-info mr-2 text-caramel"></i>@payHint
                    </p>
                </section>

            </div>

            <!-- RIGHT -->

                <div class="@cardCls">
                    <h2 class="@h2 border-b border-white/10 pb-4">Order Summary</h2>

                    <ul class="divide-y divide-white/10">
                        @foreach (var i in Model.Items)
                        {
                            <li class="flex items-start justify-between gap-3 py-4 text-sm">

                                    <img src="@Images.Resolve(i.MenuItem?.ImageUrl, i.ItemName)" alt="@i.ItemName" class="size-16 shrink-0 rounded-lg object-cover" />
                                    <div class="min-w-0">
                                        <p class="font-medium text-cream">@i.ItemName</p>
                                        @if (i.SizeName is not null || i.AddOns.Count > 0)
                                        {
                                            <p class="text-xs text-neutral-400">
                                                @(i.SizeName ?? "")@(i.SizeName is not null && i.AddOns.Count > 0 ? " · " : "")@string.Join(", ", i.AddOns.Select(a => a.Name))

                        <div class="flex justify-between text-neutral-300"><dt>Subtotal</dt><dd>&#8369;@subtotal.ToString("N2")</dd></div>

                        }

                            <dt class="font-serif text-lg font-bold text-cream">Total</dt>

@model BrewStop.Models.Order
@inject BrewStop.Services.IPickupSlotService Slots
@{
    ViewData["Title"] = $"Order #{Model.Id}";
    ViewData["LiveReload"] = true;
    var pickupLocal = Slots.ToLocal(Model.PickupAtUtc);
    var subtotal = Model.Items.Sum(i => i.UnitPrice * i.Quantity);
    var isActive = Model.Status is OrderStatus.Placed or OrderStatus.Preparing or OrderStatus.Ready;
}

<div class="tw">
    <div class="mx-auto max-w-3xl px-4 pt-24 pb-12">
        <a asp-action="Index" class="text-sm font-semibold text-caramel hover:text-cream">&larr; Back to orders</a>

        <div class="mt-2 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
            <h1 class="font-serif text-3xl font-bold text-cream">Order #@Model.Id</h1>
            <partial name="_StatusBadge" model="Model" />
        </div>

        <div class="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            <form asp-action="Reorder" asp-route-id="@Model.Id" method="post">
                <button type="submit" class="rounded-full bg-caramel px-5 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-black transition hover:bg-cream">
                    <i class="fa-solid fa-rotate-right mr-1"></i>Reorder
                </button>
            </form>
            <a asp-action="Receipt" asp-route-id="@Model.Id"
               class="rounded-full border border-white/15 px-5 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-cream transition hover:border-caramel hover:text-caramel">
                <i class="fa-solid fa-receipt mr-1"></i>View Receipt
            </a>
        </div>

        @if (TempData["Error"] is string err)
        {
            <p class="mt-4 rounded-xl border border-red-500/30 bg-red-500/10 p-3 text-sm text-red-300">@err</p>
        }

        @if (Model.Status == OrderStatus.Ready)
        {
            <div class="mt-4 flex items-start gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-emerald-400/40 bg-emerald-500/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-emerald-200">
                <i class="fa-solid fa-bell mt-0.5"></i>
                <span>Your order is ready! Show the QR code below at the counter.</span>
            </div>
        }

        <section class="mt-5 rounded-3xl border border-white/10 bg-card p-5 sm:p-6">
            <partial name="_Progress" model="Model" />

            <dl class="mt-6 grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 border-t border-white/10 pt-5 text-sm">
                <div>
                    <dt class="text-neutral-400">@(isActive ? "Estimated ready" : "Pickup slot")</dt>
                    <dd class="text-cream">@pickupLocal.ToString("ddd, MMM d · h:mm tt")</dd>
                </div>
                <div>
                    <dt class="text-neutral-400">Payment</dt>
                    <dd class="text-cream">@Model.PaymentMethod · @Model.PaymentStatus.Label()</dd>
                </div>
                @if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Model.PaymentReference))
                {
                    <div class="col-span-2">
                        <dt class="text-neutral-400">GCash reference</dt>
                        <dd class="font-mono text-cream">@Model.PaymentReference</dd>
                    </div>
                }
            </dl>
        </section>

        <ul class="mt-5 divide-y divide-white/10 rounded-3xl border border-white/10 bg-card">
            @foreach (var i in Model.Items)
            {
                <li class="p-4 text-sm text-cream sm:px-6">
                    <div class="flex justify-between gap-4">
                        <span>@i.Quantity &times; @i.ItemName @(i.SizeName is null ? "" : $"({i.SizeName})")</span>
                        <span class="shrink-0">&#8369;@((i.UnitPrice * i.Quantity).ToString("N2"))</span>
                    </div>
                    @if (i.AddOns.Count > 0)
                    {
                        <p class="mt-0.5 text-xs text-neutral-400">+ @string.Join(", ", i.AddOns.Select(a => a.Name))</p>
                    }
                </li>
            }
        </ul>

        @if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Model.Note))
        {
            <p class="mt-3 text-sm text-neutral-300"><span class="text-neutral-400">Note:</span> @Model.Note</p>
        }

        <dl class="mt-4 space-y-1 text-sm">
            <div class="flex justify-between text-neutral-300"><dt>Subtotal</dt><dd>&#8369;@subtotal.ToString("N2")</dd></div>
            @if (Model.DiscountAmount > 0)
            {
                <div class="flex justify-between text-emerald-300"><dt>Promo @Model.PromoCode</dt><dd>&minus;&#8369;@Model.DiscountAmount.ToString("N2")</dd></div>
            }
            <div class="flex items-baseline justify-between pt-1">
                <dt class="text-neutral-300">Total</dt>
                <dd class="text-xl font-bold text-caramel">&#8369;@Model.Total.ToString("N2")</dd>
            </div>
        </dl>

        @if (Model.Status == OrderStatus.Ready)
        {
            <partial name="_Qr" />
        }

        @if (Model.Status == OrderStatus.Claimed)
        {
            var reviews = ViewBag.Reviews as Dictionary<int, ProductReview> ?? new Dictionary<int, ProductReview>();
            var distinct = Model.Items.GroupBy(i => i.MenuItemId).Select(g => g.First()).ToList();

            <section class="mt-8 rounded-3xl border border-white/10 bg-card p-5 sm:p-6" aria-labelledby="rate-heading">
                <h2 id="rate-heading" class="font-serif text-xl font-semibold text-cream">Rate your order</h2>
                <p class="mt-1 text-sm text-neutral-400">Verified reviews &mdash; only items you picked up can be rated.</p>

                @if (TempData["ReviewSuccess"] is string rok)
                {
                    <p class="mt-3 rounded-xl border border-emerald-500/30 bg-emerald-500/10 p-3 text-sm text-emerald-300" role="status">@rok</p>
                }
                @if (TempData["ReviewError"] is string rerr)
                {
                    <p class="mt-3 rounded-xl border border-red-500/30 bg-red-500/10 p-3 text-sm text-red-300" role="alert">@rerr</p>
                }

                <ul class="mt-4 divide-y divide-white/10">
                    @foreach (var it in distinct)
                    {
                        <li class="py-5 first:pt-0 last:pb-0">
                            <p class="text-sm font-semibold text-cream">@it.ItemName</p>

                            @if (reviews.TryGetValue(it.MenuItemId, out var rv))
                            {
                                <p class="mt-1 text-lg tracking-wide text-amber-400" aria-label="You rated @rv.Rating out of 5">
                                    @(new string('?', rv.Rating))<span class="text-neutral-600">@(new string('?', 5 - rv.Rating))</span>
                                </p>
                                @if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rv.Comment))
                                {
                                    <p class="mt-1 whitespace-pre-line text-sm text-neutral-300">@rv.Comment</p>
                                }
                                <p class="mt-1 text-xs text-neutral-500">Thanks for your review!</p>
                            }
                            else
                            {
                                <form asp-controller="Reviews" asp-action="Create" method="post" class="mt-3 space-y-3">
                                    <input type="hidden" name="OrderId" value="@Model.Id" />
                                    <input type="hidden" name="MenuItemId" value="@it.MenuItemId" />

                                    <fieldset>
                                        <legend class="sr-only">Rating for @it.ItemName</legend>
                                        <div class="inline-flex flex-row-reverse justify-end rounded-lg text-3xl leading-none focus-within:ring-2 focus-within:ring-caramel">
                                            @for (var n = 5; n >= 1; n--)
                                            {
                                                <input type="radio" name="Rating" value="@n" id="star-@it.MenuItemId-@n" required class="peer sr-only" />
                                                <label for="star-@it.MenuItemId-@n"
                                                       class="cursor-pointer px-0.5 text-neutral-600 transition hover:text-amber-300 peer-checked:text-amber-400 [&:hover~label]:text-amber-300">
                                                    <span class="sr-only">@n star@(n == 1 ? "" : "s")</span>&#9733;
                                                </label>
                                            }
                                        </div>
                                    </fieldset>

                                    <textarea name="Comment" rows="2" maxlength="300"
                                              placeholder="Optional comment (max 300 characters)"
                                              aria-label="Comment for @it.ItemName"
                                              class="w-full rounded-xl border border-white/15 bg-transparent px-3 py-2.5 text-sm text-cream placeholder:text-neutral-500 focus:border-caramel focus:outline-none"></textarea>

                                    <button type="submit"
                                            class="rounded-full bg-caramel px-5 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-black transition hover:bg-cream">
                                        Submit review
                                    </button>
                                </form>
                            }
                        </li>
                    }
                </ul>
            </section>
        }

        @if (Model.Status == OrderStatus.Placed)
        {
            <form asp-action="Cancel" asp-route-id="@Model.Id" method="post" class="mt-6"
                  onsubmit="return confirm('Cancel this order?');">
                <button type="submit" class="rounded-full border border-red-400/40 px-5 py-2 text-sm text-red-300 hover:bg-red-500/10">Cancel order</button>
            </form>
        }
    </div>
</div>

@section Scripts {
    @if (isActive)
    {
        <partial name="_OrderLive" />
    }
}

@model List<BrewStop.Models.Order>
@inject BrewStop.Services.IPickupSlotService Slots
@inject BrewStop.Services.IImageResolver Images
@{
    ViewData["Title"] = "My Orders";
    ViewData["LiveReload"] = false;

    var active = Model.Where(o => o.Status is OrderStatus.Placed or OrderStatus.Preparing or OrderStatus.Ready).ToList();
    var past = Model.Where(o => !active.Contains(o)).ToList();

    const string whenFmt = "ddd, MMM d \u00B7 h:mm tt";
    const string payTone = "text-cream";
}

<div class="tw">
    <div class="mx-auto max-w-5xl px-4 pt-24 pb-16">

        <!-- Header -->
        <header class="flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-3 border-b border-white/10 pb-5">

                <p class="mt-1 text-sm text-neutral-400">Manage and track your coffee orders.</p>
            </div>
            <a asp-controller="Account" asp-action="Manage"
               class="text-sm font-semibold text-caramel transition hover:text-cream">My Account &rarr;</a>
        </header>

        @if (TempData["Success"] is string ok)
        {
            <p class="mt-5 rounded-xl border border-emerald-500/30 bg-emerald-500/10 p-3 text-sm text-emerald-300" role="status">@ok</p>
        }
        @if (TempData["Warning"] is string warn)
        {
            <p class="mt-5 rounded-xl border border-amber-400/40 bg-amber-500/10 p-3 text-sm text-amber-200" role="alert">@warn</p>
        }
        @if (TempData["Error"] is string err)
        {
            <p class="mt-5 rounded-xl border border-red-500/40 bg-red-500/10 p-3 text-sm text-red-200" role="alert">@err</p>

                    <i class="fa-solid fa-mug-hot text-5xl text-caramel"></i>
                    <i class="fa-solid fa-wind absolute -top-1 right-5 -rotate-12 text-sm text-caramel/50"></i>
                    <i class="fa-solid fa-wind absolute -top-3 right-11 -rotate-12 text-xs text-caramel/30"></i>
                </div>
                <h2 class="mt-6 font-serif text-2xl font-semibold text-cream">No orders yet</h2>
                <p class="mx-auto mt-2 max-w-sm text-sm text-neutral-400">
                    Your first cup is waiting. Order ahead and skip the queue at pickup.
                </p>

                   class="mt-7 inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-r from-caramel to-[#e9c46a] px-8 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-black shadow-lg shadow-caramel/20 transition hover:brightness-110">
                    Explore Menu <i class="fa-solid fa-arrow-right text-xs"></i>
                </a>
            </div>
        }
        else
        {
            <!-- Active Orders -->
            <section class="mt-8" aria-labelledby="active-heading">
                <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <h2 id="active-heading" class="font-serif text-2xl font-semibold text-cream">Active Orders</h2>
                    @if (active.Count > 0)
                    {
                        <span class="rounded-full bg-caramel/15 px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-bold text-caramel">@active.Count</span>
                    }
                </div>

                @if (active.Count == 0)
                {
                    <div class="mt-4 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4 rounded-3xl border border-dashed border-white/15 bg-card/60 px-5 py-6">
                        <div class="flex items-center gap-4">
                            <div class="grid size-12 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full bg-caramel/10 text-lg text-caramel"><i class="fa-solid fa-mug-hot"></i></div>
                            <p class="text-sm text-neutral-300">Nothing brewing right now. Ready for another round?</p>
                        </div>
                        <a asp-controller="Menu" asp-action="Index"
                           class="rounded-full bg-caramel px-5 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-black transition hover:bg-cream">Explore Menu</a>
                    </div>
                }
                else
                {
                    <div class="mt-4 space-y-5">
                        @foreach (var o in active)
                        {

                            var shown = o.Items.Take(3).ToList();
                            var more = o.Items.Count - shown.Count;

                            <article class="rounded-3xl border border-white/10 bg-card p-5 shadow-lg shadow-black/20 transition hover:border-caramel/40 sm:p-6">
                                <div class="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                                    <div class="flex min-w-0 flex-wrap items-center gap-2.5">
                                        <h3 class="font-serif text-xl font-semibold text-cream">Order #@o.Id</h3>
                                        <partial name="_StatusBadge" model="o" />
                                    </div>
                                    <p class="shrink-0 text-xl font-bold text-caramel">&#8369;@o.Total.ToString("N2")</p>
                                </div>

                                <div class="mt-4 flex gap-4">
                                    <div class="flex shrink-0 -space-x-4 self-start">
                                        @foreach (var i in shown)
                                        {
                                            <img src="@Images.Resolve(i.MenuItem?.ImageUrl, i.ItemName)" alt="@i.ItemName" loading="lazy"
                                                 class="size-16 rounded-2xl object-cover ring-2 ring-card sm:size-20" />
                                        }
                                        @if (more > 0)
                                        {
                                            <span class="grid size-16 place-items-center rounded-2xl bg-espresso text-xs font-bold text-caramel ring-2 ring-card sm:size-20">+@more</span>
                                        }
                                    </div>

                                    <ul class="min-w-0 flex-1 space-y-2">
                                        @foreach (var i in o.Items)
                                        {

                                                <p class="truncate text-sm font-semibold text-cream">@i.Quantity&times; @i.ItemName</p>
                                                @if (i.SizeName is not null || i.AddOns.Count > 0)
                                                {
                                                    <div class="mt-1 flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                                                        @if (i.SizeName is not null)
                                                        {
                                                            <span class="rounded-full bg-white/5 px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] text-neutral-300">@i.SizeName</span>
                                                        }
                                                        @foreach (var a in i.AddOns)
                                                        {
                                                            <span class="rounded-full bg-caramel/10 px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] text-caramel">+ @a.Name</span>
                                                        }
                                                    </div>
                                                }
                                            </li>
                                        }
                                    </ul>
                                </div>

FILE: BrewStop\Views\Order\_OrderLive.cshtml
========================================
@{
    var reload = ViewData["LiveReload"] is true;
}
<div class="tw">
    <div id="order-toast-host" aria-live="polite"
         class="pointer-events-none fixed inset-x-0 top-20 z-[1100] flex flex-col items-center gap-2 px-4"></div>
</div>
<script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/microsoft-signalr/8.0.7/signalr.min.js"></script>
<script>
    (function() {
        if (!window.signalR) return;
        var RELOAD = @(reload ? "true" : "false");
        var ORDER = ['Placed', 'Preparing', 'Ready', 'Claimed'];
        var host = document.getElementById('order-toast-host');

        function toast(text, tone) {
            var el = document.createElement('div');
            el.setAttribute('role', 'status');
            el.className = 'pointer-events-auto flex w-full max-w-md items-center gap-3 rounded-2xl border px-4 py-3 text-sm font-semibold shadow-xl backdrop-blur ' +
                (tone === 'ready'
                    ? 'border-emerald-400/50 bg-emerald-500/90 text-black'
                    : 'border-caramel/40 bg-card/95 text-cream');
            var icon = document.createElement('i');
            icon.className = 'fa-solid ' + (tone === 'ready' ? 'fa-bell' : 'fa-mug-hot');
            var span = document.createElement('span');
            span.className = 'flex-1';
            span.textContent = text;
            var close = document.createElement('button');
            close.type = 'button';
            close.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Dismiss');
            close.className = 'text-lg leading-none opacity-70 hover:opacity-100';
            close.textContent = '\u00d7';
            close.onclick = function() { el.remove(); };
            el.append(icon, span, close);
            host.appendChild(el);
            setTimeout(function() { el.remove(); }, 9000);
        }

        function render(orderId, status) {
            document.querySelectorAll('[data-order-status="' + orderId + '"]').forEach(function(e) {
                e.dataset.status = status;
                e.textContent = status;
            });
            var idx = ORDER.indexOf(status);
            document.querySelectorAll('[data-progress="' + orderId + '"] [data-step]').forEach(function(li, i) {
                li.dataset.state = status === 'Claimed' || i < idx ? 'done' : i === idx ? 'active' : 'todo';
            });
        }

        var conn = new signalR.HubConnectionBuilder()
            .withUrl('/hubs/orders')
            .withAutomaticReconnect()
            .build();

        conn.on('OrderStatusChanged', function(u) {
            if (!u || !u.orderId || !u.status) return;
            render(u.orderId, u.status);
            if (u.status === 'Ready') toast('Order #' + u.orderId + ' is ready for pickup! Show your QR code at the counter.', 'ready');
            else if (u.status === 'Preparing') toast('Order #' + u.orderId + ' is now being prepared.', 'info');
            else if (u.status === 'Claimed') toast('Order #' + u.orderId + ' was picked up. Enjoy!', 'info');
            if (RELOAD && (u.status === 'Ready' || u.status === 'Claimed')) setTimeout(function() { location.reload(); }, 1800);
        });
        conn.onreconnected(function() { if (RELOAD) location.reload(); });
        conn.start().catch(function(e) { console.warn('Order hub unavailable', e); });
    })();
</script>

FILE: BrewStop\Views\Order\_Progress.cshtml

@model BrewStop.Models.Order
@{
    var steps = new[] { OrderStatus.Placed, OrderStatus.Preparing, OrderStatus.Ready, OrderStatus.Claimed };
    var current = Array.IndexOf(steps, Model.Status);
}
@if (Model.Status == OrderStatus.Cancelled)
{
    <p data-progress="@Model.Id" class="rounded-xl border border-red-500/30 bg-red-500/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-300">
        <i class="fa-solid fa-ban me-1"></i> This order was cancelled.
    </p>
}
else
{
    <ol data-progress="@Model.Id" class="flex items-start" aria-label="Order progress">
        @for (var i = 0; i < steps.Length; i++)
        {
            var state = Model.Status == OrderStatus.Claimed || i < current ? "done" : i == current ? "active" : "todo";
            <li data-step="@steps[i]" data-state="@state" class="group relative flex flex-1 flex-col items-center text-center">
                @if (i > 0)
                {
                    <span class="absolute right-1/2 top-4 h-0.5 w-full bg-white/15 group-data-[state=done]:bg-caramel group-data-[state=active]:bg-caramel"></span>
                }
                <span class="relative z-10 grid size-8 place-items-center rounded-full border border-white/20 bg-card text-xs font-bold text-neutral-500 group-data-[state=active]:border-caramel group-data-[state=active]:text-caramel group-data-[state=active]:ring-4 group-data-[state=active]:ring-caramel/20 group-data-[state=done]:border-caramel group-data-[state=done]:bg-caramel group-data-[state=done]:text-black">
                    <i class="fa-solid fa-check hidden group-data-[state=done]:inline"></i>
                    <span class="group-data-[state=done]:hidden">@(i + 1)</span>
                </span>
                <span class="mt-2 text-[11px] font-semibold text-neutral-500 group-data-[state=active]:text-caramel group-data-[state=done]:text-cream sm:text-xs">@steps[i]</span>
            </li>
        }
    </ol>
}

FILE: BrewStop\Views\Order\_Qr.cshtml
========================================
@if (ViewBag.QrToken is string token)
{
    <div class="mt-6 rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-card p-6 text-center">
        <p class="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-caramel">Pickup QR Code (single use)</p>
        <div id="qr" data-token="@token" class="mx-auto mt-4 inline-block rounded-xl bg-white p-3"></div>

        <div class="mx-auto mt-4 flex max-w-xl items-stretch gap-2">
            <input id="qr-token" type="text" readonly value="@token"
                   class="min-w-0 flex-1 rounded-xl border border-white/10 bg-black/30 px-3 py-2 font-mono text-xs text-cream" />
            <button id="qr-copy" type="button"
                    class="rounded-xl bg-caramel px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-black hover:opacity-90">
                Copy Token
            </button>
        </div>
        <p id="qr-copy-msg" class="mt-2 h-4 text-xs text-green-400" aria-live="polite"></p>
    </div>
    <script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/qrcodejs/1.0.0/qrcode.min.js"></script>
    <script>
        (function() {
            var el = document.getElementById('qr');
            new QRCode(el, { text: el.dataset.token, width: 200, height: 200, correctLevel: QRCode.CorrectLevel.M });

            var input = document.getElementById('qr-token');
            var msg = document.getElementById('qr-copy-msg');
            function done(ok) {
                msg.textContent = ok ? 'Token copied.' : 'Copy failed. Select the text and copy manually.';
                setTimeout(function() { msg.textContent = ''; }, 2500);
            }
            document.getElementById('qr-copy').addEventListener('click', function() {
                if (navigator.clipboard && window.isSecureContext) {
                    navigator.clipboard.writeText(input.value).then(function() { done(true); }, function() { done(false); });
                } else {
                    input.select();
                    input.setSelectionRange(0, input.value.length);
                    var ok = false;
                    try { ok = document.execCommand('copy'); } catch (e) { }
                    done(ok);
                }
            });
        })();
    </script>
}

FILE: BrewStop\Views\Shared\_BaristaReference.cshtml

@model (string Group, List<BrewStop.Models.ItemSize> Sizes, List<BrewStop.Models.AddOn> AddOns)
@{
    var adds = Model.AddOns.Where(a => a.Group == Model.Group).ToList();
    var showSizes = Model.Group == "Drinks" && Model.Sizes.Count > 0;
}
<div class="space-y-2 rounded-xl border border-solid border-white/10 bg-espresso/60 p-3 text-xs">
    <p class="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-caramel">
        <i class="fa-solid fa-eye mr-1"></i>Reference only
    </p>
    @if (showSizes)
    {
        <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
            <span class="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-500">Sizes</span>
            @foreach (var s in Model.Sizes)
            {
                <span class="rounded-md bg-cream px-2 py-0.5 font-bold text-[#1e3932]">@s.Name@(s.PriceDelta > 0 ? " +\u20B1" + s.PriceDelta.ToString("N2") : "")</span>
            }
        </div>
    }
    <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
        <span class="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-500">Add-ons</span>
        @if (adds.Count == 0)
        {
            <span class="text-neutral-500">None</span>
        }
        @foreach (var a in adds)
        {
            <span class="rounded-md border border-solid border-[#00754a] bg-[#00754a]/20 px-2 py-0.5 font-semibold text-emerald-200">@a.Name @(a.Price > 0 ? "+\u20B1" + a.Price.ToString("N2") : "")</span>
        }
    </div>
</div>

@model string
@{
    var id = "bs-cc-" + Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N")[..8];
}
<div id="@id" class="group/cc my-1" data-state="idle" data-action="@Model">

    @* Trigger checkbox (Unchanged dark theme for the main page) *@
    <label class="flex cursor-pointer select-none items-center gap-3 rounded-xl border border-stone-700 bg-stone-800/50 px-3.5 py-3 transition hover:border-amber-400
        group-data-[state=success]/cc:cursor-default group-data-[state=success]/cc:border-green-400
        group-data-[state=error]/cc:border-red-400">
        <input type="checkbox" class="bs-cc-check peer sr-only" aria-describedby="@id-status" aria-haspopup="dialog" />

        @* Bulletproof custom checkbox rendering *@
        <div aria-hidden="true"
             style="width: 24px !important; height: 24px !important; min-width: 24px !important; min-height: 24px !important; display: grid !important; border-width: 2px !important; border-style: solid !important;"
             class="flex-none place-items-center rounded-md border-stone-400
            peer-focus-visible:outline peer-focus-visible:outline-2 peer-focus-visible:outline-offset-2 peer-focus-visible:outline-amber-400
            group-data-[state=success]/cc:!border-green-400 group-data-[state=success]/cc:!bg-green-400">
            <svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" class="hidden w-4 h-4 text-stone-950 group-data-[state=success]/cc:block" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round">
                <path d="M4 10.5l4 4 8-9" />
            </svg>
        </div>

        <span class="flex-1 text-sm">I am not a robot</span>
        <span class="text-xl" aria-hidden="true">&#9749;</span>
    </label>

    <p id="@id-status" class="mt-1.5 min-h-4 px-0.5 text-xs text-stone-400 group-data-[state=error]/cc:text-red-300" role="status" aria-live="polite"></p>

    <input type="hidden" name="bs-captcha-token" class="bs-cc-token" />

    @* Modal - Replaced with Starbucks/reCAPTCHA light theme *@
    <dialog class="bs-cc-dialog m-auto w-[calc(100%-2rem)] max-w-[400px] overflow-hidden rounded-sm border border-stone-300 bg-white p-0 text-stone-900 shadow-2xl backdrop:bg-black/50"
            aria-labelledby="@id-title">
        <div class="p-2">
            <div id="@id-title" class="bg-blue-600 px-5 py-4 text-white">
                <p class="text-sm leading-tight">Select all images with</p>
                <p class="text-2xl font-bold leading-tight">coffee</p>
            </div>

            <div class="bs-cc-grid mt-0.5 grid grid-cols-3 gap-0.5 bg-white" role="group" aria-labelledby="@id-title"></div>

            <p class="bs-cc-error mt-2 hidden px-1 text-sm text-red-600" role="alert">Please try again.</p>

            <div class="mt-2 flex items-center justify-between border-t border-stone-200 px-1 pt-2">
                <div class="flex items-center gap-1 text-stone-500">
                    <button type="button" class="bs-cc-refresh grid size-9 place-items-center rounded hover:bg-stone-100" aria-label="Get a new challenge">
                        <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" class="size-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round">
                            <path d="M21 12a9 9 0 1 1-3-6.7" />
                            <path d="M21 3v6h-6" />
                        </svg>
                    </button>
                    <button type="button" class="bs-cc-cancel rounded px-2 py-1 text-xs hover:bg-stone-100">Cancel</button>
                </div>
                <button type="button" disabled
                        class="bs-cc-verify rounded-sm bg-blue-600 px-6 py-3 text-sm font-semibold uppercase text-white transition hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50">
                    Verify
                </button>
            </div>
        </div>
    </dialog>

    @* Tile template - Replaced with blue checkmark styling *@
    <template class="bs-cc-tile">
        <button type="button" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="Image option"
                class="group/tile relative aspect-square overflow-hidden bg-stone-200 focus-visible:outline focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-blue-600">
            <img alt="" draggable="false"
                 class="size-full object-cover transition-transform duration-150 group-aria-pressed/tile:scale-[0.8]" />

            <div class="pointer-events-none absolute left-1 top-1 hidden place-items-center rounded-full bg-blue-600 text-white group-aria-pressed/tile:grid"
                 style="width: 24px !important; height: 24px !important;">
                <svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" style="width: 16px !important; height: 16px !important;" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true">
                    <path d="M4 10.5l4 4 8-9" />
                </svg>
            </div>
        </button>
    </template>
</div>

<script>
    (function() {
        const root = document.getElementById('@id');
        const form = root.closest('form');
        const base = '@Url.Content("~/captcha")';
        const action = root.dataset.action;

        const check = root.querySelector('.bs-cc-check');
        const status = root.querySelector('[role="status"]');
        const tokenInput = root.querySelector('.bs-cc-token');
        const dialog = root.querySelector('.bs-cc-dialog');
        const grid = root.querySelector('.bs-cc-grid');
        const errorEl = root.querySelector('.bs-cc-error');
        const verifyBtn = root.querySelector('.bs-cc-verify');
        const cancelBtn = root.querySelector('.bs-cc-cancel');
        const refreshBtn = root.querySelector('.bs-cc-refresh');
        const tileTpl = root.querySelector('.bs-cc-tile');

        let challengeId = null;
        let verified = false;
        let expiresAt = 0;

        function setState(state, msg) {
            root.dataset.state = state;
            status.textContent = msg || '';
        }

        function reset() {
            verified = false;
            expiresAt = 0;
            tokenInput.value = '';
            check.checked = false;
            check.disabled = false;
            setState('idle');
        }

        function openChallenge() {
            check.checked = true;
            setState('idle');
            if (!dialog.open) dialog.showModal();
            loadChallenge();
        }

        function antiforgery() {
            const el = form && form.querySelector('input[name="__RequestVerificationToken"]');
            return el ? el.value : '';
        }

        function selected() {
            return [...grid.querySelectorAll('[aria-pressed="true"]')];
        }

        function buildTile(t, i) {
            const node = tileTpl.content.firstElementChild.cloneNode(true);
            node.dataset.id = t.id;
            node.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Image ' + (i + 1));
            node.querySelector('img').src = t.src;
            node.addEventListener('click', function() {
                node.setAttribute('aria-pressed', node.getAttribute('aria-pressed') === 'true' ? 'false' : 'true');
                errorEl.classList.add('hidden');
                verifyBtn.disabled = selected().length === 0;
            });
            return node;
        }

        async function loadChallenge() {
            verifyBtn.disabled = true;
            errorEl.classList.add('hidden');

            // Restore grid structure in case the previous load errored out
            grid.className = 'bs-cc-grid mt-0.5 grid grid-cols-3 gap-0.5 bg-white';
            grid.setAttribute('aria-busy', 'true');

            grid.replaceChildren(...Array.from({ length: 9 }, function() {
                const s = document.createElement('div');
                s.className = 'aspect-square animate-pulse bg-stone-200';
                return s;
            }));

            try {
                const r = await fetch(base + '/challenge?action=' + encodeURIComponent(action), { cache: 'no-store' });
                if (!r.ok) throw new Error('challenge');
                const data = await r.json();
                challengeId = data.id;
                grid.replaceChildren(...data.tiles.map(buildTile));
            } catch (e) {
                challengeId = null;
                grid.textContent = 'Could not load the challenge. Close this window and try again.';
                grid.className = 'bs-cc-grid p-4 text-sm text-red-600';
            } finally {
                grid.removeAttribute('aria-busy');
            }
        }

        async function verify() {
            if (!challengeId) return;
            verifyBtn.disabled = true;
            const tiles = selected().map(function(b) { return b.dataset.id; });

            let data = { ok: false };
            try {
                const r = await fetch(base + '/verify', {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'RequestVerificationToken': antiforgery() },
                    body: JSON.stringify({ challengeId: challengeId, tiles: tiles, action: action })
                });
                if (r.ok) data = await r.json();
            } catch (e) { /* treated as failure */ }

            if (data.ok) {
                verified = true;
                expiresAt = Date.now() + Math.max(30, (data.expiresIn || 600) - 15) * 1000;
                tokenInput.value = data.token;
                check.checked = true;
                check.disabled = true;
                setState('success', 'Verified.');
                dialog.close('success');
            } else {
                await loadChallenge();
                errorEl.classList.remove('hidden');
            }
        }

        check.addEventListener('change', function() {
            if (verified) { check.checked = true; return; }
            if (check.checked) openChallenge();
        });

        dialog.addEventListener('close', function() {
            if (!verified) reset();
        });

        dialog.addEventListener('click', function(e) { if (e.target === dialog) dialog.close(); });
        cancelBtn.addEventListener('click', function() { dialog.close(); });
        refreshBtn.addEventListener('click', loadChallenge);
        verifyBtn.addEventListener('click', verify);

        form.addEventListener('submit', function(e) {
            if (verified && Date.now() < expiresAt) return;
            e.preventDefault();
            reset();
            openChallenge();
        });

        window.addEventListener('pageshow', function(e) {
            if (e.persisted) reset();
        });
    })();
</script>

FILE: BrewStop\Views\Shared\_Layout.cshtml

@using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Identity
@inject BrewStop.Services.ICartService Cart
@inject UserManager<ApplicationUser> Users
@{
    var isAdmin = User.IsInRole("Admin");
    var isBarista = User.IsInRole("Barista");
    var staffShell = isBarista && !isAdmin;   // Barista-only accounts get the sidebar shell
    var isCustomer = !isAdmin && !isBarista;
    var signedIn = User.Identity?.IsAuthenticated == true;

    ApplicationUser? me = null;
    var hasPwd = false;
    if (signedIn && isCustomer)
    {
        me = await Users.GetUserAsync(User);
        hasPwd = me is not null && await Users.HasPasswordAsync(me);
    }

    var ctl = ViewContext.RouteData.Values["controller"]?.ToString() ?? "";
    var act = ViewContext.RouteData.Values["action"]?.ToString() ?? "";
    string Nav(string c, string a = "Index") =>
        "rounded-full px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium transition-colors duration-200 " +
        (string.Equals(ctl, c, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) && (c != "Home" || string.Equals(act, a, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            ? "bg-caramel/15 text-caramel"
            : "text-neutral-200 hover:bg-caramel/10 hover:text-caramel");
    const string ib = "relative grid size-10 place-items-center rounded-full text-cream transition hover:bg-caramel/15 hover:text-caramel focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-caramel";
    const string panelBase = "invisible absolute right-0 top-full z-50 mt-2 origin-top-right scale-95 rounded-2xl border border-caramel/20 bg-card/95 text-cream opacity-0 shadow-2xl backdrop-blur transition duration-150 ease-out";
    const string item = "flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-sm text-neutral-200 transition hover:bg-caramel/10 hover:text-caramel";
}

    <script>
            (function() {
                var OPEN = ['visible', 'opacity-100', 'scale-100'], CLOSED = ['invisible', 'opacity-0', 'scale-95'];
                var dds = [].slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('[data-dd]'));

                function setOpen(dd, open) {
                    var b = dd.querySelector('[data-dd-btn]'), p = dd.querySelector('[data-dd-panel]');
                    b.setAttribute('aria-expanded', open ? 'true' : 'false');
                    p.setAttribute('aria-hidden', open ? 'false' : 'true');
                    CLOSED.forEach(function(c) { p.classList.toggle(c, !open); });
                    OPEN.forEach(function(c) { p.classList.toggle(c, open); });
                    if (open) { var f = p.querySelector('[data-autofocus]'); if (f) setTimeout(function() { f.focus(); }, 60); }
                    dd.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('dd:toggle', { detail: open }));
                }
                function isOpen(dd) { return dd.querySelector('[data-dd-btn]').getAttribute('aria-expanded') === 'true'; }
                function closeAll(except) { dds.forEach(function(d) { if (d !== except && isOpen(d)) setOpen(d, false); }); }

                dds.forEach(function(dd) {
                    dd.querySelector('[data-dd-btn]').addEventListener('click', function(e) {
                        e.stopPropagation();
                        var open = !isOpen(dd);
                        closeAll(dd);
                        setOpen(dd, open);
                    });
                });
                document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
                    dds.forEach(function(d) { if (isOpen(d) && !d.contains(e.target)) setOpen(d, false); });
                });
                document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
                    if (e.key !== 'Escape') return;
                    dds.forEach(function(d) {
                        if (isOpen(d)) { setOpen(d, false); d.querySelector('[data-dd-btn]').focus(); }
                    });
                    var dlg = document.getElementById('delete-dialog'); if (dlg && dlg.open) dlg.close();
                });

                // Mobile collapse
                var tg = document.getElementById('nav-toggle'), links = document.getElementById('nav-links');
                if (tg && links) tg.addEventListener('click', function() {
                    var show = links.classList.contains('hidden');
                    links.classList.toggle('hidden', !show);
                    links.classList.toggle('flex', show);
                    tg.setAttribute('aria-expanded', show ? 'true' : 'false');
                });

                // Delete-account dialog
                var dlg = document.getElementById('delete-dialog'), openBtn = document.getElementById('open-delete-dialog');
                if (dlg && openBtn) {
                    openBtn.addEventListener('click', function() { closeAll(); dlg.showModal(); });
                    document.getElementById('cancel-delete-dialog').addEventListener('click', function() { dlg.close(); });
                    dlg.addEventListener('click', function(e) { if (e.target === dlg) dlg.close(); });
                }
            })();
    </script>
    @if (signedIn && isCustomer)
    {
        <script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/microsoft-signalr/8.0.7/signalr.min.js"></script>
        <script>
                (function() {
                    var dd = document.getElementById('bell-dd'); if (!dd) return;
                    var badge = document.getElementById('bell-badge'), list = document.getElementById('bell-list');
                    var KEY = 'bs.notif.unread', loaded = false;

                    function unread() { try { return parseInt(localStorage.getItem(KEY) || '0', 10) || 0; } catch (e) { return 0; } }
                    function setUnread(n) {
                        try { localStorage.setItem(KEY, String(n)); } catch (e) { }
                        badge.textContent = n > 9 ? '9+' : String(n);
                        badge.classList.toggle('hidden', n <= 0);
                    }
                    var LABEL = { Placed: 'was placed', Preparing: 'is being prepared', Ready: 'is ready for pickup', Claimed: 'was picked up', Cancelled: 'was cancelled' };

                    function row(orderId, status, when) {
                        var li = document.createElement('li');
                        var a = document.createElement('a');
                        a.href = '/Order/Details/' + encodeURIComponent(orderId);
                        a.className = 'flex items-start gap-3 px-4 py-3 text-sm transition hover:bg-caramel/10';
                        var i = document.createElement('i');
                        i.className = 'fa-solid mt-0.5 ' + (status === 'Ready' ? 'fa-circle-check text-emerald-400' : 'fa-mug-hot text-caramel');
                        var box = document.createElement('div');
                        var t = document.createElement('p'); t.className = 'text-cream';
                        t.textContent = 'Order #' + orderId + ' ' + (LABEL[status] || status.toLowerCase());
                        var s = document.createElement('p'); s.className = 'text-xs text-neutral-400';
                        s.textContent = when ? new Date(when).toLocaleString([], { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit' }) : 'Just now';
                        box.append(t, s); a.append(i, box); li.append(a);
                        return li;
                    }
                    function clearEmpty() { var e = list.querySelector('li:not(:has(a))'); if (e) e.remove(); }

                    function load() {
                        if (loaded) return; loaded = true;
                        fetch('/Order/RecentUpdates', { credentials: 'same-origin', headers: { Accept: 'application/json' } })
                            .then(function(r) { return r.ok ? r.json() : []; })
                            .then(function(rows) {
                                if (!rows.length) return;
                                list.replaceChildren.apply(list, rows.map(function(o) { return row(o.id, o.status, o.createdAtUtc); }));
                            }).catch(function() { loaded = false; });
                    }

                    setUnread(unread());
                    dd.addEventListener('dd:toggle', function(e) { if (e.detail) { load(); setUnread(0); } });

                    if (!window.signalR) return;
                    var conn = new signalR.HubConnectionBuilder().withUrl('/hubs/orders').withAutomaticReconnect().build();
                    conn.on('OrderStatusChanged', function(u) {
                        if (!u || !u.orderId || !u.status) return;
                        clearEmpty();
                        list.prepend(row(u.orderId, u.status, null));
                        while (list.children.length > 8) list.lastElementChild.remove();
                        var open = dd.querySelector('[data-dd-btn]').getAttribute('aria-expanded') === 'true';
                        if (!open) setUnread(unread() + 1);
                    });
                    conn.start().catch(function(e) { console.warn('Order hub unavailable', e); });
                })();
        </script>
    }
    @await RenderSectionAsync("Scripts", required: false)
</body>
</html>

@{
    var path = Context.Request.Path.Value ?? string.Empty;

    var main = new (string Href, string Label, string Icon, bool Exact, string? Badge)[]
    {
        ("/Barista", "Dashboard", "fa-gauge", true, null),
        ("/Barista/Queue", "Order Queue", "fa-list-ol", false, "placed"),
        ("/Barista/Preparation", "Order Preparation", "fa-mug-hot", false, "preparing"),
        ("/Barista/Status", "Order Status", "fa-table-columns", false, null),
        ("/Barista/Recipes", "Coffee Recipe Guide", "fa-book-open", false, null),
        ("/Barista/Notifications", "Notifications", "fa-bell", false, "alerts"),
    };
    var account = new (string Href, string Label, string Icon, bool Exact, string? Badge)[]
    {
        ("/Account/Manage", "My Profile", "fa-user", false, null),
        ("/Account/ChangePassword", "Change Password", "fa-key", false, null),
        ("/Barista/Shifts", "Shift Management", "fa-clock", false, null),
    };

    bool IsActive(string href, bool exact) =>
        href == "/Barista" && exact
            ? path.TrimEnd('/').Equals("/Barista", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) || path.Equals("/Barista/Dashboard", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
            : path.StartsWith(href, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

    const string linkBase = "flex items-center gap-3 rounded-lg border-l-2 border-solid px-3 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium no-underline transition";

<script>
    (function() {
        var aside = document.getElementById('staff-aside');
        var overlay = document.getElementById('staff-overlay');
        var openBtn = document.getElementById('staff-open');
        if (!aside || !overlay || !openBtn) return;

        function setOpen(on) {
            aside.classList.toggle('is-open', on);
            overlay.hidden = !on;
            openBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', on ? 'true' : 'false');
            document.body.style.overflow = on ? 'hidden' : '';
        }
        openBtn.addEventListener('click', function() { setOpen(true); });
        overlay.addEventListener('click', function() { setOpen(false); });
        aside.addEventListener('click', function(e) { if (e.target.closest('a')) setOpen(false); });
        document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) { if (e.key === 'Escape') setOpen(false); });
        window.addEventListener('resize', function() { if (window.innerWidth >= 768) setOpen(false); });

        // Live badge counts (queue / preparation / notifications)
        function loadCounts() {
            if (!window.fetch || document.hidden) return;
            fetch('/Barista/Counts', { credentials: 'same-origin' })
                .then(function(r) { return r.ok ? r.json() : null; })
                .then(function(d) {
                    if (!d) return;
                    [].slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('[data-badge]')).forEach(function(el) {
                        var n = d[el.getAttribute('data-badge')] || 0;
                        el.textContent = n > 99 ? '99+' : n;
                        el.hidden = n === 0;
                    });
                })
                .catch(function() { });
        }
        loadCounts();
        setInterval(loadCounts, 30000);
        window.addEventListener('barista:changed', loadCounts);
    })();
</script>

// Please see documentation at https://learn.microsoft.com/aspnet/core/client-side/bundling-and-minification
// for details on configuring this project to bundle and minify static web assets.

document.addEventListener('submit', function (e) {
    var f = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('form[data-fav-form]') : null;
    if (!f || !window.fetch) return;

    e.preventDefault();
    var btn = f.querySelector('button');
    if (btn) btn.disabled = true;

    fetch(f.action, {
        method: 'POST',
        body: new FormData(f),   // includes the antiforgery token field
        headers: { 'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest', 'Accept': 'application/json' },
        credentials: 'same-origin'
    })
        .then(function (r) {
            if (!r.ok || r.redirected) throw new Error('toggle failed');
            return r.json();
        })
        .then(function (d) {
            var icon = btn.querySelector('i');
            icon.className = (d.saved ? 'fa-solid' : 'fa-regular') + ' fa-heart';
            btn.classList.toggle('text-rose-400', d.saved);
            btn.classList.toggle('text-white', !d.saved);
            btn.setAttribute('aria-pressed', d.saved ? 'true' : 'false');
            btn.disabled = false;
        })
        .catch(function () { f.submit(); });
});
